==============================================================================
FILE: api/deps.py
==============================================================================
"""FastAPI dependencies — Bearer session token → current user."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, status
from fastapi.security import HTTPAuthorizationCredentials, HTTPBearer

from modules.authentication.auth_service import AuthenticationError, resolve_session

bearer_scheme = HTTPBearer(auto_error=False)


def get_session_token(
    creds: HTTPAuthorizationCredentials | None = Depends(bearer_scheme),
) -> str:
    if creds is None or not creds.credentials:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Login first, then Authorize with your session_token.",
            headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
        )
    return creds.credentials.strip()


def get_current_user(token: str = Depends(get_session_token)) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        return resolve_session(token)
    except AuthenticationError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail=str(exc),
            headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
        ) from exc


==============================================================================
FILE: api/http_errors.py
==============================================================================
"""Map app errors to HTTP responses (shared by all routers)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import HTTPException, status

from utils.errors import AuthError, NotFoundError, PermissionDeniedError, ValidationError


def raise_http_error(exc: Exception) -> None:
    """Convert known app errors to HTTPException. Always raises."""
    if isinstance(exc, AuthError):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail=exc.message,
        ) from exc
    if isinstance(exc, PermissionDeniedError):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail=exc.message,
        ) from exc
    if isinstance(exc, NotFoundError):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail=exc.message,
        ) from exc
    if isinstance(exc, ValidationError):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            detail=exc.message,
        ) from exc
    raise exc


==============================================================================
FILE: api/main.py
==============================================================================
"""FastAPI app — Swagger UI at /docs."""

from __future__ import annotations

from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

from fastapi import FastAPI, Request, status
from fastapi.exceptions import RequestValidationError
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse

from api.routers import (
    aia_router,
    auth_router,
    cae_router,
    editor_router,
    journalist_router,
    manager_router,
    publisher_router,
)
from config.env_loader import load_dotenv
from utils.logger import get_logger

log = get_logger("api")

OPENAPI_TAGS = [
    {
        "name": "1. Login",
        "description": "Login, logout, change password, admin reset. Authorize with Bearer token.",
    },
    {
        "name": "2. Journalist tools",
        "description": "AI tools + desk content (news/articles/evidence/raw ingest).",
    },
    {
        "name": "3. Audience Insight Analyst",
        "description": "Heuristic engagement/interest, category/trends, performance loop, story pitches.",
    },
    {
        "name": "4. Content Adaptation (CAE)",
        "description": "Adapt articles for social, print, web, and newsletter; review drafts.",
    },
    {
        "name": "5. Publisher",
        "description": "Publication queue, templates, preview, publish, and schedule.",
    },
    {
        "name": "6. Editor",
        "description": "Pitch review, content review, and assignment.",
    },
    {
        "name": "7. Newsroom Manager",
        "description": "Desk overview plus pitch/content review and assignment.",
    },
]


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(_app: FastAPI):
    load_dotenv()
    log.info("NewsMind API started. Swagger: /docs")
    yield


def create_app() -> FastAPI:
    application = FastAPI(
        title="NewsMind API",
        summary="Login once, Authorize, then use role tools in Swagger.",
        description=(
            "### Quick start\n"
            "1. Open **1. Login** → `POST /auth/login` → Try it out\n"
            "2. Copy `session_token` from the response\n"
            "3. Click **Authorize** → paste token → Authorize\n"
            "4. Use role endpoints (Journalist, AIA, CAE, Publisher, Editor, Manager)\n\n"
            "### Password rules (change/reset)\n"
            "Min 10 characters, with uppercase, lowercase, digit, and special character.\n\n"
            "Models: `editor_queue_urgency.joblib` (editor review urgency) and "
            "`news_category.joblib` (topic). Engagement/interest are simple formulas.\n"
            "Naming notes: see `docs/NAMING.md`."
        ),
        version="0.3.0",
        lifespan=lifespan,
        docs_url="/docs",
        redoc_url=None,
        openapi_tags=OPENAPI_TAGS,
        swagger_ui_parameters={
            "docExpansion": "list",
            "defaultModelsExpandDepth": -1,
            "defaultModelExpandDepth": 1,
            "filter": True,
            "tryItOutEnabled": True,
            "persistAuthorization": True,
        },
    )

    @application.exception_handler(RequestValidationError)
    async def validation_exception_handler(
        _request: Request,
        exc: RequestValidationError,
    ) -> JSONResponse:
        errors = []
        for err in exc.errors():
            loc = ".".join(str(part) for part in err.get("loc", ()) if part != "body")
            errors.append({"field": loc or "body", "message": err.get("msg", "Invalid value")})
        return JSONResponse(
            status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
            content={"detail": "Validation failed", "errors": errors},
        )

    application.include_router(auth_router)
    application.include_router(journalist_router)
    application.include_router(aia_router)
    application.include_router(cae_router)
    application.include_router(publisher_router)
    application.include_router(editor_router)
    application.include_router(manager_router)
    return application


app = create_app()


==============================================================================
FILE: api/README.md
==============================================================================
# `api/`

FastAPI + Swagger (`/docs`).

## Layout

| Path | Role |
|------|------|
| `main.py` | App + OpenAPI tags |
| `deps.py` | Bearer session token dependency |
| `http_errors.py` | Shared error → HTTP mapping |
| `routers/` | One router per desk (HTTP paths only) |
| `schemas/` | Pydantic request/response models |

## What are schemas?

`api/schemas/` defines the **HTTP contract**: allowed request fields, response shapes, and validation (lengths, enums via `rules.py`).  
They do **not** talk to the database or run ML. Routers parse/validate with schemas, then call `modules/` or `services/`.

## Run

```powershell
$env:PYTHONPATH = "G:\NewsMind AI\NewsMindAI V2"
uvicorn api.main:app --reload
```

Open http://127.0.0.1:8000/docs

## ML policy

- Similarity / ranking: local numpy (`ml.common`).
- Metadata NER: spaCy blank + EntityRuler.
- Pretrained joblibs: Editor priority + AIA category only (`artifacts/models/`).

"""Audience Insight Analyst endpoints."""

from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends

from api.deps import get_session_token
from api.http_errors import raise_http_error
from api.schemas.aia import (
    CategoryRequest,
    CategoryResponse,
    EngagementResponse,
    InterestResponse,
    MetricsRequest,
    PerformanceAnalyticsResponse,
    StoryPitchListResponse,
    StoryPitchRequest,
    StoryPitchResponse,
    TrendRequest,
    TrendResponse,
)
from config.constants import CAP_ANALYTICS
from services.access import require_capability_session
from services.aia import AIAService
from utils.errors import AuthError, NotFoundError, PermissionDeniedError, ValidationError

router = APIRouter(prefix="/aia", tags=["3. Audience Insight Analyst"])
_service = AIAService()
_APP_ERRORS = (AuthError, PermissionDeniedError, ValidationError, NotFoundError)


def _require_aia(token: str) -> None:
    require_capability_session(CAP_ANALYTICS, token)


@router.post("/analyze-engagement", response_model=EngagementResponse, summary="Analyze engagement")
def analyze_engagement(
    body: MetricsRequest | None = None,
    token: str = Depends(get_session_token),
):
    try:
        _require_aia(token)
        metrics = body or MetricsRequest()
        return _service.analyze_engagement(
            views=metrics.views,
            likes=metrics.likes,
            shares=metrics.shares,
            comments=metrics.comments,
        )
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post("/analyze-interest", response_model=InterestResponse, summary="Analyze interest")
def analyze_interest(
    body: MetricsRequest | None = None,
    token: str = Depends(get_session_token),
):
    try:
        _require_aia(token)
        metrics = body or MetricsRequest()
        return _service.analyze_interest(
            views=metrics.views,
            likes=metrics.likes,
            shares=metrics.shares,
            comments=metrics.comments,
        )
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post("/analyze-trends", response_model=TrendResponse, summary="Analyze trends")
def analyze_trends(
    body: TrendRequest | None = None,
    token: str = Depends(get_session_token),
):
    try:
        _require_aia(token)
        payload = body or TrendRequest()
        return _service.analyze_trends(payload.title or "", payload.summary or "")
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post(
    "/predict-category",
    response_model=CategoryResponse,
    summary="Predict news category (pretrained RandomForest)",
)
def predict_category(body: CategoryRequest, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        _require_aia(token)
        return _service.predict_news_category(body.title, body.summary)
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.get(
    "/performance",
    response_model=PerformanceAnalyticsResponse,
    summary="Performance analytics loop (publication → AIA)",
)
def performance_analytics(token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):

    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post(

==============================================================================
FILE: api/routers/auth.py
==============================================================================
"""Login, logout, change password, admin reset."""

from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status

from api.deps import get_current_user, get_session_token
from api.http_errors import raise_http_error
from api.schemas.auth import (
    AdminResetPasswordRequest,
    ChangePasswordRequest,
    LoginRequest,
    LoginResponse,
    MessageResponse,
)
from config.constants import CAP_ADMIN
from modules.authentication.auth_service import (
    AuthenticationError,
    authenticate,
    change_password,
    logout,
)
from services.access import require_capability_session
from services.user import UserService
from utils.errors import AuthError, PermissionDeniedError, ValidationError

router = APIRouter(prefix="/auth", tags=["1. Login"])
_APP_ERRORS = (AuthError, PermissionDeniedError, ValidationError)


@router.post(
    "/login",
    response_model=LoginResponse,
    summary="Login",
    description="Returns a session_token. Click **Authorize**, choose Bearer, paste the token.",
)
def login(body: LoginRequest) -> LoginResponse:
    try:
        payload = authenticate(body.username, body.password)
    except AuthenticationError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail=str(exc)) from exc
    return LoginResponse(
        session_token=str(payload["session_token"]),
        user_id=int(payload["user_id"]),
        username=str(payload["username"]),
        role_code=str(payload["role_code"]),
        capabilities=list(payload.get("capabilities") or []),
    )


@router.get("/me", summary="Current session user")
def me(user: dict = Depends(get_current_user)) -> dict:
    return {
        "user_id": int(user["user_id"]),
        "username": str(user["username"]),
        "role_code": str(user["role_code"]),
        "capabilities": list(user.get("capabilities") or []),
    }


@router.post("/logout", response_model=MessageResponse, summary="Logout")
def do_logout(token: str = Depends(get_session_token)) -> MessageResponse:
    logout(token)
    return MessageResponse(message="Logged out.")


@router.post("/change-password", response_model=MessageResponse, summary="Change password")
def do_change_password(
    body: ChangePasswordRequest,
    user: dict = Depends(get_current_user),
):
    try:
        change_password(int(user["user_id"]), body.current_password, body.new_password)
        return MessageResponse(message="Password changed.")
    except ValidationError as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)
    except AuthenticationError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from exc


@router.post(
    "/admin/reset-password",
    response_model=MessageResponse,
    summary="Admin reset password",
)
def do_admin_reset_password(
    body: AdminResetPasswordRequest,
    token: str = Depends(get_session_token),
):
    try:
        require_capability_session(CAP_ADMIN, token)
        UserService().reset_password(body.username, body.new_password)
        return MessageResponse(message=f"Password reset for {body.username}.")
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)

FILE: api/routers/cae.py

"""Content Adaptation Editor endpoints."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, status

from api.deps import get_session_token
from api.http_errors import raise_http_error
from api.schemas.cae import (
    AdaptationCreate,
    AdaptationResponse,
    DraftResponse,
    FinalVersionRequest,
    RemarksRequest,
    RequiredRemarksRequest,
)
from config.constants import CAP_ADAPT
from services.access import require_capability_session
from services.adaptation import AdaptationService, ReviewService
from utils.errors import AuthError, NotFoundError, PermissionDeniedError, ValidationError

router = APIRouter(prefix="/cae", tags=["4. Content Adaptation (CAE)"])
_adapt = AdaptationService()
_review = ReviewService()
_APP_ERRORS = (AuthError, PermissionDeniedError, ValidationError, NotFoundError)


def _user(token: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return require_capability_session(CAP_ADAPT, token)


def _draft_response(row: dict[str, Any]) -> DraftResponse:
    params = row.get("generation_parameters") or {}
    flags = row.get("validation_flags") or {}
    if isinstance(params, str):
        import json

        params = json.loads(params)
    if isinstance(flags, str):
        import json

        flags = json.loads(flags)
    return DraftResponse(
        id=int(row["id"]),
        platform=str(row["platform"]),
        version_number=int(row["version_number"]),
        adapted_title=str(row["adapted_title"]),
        adapted_content=str(row["adapted_content"]),
        model_version=str(row["model_version"]),
        status=str(row["status"]),
        generation_parameters=dict(params),
        validation_flags=dict(flags),
        created_at=row.get("created_at"),
    )


def _adaptation_response(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> AdaptationResponse:
    return AdaptationResponse(
        id=int(payload["id"]),
        original_title=str(payload["original_title"]),
        original_content=str(payload["original_content"]),
        created_by=payload.get("created_by"),
        status=str(payload["status"]),
        created_at=payload.get("created_at"),
        drafts=[_draft_response(d) for d in payload.get("drafts") or []],
    )


@router.post(
    "/adaptations",
    response_model=AdaptationResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
    summary="Create adaptations",
)
def create_adaptation(body: AdaptationCreate, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        user = _user(token)
        result = _adapt.create(
            original_title=body.original_title,
            original_content=body.original_content,
            target_platforms=body.target_platforms,
            created_by=int(user["user_id"]),
        )
        return _adaptation_response(result)
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.get(
    "/adaptations/{request_id}",
    response_model=AdaptationResponse,
    summary="Get adaptation request",
)
def read_adaptation(request_id: int, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        _user(token)
        return _adaptation_response(_adapt.get_request(request_id))
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post(
    "/adaptation-drafts/{draft_id}/approve",
    response_model=DraftResponse,
    summary="Approve draft",
)
def approve_draft(
    draft_id: int,
    body: RemarksRequest | None = None,
    token: str = Depends(get_session_token),
):
    try:
        user = _user(token)
        remarks = body.remarks if body else None
        return _draft_response(_review.approve(draft_id, int(user["user_id"]), remarks))
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post(
    "/adaptation-drafts/{draft_id}/reject",
    response_model=DraftResponse,
    summary="Reject draft",
)
def reject_draft(
    draft_id: int,
    body: RequiredRemarksRequest,
    token: str = Depends(get_session_token),
):
    try:
        user = _user(token)
        return _draft_response(_review.reject(draft_id, int(user["user_id"]), body.remarks))
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post(
    "/adaptation-drafts/{draft_id}/regenerate",
    response_model=DraftResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
    summary="Regenerate draft",
)
def regenerate_draft(
    draft_id: int,
    body: RequiredRemarksRequest,
    token: str = Depends(get_session_token),
):
    try:
        user = _user(token)
        return _draft_response(
            _review.regenerate(draft_id, int(user["user_id"]), body.remarks)
        )
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.put(
    "/adaptation-drafts/{draft_id}/final",
    response_model=DraftResponse,
    summary="Finalize draft",
)
def finalize_draft(
    draft_id: int,
    body: FinalVersionRequest,
    token: str = Depends(get_session_token),
):
    try:
        user = _user(token)
        return _draft_response(
            _review.finalize(
                draft_id,
                int(user["user_id"]),
                body.final_title,
                body.final_content,
                body.remarks,
            )
        )
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.get(
    "/adaptation-drafts/{draft_id}/versions",
    response_model=list[DraftResponse],
    summary="List draft versions",
)
def draft_versions(draft_id: int, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        _user(token)
        return [_draft_response(row) for row in _review.versions(draft_id)]
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)

"""Editor desk endpoints — queue ordered by editor_queue_urgency model."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query

from api.deps import get_session_token
from api.http_errors import raise_http_error
from api.schemas.editor import (
    AssignRequest,
    ContentListResponse,
    EditorQueueResponse,
    EventGroupsResponse,
    PitchDuplicateRequest,
    PitchDuplicateResponse,
    PitchListResponse,

    SuggestJournalistRequest,
    SuggestJournalistResponse,
    UrgencyScoreRequest,
    UrgencyScoreResponse,
)

_service = EditorService()

def _user(token: str, capability: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return require_capability_session(capability, token)


@router.get(
    "/event-groups",
    response_model=EventGroupsResponse,
    summary="Group related stories into events (Editor)",
)
def event_groups(
    min_similarity: float = Query(default=0.45, ge=0.1, le=0.95),
    limit: int = Query(default=200, ge=10, le=500),
    token: str = Depends(get_session_token),
):
    try:
        _user(token, CAP_CONTENT_REVIEW)
        return _manager.discover_event_groups(
            min_similarity=min_similarity,
            limit=limit,
        )
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)

    "/queue",
    response_model=EditorQueueResponse,

)

    pitch_status: str = Query(default="submitted"),
    content_status: str = Query(default="submitted"),
    token: str = Depends(get_session_token),

            pitch_status=pitch_status,

    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:

"""Journalist tools + desk content endpoints."""

from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query

from api.deps import get_session_token
from api.http_errors import raise_http_error
from api.schemas.journalist import (
    ArticleCreateRequest,
    ContentCreateResponse,
    ContentItemResponse,
    ContentListResponse,
    ContentStatusRequest,
    ContentStatusResponse,
    CredibilityRequest,
    CredibilityResponse,
    DuplicatesRequest,
    EntitiesResponse,
    EvidenceCreateRequest,
    EvidenceCreateResponse,
    NewsCreateRequest,
    PairsResponse,
    PatentSearchRequest,
    RankedListResponse,
    RawIngestListResponse,
    SearchRequest,
    SuggestCategoryRequest,
    SuggestCategoryResponse,
    TextBody,
    ValidateRequest,
    ValidateResponse,
)
from modules.journalist import JournalistModule
from utils.errors import AuthError, NotFoundError, PermissionDeniedError, ValidationError

router = APIRouter(prefix="/journalist", tags=["2. Journalist tools"])

_APP_ERRORS = (AuthError, PermissionDeniedError, ValidationError, NotFoundError)


def _tools(token: str) -> JournalistModule:
    return JournalistModule(session_token=token)


def _as_dicts(items) -> list[dict]:
    return [item.model_dump() for item in items]


# ── AI tools ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────


@router.post("/research", response_model=RankedListResponse, summary="Research related news")
def research(body: SearchRequest, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        return _tools(token).research(body.query, _as_dicts(body.documents), limit=body.limit)
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post("/evidence-search", response_model=RankedListResponse, summary="Search evidence")
def evidence_search(body: SearchRequest, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        return _tools(token).evidence_search(
            body.query, _as_dicts(body.documents), limit=body.limit
        )
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post("/metadata", response_model=EntitiesResponse, summary="Extract metadata")
def metadata(body: TextBody, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        return _tools(token).metadata(body.text)
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post("/duplicates", response_model=PairsResponse, summary="Find duplicates")
def duplicates(body: DuplicatesRequest, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        return _tools(token).duplicates(
            _as_dicts(body.documents),
            threshold=body.threshold,
            limit=body.limit,
        )
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)

def validate(body: ValidateRequest, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        return _tools(token).validate(body.model_dump())
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post(
    "/suggest-category",
    response_model=SuggestCategoryResponse,
    summary="Suggest category (reuses AIA news_category_rf)",
)
def suggest_category(body: SuggestCategoryRequest, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        return _tools(token).suggest_category(
            body.title,
            summary=body.summary or "",
            body=body.body or "",
        )
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)

        return _tools(token).credibility(
            body.text,
            _as_dicts(body.evidence_docs),
            limit=body.limit,
        )
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post("/patents", response_model=RankedListResponse, summary="Search patents")
def patents(body: PatentSearchRequest, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        docs = None if body.documents is None else _as_dicts(body.documents)
        return _tools(token).patents(body.query, docs, limit=body.limit)
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)

    try:
        _user(token, CAP_PITCH_REVIEW)
        return _service.reject_pitch(story_pitch_id, remarks=(body.remarks if body else ""))
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.get("/content", response_model=ContentListResponse, summary="List content")
def list_content(
    status: str = Query(default="submitted"),
    token: str = Depends(get_session_token),
):
    try:
        _user(token, CAP_CONTENT_REVIEW)
        return _service.list_content(status=status)
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.put("/content/{content_id}/approve", summary="Approve content")
def approve_content(content_id: int, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        _user(token, CAP_CONTENT_REVIEW)
        return _service.approve_content(content_id)
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.put("/content/{content_id}/reject", summary="Reject content")
def reject_content(
    content_id: int,
    body: RemarksBody | None = None,
    token: str = Depends(get_session_token),
):
    try:
        _user(token, CAP_CONTENT_REVIEW)
        return _service.reject_content(content_id, remarks=(body.remarks if body else ""))
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.put("/content/{content_id}/assign", summary="Assign content")
def assign_content(
    content_id: int,
    body: AssignRequest,
    token: str = Depends(get_session_token),
):
    try:
        _user(token, CAP_ASSIGN)
        return _service.assign_content(content_id, body.assignee_user_id, note=body.note)
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)

==============================================================================
FILE: api/schemas/__init__.py
==============================================================================
"""Pydantic request/response models for FastAPI routers.

Schemas are **not** business logic. They define:
  - what JSON a client may send (request models)
  - what JSON the API returns (response models)
  - field validation (types, min length, allowed status values via `rules.py`)

Routers import schemas for `response_model=` and body parameters.
Domain work stays in `services/` / `modules/` / `ml/`.
"""

from typing import Any

"""Auth request/response bodies."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field

from api.schemas.common import MessageResponse
from api.schemas.rules import LoginPasswordStr, StrongPasswordStr, UsernameStr

__all__ = [
    "LoginRequest",
    "LoginResponse",
    "ChangePasswordRequest",
    "AdminResetPasswordRequest",
    "MessageResponse",
]


class LoginRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(
        json_schema_extra={
            "examples": [
                {"username": "journalist", "password": "Journalist@NewsMind2026!"}
            ]
        }
    )

    username: UsernameStr
    password: LoginPasswordStr


class LoginResponse(BaseModel):
    session_token: str = Field(description="Paste into Swagger Authorize as Bearer token")
    user_id: int
    username: str
    role_code: str
    capabilities: list[str] = []


class ChangePasswordRequest(BaseModel):
    current_password: LoginPasswordStr
    new_password: StrongPasswordStr = Field(
        ...,
        description="Min 10 chars with upper, lower, digit, and special character.",
    )


class AdminResetPasswordRequest(BaseModel):
    username: UsernameStr
    new_password: StrongPasswordStr = Field(
        ...,
        description="Min 10 chars with upper, lower, digit, and special character.",
    )


==============================================================================
FILE: api/schemas/cae.py
==============================================================================
"""CAE request/response models."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, field_validator

from config.constants import ADAPTATION_PLATFORMS


class AdaptationCreate(BaseModel):
    original_title: str = Field(..., min_length=5, max_length=500)
    original_content: str = Field(..., min_length=20)
    target_platforms: list[str] = Field(..., min_length=1)

    @field_validator("target_platforms")
    @classmethod
    def validate_platforms(cls, value: list[str]) -> list[str]:
        normalized = [str(v).strip().upper() for v in value]
        if len(set(normalized)) != len(normalized):
            raise ValueError("target_platforms must not contain duplicates")
        bad = [p for p in normalized if p not in ADAPTATION_PLATFORMS]
        if bad:
            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported platforms: {bad}")
        return normalized


class DraftResponse(BaseModel):
    id: int
    platform: str
    version_number: int
    adapted_title: str
    adapted_content: str
    model_version: str
    status: str
    generation_parameters: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    validation_flags: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    created_at: datetime | None = None


class AdaptationResponse(BaseModel):
    id: int
    original_title: str
    original_content: str
    created_by: int | None = None
    status: str
    created_at: datetime | None = None
    drafts: list[DraftResponse] = Field(default_factory=list)


class RemarksRequest(BaseModel):
    remarks: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=2000)


class RequiredRemarksRequest(BaseModel):
    remarks: str = Field(..., min_length=3, max_length=2000)


class FinalVersionRequest(BaseModel):
    final_title: str = Field(..., min_length=5, max_length=500)
    final_content: str = Field(..., min_length=20)
    remarks: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=2000)


==============================================================================
FILE: api/schemas/common.py
==============================================================================
"""Shared request/response pieces used by multiple endpoints."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field


class DocumentItem(BaseModel):
    """One article or evidence snippet for search / compare."""

    model_config = ConfigDict(
        json_schema_extra={
            "examples": [
                {
                    "id": 1,
                    "title": "Budget vote in parliament",
                    "text": "Parliament held a budget vote today.",
                }
            ]
        }
    )

    id: int | str | None = None
    title: str = ""
    text: str = ""


class SearchRequest(BaseModel):
    """Query plus optional documents to rank against."""

    model_config = ConfigDict(
        json_schema_extra={
            "examples": [
                {
                    "query": "budget vote",
                    "documents": [
                        {
                            "id": 1,
                            "title": "Budget vote in parliament",
                            "text": "Parliament held a budget vote today.",
                        }
                    ],
                    "limit": 5,
                }
            ]
        }
    )

    query: str = Field(..., min_length=1, description="Search text")
    documents: list[DocumentItem] = Field(
        default_factory=list,
        description="Docs to search (empty list → empty results)",
    )
    limit: int = Field(default=10, ge=1, le=100)


class TextBody(BaseModel):
    text: str = Field(..., min_length=1, examples=["Alice Smith visited Paris on 2024-01-15."])


class MessageResponse(BaseModel):
    message: str


class RankedListResponse(BaseModel):
    actor: str
    results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

==============================================================================
FILE: api/schemas/rules.py
==============================================================================
"""Shared Pydantic field rules used across API schemas."""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from typing import Annotated

from pydantic import AfterValidator, Field, StringConstraints

from config.constants import ALLOWED_CONTENT_STATUSES, MIN_BODY_LENGTH, MIN_TITLE_LENGTH
from utils.errors import ValidationError as AppValidationError
from utils.validators import (
    ALLOWED_ARTICLE_TYPES,
    ALLOWED_PRIORITIES,
    ALLOWED_SEVERITIES,
    require_password,
)

UsernameStr = Annotated[
    str,
    StringConstraints(strip_whitespace=True, min_length=3, max_length=64),
]

# Login accepts any non-empty password (existing accounts may predate stricter rules).
LoginPasswordStr = Annotated[str, StringConstraints(min_length=1, max_length=128)]

TitleStr = Annotated[
    str,
    StringConstraints(strip_whitespace=True, min_length=MIN_TITLE_LENGTH, max_length=512),
]

BodyStr = Annotated[
    str,
    StringConstraints(strip_whitespace=True, min_length=MIN_BODY_LENGTH, max_length=50_000),
]

SummaryStr = Annotated[str, StringConstraints(strip_whitespace=True, max_length=4000)]


def _strong_password(value: str) -> str:
    try:
        return require_password(value)
    except AppValidationError as exc:
        raise ValueError(exc.message) from exc


StrongPasswordStr = Annotated[str, AfterValidator(_strong_password)]


def _content_status(value: str) -> str:
    text = (value or "").strip().lower()
    if text not in ALLOWED_CONTENT_STATUSES:
        raise ValueError(f"Invalid status. Allowed: {', '.join(ALLOWED_CONTENT_STATUSES)}")
    return text


def _severity(value: str) -> str:
    text = (value or "").strip().lower()
    if text not in ALLOWED_SEVERITIES:
        raise ValueError(f"Invalid severity. Allowed: {', '.join(ALLOWED_SEVERITIES)}")
    return text


def _article_type(value: str) -> str:
    text = (value or "").strip().lower()
    if text not in ALLOWED_ARTICLE_TYPES:
        raise ValueError(f"Invalid article_type. Allowed: {', '.join(ALLOWED_ARTICLE_TYPES)}")
    return text


def _priority(value: str) -> str:
    text = (value or "").strip().lower()
    if text not in ALLOWED_PRIORITIES:
        raise ValueError(f"Invalid priority. Allowed: {', '.join(ALLOWED_PRIORITIES)}")
    return text


ContentStatusStr = Annotated[str, AfterValidator(_content_status)]
SeverityStr = Annotated[str, AfterValidator(_severity)]
ArticleTypeStr = Annotated[str, AfterValidator(_article_type)]
PriorityStr = Annotated[str, AfterValidator(_priority)]

ScoreFloat = Annotated[float, Field(ge=0, le=1000)]
NonNegFloat = Annotated[float, Field(ge=0)]


==============================================================================
FILE: app.py
==============================================================================
"""
NewsMind console application.

Feature modules: authentication, admin, content
Services: pipeline at services/ root; content/ + user/ grouped
Cleaning: cleaners/ (+ stages/) | Repositories: content + user
Menus are role-based after login.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
from getpass import getpass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Callable, Optional

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from config.constants import (
    ALLOWED_CONTENT_STATUSES,
    ALLOWED_PITCH_STATUSES,
    CAP_ADMIN,
    CAP_CLEAN,
    CAP_INGEST,
    CAP_PERSIST,
    CAP_PIPELINE,
    CAP_PITCH_REVIEW,
    ROLE_ADMIN,
    ROLE_ADAPTATION_EDITOR,
    ROLE_AUDIENCE_ANALYST,
    ROLE_EDITOR,
    ROLE_JOURNALIST,
    ROLE_NEWSROOM_MANAGER,
    ROLE_PUBLISHER,
)
from config.env_loader import load_dotenv
from database.connection import check_connection, init_schema
from modules.admin import AdminModule
from modules.authentication import (
    AuthenticationError,
    authenticate,
    change_password,
    logout,
    resolve_session,
)
from modules.content import ContentModule
from services.permission_service import has_capability
from services.user import UserService
from utils.console_ui import (
    UserCancelled,
    blank,
    error,
    header,
    info,
    is_cancel,
    kv,
    prompt_choice,
    prompt_nonempty,
    prompt_optional,
    show_dict_rows,
    show_stats,
    show_user,
    success,
)
from utils.errors import AppError, AuthError, PermissionDeniedError, ValidationError
from utils.logger import get_logger
from utils.validators import require_pitch_status

load_dotenv()

log = get_logger("app")
admin = AdminModule()
content = ContentModule()
users = UserService()

Action = Callable[[], int]


def _current_user() -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
    try:
        return resolve_session()
    except AuthenticationError:
        return None


def _handle(exc: Exception) -> int:
    if isinstance(exc, UserCancelled):
        raise exc
    if isinstance(exc, AppError):
        error(exc.message)
        return 1
    if isinstance(exc, AuthenticationError):
        error(str(exc))
        return 1
    log.exception("Unhandled error")
    error(f"Unexpected failure: {exc}")
    return 1


def _run_admin(fn, *, title: str) -> int:
    try:
        result = fn()
    except UserCancelled:
        raise
    except (AuthError, PermissionDeniedError, AppError) as exc:
        return _handle(exc)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)

    header(title)
    kv("Actor", result.get("actor"))
    kv("Role", result.get("role"))
    if result.get("message"):
        info(str(result["message"]))
    if "all_files" in result:
        kv("CSV Files", len(result.get("all_files") or []))
        for name in result.get("all_files") or []:
            mark = " [new/changed]" if name in (result.get("new_or_changed") or []) else ""
            print(f"    - {name}{mark}")
        kv("New Or Changed", len(result.get("new_or_changed") or []))
    if "content_rows" in result:
        kv("Content Rows", result["content_rows"])
        kv("Pitch Rows", result.get("pitch_rows"))
        files = result.get("source_files") or []
        kv("Source Files", len(files))
        for name in files[:20]:
            print(f"    - {name}")
        if len(files) > 20:
            print(f"    ... and {len(files) - 20} more")
        collections = result.get("collections") or {}
        if collections:
            kv("Categories", ", ".join(collections.get("categories") or []) or "none")
            kv("Unique Titles", collections.get("unique_titles"))
        kv("Raw Rows Stored", len(result.get("raw_ingest_ids") or []))
    if "ingest" in result:
        ingest = result["ingest"]
        kv("Ingested Content", ingest.get("content_rows"))
        kv("Ingested Pitches", ingest.get("pitch_rows"))
        kv("Raw Stored", ingest.get("raw_stored"))
        files = ingest.get("source_files") or []
        kv("Source Files", len(files))
        for name in files[:20]:
            print(f"    - {name}")
    if "content_stats" in result:
        stats = result["content_stats"]
        kv("Clean Kept", stats.get("kept"))
        kv("Clean Rejected", stats.get("rejected"))
        kv("Quality Report File", result.get("content_report") or result.get("quality_report"))
        kv("Cleaning Report ID", result.get("cleaning_report_id"))
        kv("Cleaned CSV", result.get("cleaned_csv"))
    if "persisted_content" in result:
        kv("Persisted Content", result.get("persisted_content"))
        kv("Persisted Pitches", result.get("persisted_pitches"))
        kv("Rejected Raw Stored", result.get("rejected_raw_stored"))
    success(f"{title} completed.")
    blank()
    return 0


# ── Auth ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def do_login(username: str = "", password: str = "") -> int:
    try:
        username = username or prompt_nonempty("Username")
        password = password or getpass("Password: ")
        if not password:
            raise ValidationError("Password cannot be empty.")
        payload = authenticate(username, password)
    except (AuthenticationError, ValidationError, AppError) as exc:
        return _handle(exc)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    success("Login successful.")
    show_user(payload)
    return 0


def do_logout() -> int:
    logout()
    success("Logged out.")
    blank()
    return 0


def do_change_password() -> int:
    header("Change Password")
    try:
        user = resolve_session()
        current = getpass("Current password: ")
        new_password = getpass("New password: ")
        confirm = getpass("Confirm new password: ")
        if new_password != confirm:
            raise ValidationError("New password and confirmation do not match.")
        change_password(int(user["user_id"]), current, new_password)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    success("Password changed. Use the new password next login.")
    blank()
    return 0


def do_admin_reset_password() -> int:
    header("Reset User Password (Admin)")
    try:
        username = prompt_nonempty("Username to reset")
        new_password = getpass("New password: ")
        confirm = getpass("Confirm new password: ")
        if new_password != confirm:
            raise ValidationError("New password and confirmation do not match.")
        result = users.reset_password(username, new_password)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    success("Password reset for user.")
    kv("Username", result["username"])
    kv("User ID", result["user_id"])
    blank()
    return 0


# ── Admin / pipeline ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def do_list_csv_files() -> int:
    return _run_admin(admin.list_csv_files, title="CSV Files In data/raw")


def do_ingest() -> int:
    return _run_admin(lambda: admin.ingest(only_new=False), title="Ingest All CSV Files")


def do_ingest_new() -> int:
    return _run_admin(lambda: admin.ingest(only_new=True), title="Ingest New/Changed CSV Files")


def do_ingest_one() -> int:
    header("Ingest One CSV File")
    try:
        listing = admin.list_csv_files()
        files = listing.get("all_files") or []
        if not files:
            raise ValidationError("No CSV files found under data/raw.")
        info(f"{len(files)} CSV file(s) available.")
        for idx, name in enumerate(files, start=1):
            mark = " [new/changed]" if name in (listing.get("new_or_changed") or []) else ""
            print(f"  {idx}) {name}{mark}")
        choice = prompt_nonempty("File number or relative path")
        if choice.isdigit():
            n = int(choice)
            if n < 1 or n > len(files):
                raise ValidationError("File number out of range.")
            source = files[n - 1]
        else:
            source = choice
        return _run_admin(lambda: admin.ingest_file(source), title=f"Ingest File — {source}")
    except (AuthError, PermissionDeniedError, AppError, UserCancelled) as exc:
        if isinstance(exc, UserCancelled):
            raise
        return _handle(exc)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)


def do_clean() -> int:
    return _run_admin(admin.clean, title="Clean Data")


def do_ingest_and_clean() -> int:
    return _run_admin(lambda: admin.ingest_and_clean(only_new=False), title="Ingest + Clean (All)")


def do_ingest_and_clean_new() -> int:
    return _run_admin(lambda: admin.ingest_and_clean(only_new=True), title="Ingest + Clean (New Only)")


def do_persist() -> int:
    return _run_admin(admin.persist, title="Persist Cleaned Data")


def do_pipeline() -> int:
    return _run_admin(lambda: admin.run_pipeline(only_new=False), title="Full Pipeline (All Files)")


def do_pipeline_new() -> int:
    return _run_admin(lambda: admin.run_pipeline(only_new=True), title="Full Pipeline (New Files Only)")


def do_pipeline_one() -> int:
    header("Pipeline One CSV File")
    try:
        listing = admin.list_csv_files()
        files = listing.get("all_files") or []
        if not files:
            raise ValidationError("No CSV files found under data/raw.")
        for idx, name in enumerate(files, start=1):
            mark = " [new/changed]" if name in (listing.get("new_or_changed") or []) else ""
            print(f"  {idx}) {name}{mark}")
        choice = prompt_nonempty("File number or relative path")
        if choice.isdigit():
            n = int(choice)
            if n < 1 or n > len(files):
                raise ValidationError("File number out of range.")
            source = files[n - 1]
        else:
            source = choice
        return _run_admin(lambda: admin.pipeline_file(source), title=f"Pipeline File — {source}")
    except (AuthError, PermissionDeniedError, AppError, UserCancelled) as exc:
        if isinstance(exc, UserCancelled):
            raise
        return _handle(exc)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)


def do_list_reports() -> int:
    try:
        payload = admin.list_reports(limit=20)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    show_dict_rows(
        "Cleaning Reports (DB)",
        payload.get("reports") or [],
        [
            ("Report ID", "cleaning_report_id"),
            ("Type", "report_type"),
            ("Input", "input_count"),
            ("Kept", "kept_count"),
            ("Rejected", "rejected_count"),
            ("Sources", "source_files"),
            ("Created", "created_at"),
        ],
    )
    return 0


def do_view_report() -> int:
    header("View Cleaning Report")
    try:
        report_id = int(prompt_nonempty("Cleaning report ID"))
        payload = admin.view_report(report_id)
        row = payload["report"]
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    kv("Report ID", row.get("cleaning_report_id"))
    kv("Type", row.get("report_type"))
    kv("Source Files", row.get("source_files"))
    kv("Input", row.get("input_count"))
    kv("Kept", row.get("kept_count"))
    kv("Rejected", row.get("rejected_count"))
    kv("Created", row.get("created_at"))
    report_json = row.get("report_json")
    if report_json is not None:
        if isinstance(report_json, str):
            try:
                report_json = json.loads(report_json)
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                pass
        print("  Report detail:")
        print(json.dumps(report_json, indent=2, default=str)[:4000])
    blank()
    return 0


def do_status() -> int:
    ok = check_connection()
    header("Database Status")
    kv("Connected", "Yes" if ok else "No")
    blank()
    return 0 if ok else 1


def do_init_db() -> int:
    try:
        init_schema()
        ok = check_connection()
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    header("Initialize Database")
    kv("Schema", "Applied")
    kv("Connected", "Yes" if ok else "No")
    success("Database ready.")
    blank()
    return 0 if ok else 1


# ── Content / users ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def do_add_user() -> int:
    header("Add User")
    try:
        result = users.add_user(
            username=prompt_nonempty("Username"),
            password=getpass("Password: "),
            first_name=prompt_nonempty("First name"),
            last_name=prompt_nonempty("Last name"),
            email=prompt_nonempty("Email"),
            role_code=prompt_optional("Role code", "journalist"),
        )
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    success("User created.")
    kv("User ID", result["user_id"])
    kv("Username", result["username"])
    kv("Role", result["role_code"])
    blank()
    return 0


def do_add_category() -> int:
    header("Add Category")
    try:
        cid = content.add_category(
            prompt_nonempty("Category name"),
            prompt_optional("Description"),
        )
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    success("Category created.")
    kv("Category ID", cid)
    blank()
    return 0


def do_add_pitch() -> int:
    header("Add Story Pitch")
    try:
        status = prompt_choice("Status", ALLOWED_PITCH_STATUSES) or "submitted"
        status = require_pitch_status(status)
        pid = content.add_pitch(
            prompt_nonempty("Title"),
            prompt_nonempty("Summary"),
            status=status,
        )
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    success("Story pitch created.")
    kv("Story Pitch ID", pid)
    blank()
    return 0


def do_add_news() -> int:
    header("Add News")
    try:
        status = prompt_choice("Status", ALLOWED_CONTENT_STATUSES) or "draft"
        result = content.add_news(
            title=prompt_nonempty("Title"),
            body=prompt_nonempty("Body"),
            summary=prompt_optional("Summary"),
            event_location=prompt_optional("Event location"),
            severity=prompt_optional("Severity", "low"),
            breaking=prompt_optional("Breaking news? (y/N)", "n").lower() == "y",
            status=status,
        )
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    success("News created.")
    kv("Content ID", result["content_id"])
    kv("News ID", result["news_id"])
    blank()
    return 0


def do_add_article() -> int:
    header("Add Article")
    try:
        status = prompt_choice("Status", ALLOWED_CONTENT_STATUSES) or "draft"
        result = content.add_article(
            title=prompt_nonempty("Title"),
            body=prompt_nonempty("Body"),
            summary=prompt_optional("Summary"),
            status=status,
        )
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    success("Article created.")
    kv("Content ID", result["content_id"])
    kv("Article ID", result["article_id"])
    blank()
    return 0


def do_list_content() -> int:
    try:
        rows = content.list_content(limit=10)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    show_dict_rows(
        "Recent Content",
        rows,
        [
            ("Content ID", "content_id"),
            ("Title", "title"),
            ("Kind", "content_kind"),
            ("Status", "status"),
            ("Source", "source_type"),
        ],
    )
    return 0


def do_list_news() -> int:
    try:
        rows = content.list_news(limit=10)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    show_dict_rows(
        "Recent News",
        rows,
        [
            ("News ID", "news_id"),
            ("Content ID", "content_id"),
            ("Event Title", "event_title"),
            ("Severity", "severity"),
            ("Breaking", "breaking_news_flag"),
        ],
    )
    return 0


def do_list_raw() -> int:
    try:
        rows = content.list_raw(limit=15)
        counts = content.raw_counts()
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    show_stats("Raw Ingest Counts", counts or {"none": 0})
    show_dict_rows(
        "Recent Raw Ingest",
        rows,
        [
            ("Raw ID", "raw_ingest_id"),
            ("Source File", "source_file"),
            ("Title", "title"),
            ("Category", "category"),
            ("Cleaning Status", "cleaning_status"),
            ("Reject Reason", "reject_reason"),
            ("Content ID", "content_id"),
        ],
    )
    return 0


def do_view_content() -> int:
    header("View Content By ID")
    try:
        row = content.get_content(prompt_nonempty("Content ID"))
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    kv("Content ID", row["content_id"])
    kv("Title", row.get("title"))
    kv("Kind", row.get("content_kind"))
    kv("Status", row.get("status"))
    kv("Source", row.get("source_type"))
    kv("Summary", row.get("summary"))
    kv("Raw Body", (row.get("raw_body") or "")[:180])
    kv("Clean Body", (row.get("content_body") or "")[:180])
    blank()
    return 0


def do_update_content_status() -> int:
    header("Update Content Status")
    try:
        content_id = content.get_content(prompt_nonempty("Content ID"))["content_id"]
        status = prompt_choice("New status", ALLOWED_CONTENT_STATUSES)
        if status is None:
            raise ValidationError("Status is required.")
        result = content.update_status(content_id, status)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    success("Content status updated.")
    kv("Content ID", result["content_id"])
    kv("Title", result["title"])
    kv("Status", result["status"])
    blank()
    return 0


# ── Role-based menu ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _build_menu(user: Optional[dict[str, Any]]) -> tuple[str, dict[str, Action]]:
    lines = [
        "",
        "=" * 56,
        "  NewsMind Console",
        "=" * 56,
    ]
    actions: dict[str, Action] = {}
    n = 1

    def add(label: str, fn: Action) -> None:
        nonlocal n
        key = str(n)
        lines.append(f"  {key}) {label}")
        actions[key] = fn
        n += 1

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("  AUTH")
    if user is None:
        add("Login", do_login)
        lines.append("  (login to unlock pipeline + role desk menus)")
        lines.append("  (password reset for users is admin-only after login)")
    else:
        add("Logout", do_logout)
        add("Change password", do_change_password)
        role = user["role_code"]
        caps = set(user.get("capabilities") or [])
        lines.append("")
        lines.append(f"  Logged in as - {user['username']}")
        lines.append(f"  Role - {user.get('role_name') or role}")

        pipeline_opts = [
            ("List CSV files (new/changed marked)", do_list_csv_files, CAP_INGEST),
            ("Ingest one CSV file", do_ingest_one, CAP_INGEST),
            ("Ingest new/changed CSV files only", do_ingest_new, CAP_INGEST),
            ("Ingest all CSV files", do_ingest, CAP_INGEST),
            ("Clean data", do_clean, CAP_CLEAN),
            ("Ingest + Clean (new only)", do_ingest_and_clean_new, CAP_CLEAN),
            ("Ingest + Clean (all)", do_ingest_and_clean, CAP_CLEAN),
            ("Persist cleaned data", do_persist, CAP_PERSIST),
            ("Pipeline one CSV file", do_pipeline_one, CAP_PIPELINE),
            ("Full pipeline (new files only)", do_pipeline_new, CAP_PIPELINE),
            ("Full pipeline (all files)", do_pipeline, CAP_PIPELINE),
            ("List cleaning reports (DB)", do_list_reports, CAP_CLEAN),
            ("View cleaning report by ID", do_view_report, CAP_CLEAN),
        ]
        visible = [(label, fn) for label, fn, cap in pipeline_opts if has_capability(role, cap)]
        if visible:
            lines.append("")
            lines.append("  DATA PIPELINE")
            for label, fn in visible:
                add(label, fn)

        lines.append("")
        lines.append(f"  {str(role).upper().replace('_', ' ')} DESK")

        if role == ROLE_ADMIN or CAP_ADMIN in caps:
            add("Add user", do_add_user)
            add("Reset user password", do_admin_reset_password)
            add("Add category", do_add_category)
            add("Add story pitch", do_add_pitch)
            add("Add news", do_add_news)
            add("Add article", do_add_article)
            add("View content by ID", do_view_content)
            add("Update content status", do_update_content_status)
            add("List content", do_list_content)
            add("List news", do_list_news)
            add("List raw ingest", do_list_raw)
        elif role == ROLE_JOURNALIST:
            add("Add news", do_add_news)
            add("Add article", do_add_article)
            add("View content by ID", do_view_content)
            add("List content", do_list_content)
            add("List news", do_list_news)
            add("List raw ingest", do_list_raw)
        elif role == ROLE_AUDIENCE_ANALYST:
            add("Add story pitch", do_add_pitch)
            add("Add category", do_add_category)
            add("List content", do_list_content)
            add("List raw ingest", do_list_raw)
        elif role == ROLE_EDITOR:
            add("View content by ID", do_view_content)
            add("List content", do_list_content)
            add("List news", do_list_news)
            if has_capability(role, CAP_PITCH_REVIEW):
                add("Update content status (review)", do_update_content_status)
        elif role == ROLE_PUBLISHER:
            add("View content by ID", do_view_content)
            add("List content", do_list_content)
            add("List news", do_list_news)
            add("Update content status (publish)", do_update_content_status)
        elif role == ROLE_NEWSROOM_MANAGER:
            add("Add category", do_add_category)
            add("View content by ID", do_view_content)
            add("List content", do_list_content)
            add("List news", do_list_news)
            add("Update content status", do_update_content_status)
            add("List raw ingest", do_list_raw)
        elif role == ROLE_ADAPTATION_EDITOR:
            add("View content by ID", do_view_content)
            add("List content", do_list_content)
            add("List news", do_list_news)
        else:
            lines.append("  (no desk actions configured for this role)")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("  SYSTEM")
    add("DB status", do_status)

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("  0) Exit")
    lines.append("=" * 56)
    lines.append("")
    return "\n".join(lines), actions


def interactive() -> int:
    info("NewsMind console ready. Login to unlock your role menu.")
    while True:
        user = _current_user()
        menu_text, actions = _build_menu(user)
        print(menu_text)
        try:
            choice = input("Choose option: ").strip()
        except (KeyboardInterrupt, EOFError):
            print("\nBye.")
            return 0
        if choice == "0" or is_cancel(choice):
            print("Bye.")
            return 0
        action = actions.get(choice)
        if action is None:
            error("Invalid option.")
            continue
        info("Tip: q / exit cancels this step | Ctrl+C cancels a running step")
        try:
            action()
        except UserCancelled:
            info("Action cancelled. Back to menu.")
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            print("\nCancelled. Back to menu.")
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            _handle(exc)
        try:
            nxt = input("\nPress Enter to continue: ").strip()
        except (KeyboardInterrupt, EOFError):
            print("\nBye.")
            return 0
        if is_cancel(nxt) or nxt == "0":
            print("Bye.")
            return 0


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="NewsMind console application")
    parser.add_argument("--menu", action="store_true", help="Open interactive console menu")
    parser.add_argument("--init-db", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--status", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--login", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--logout", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--username", default="")
    parser.add_argument("--password", default="")
    parser.add_argument("--list-csv", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--ingest", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--ingest-new", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--ingest-file", default="", help="Relative path under data/raw")
    parser.add_argument("--clean", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--ingest-clean", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--persist", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--pipeline", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--pipeline-new", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--pipeline-file", default="", help="Relative path under data/raw")
    parser.add_argument("--list-reports", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--view-report", type=int, default=0)
    args = parser.parse_args()

    if len(sys.argv) == 1 or args.menu:
        return interactive()

    if args.init_db:
        return do_init_db()
    if args.status:
        return do_status()
    if args.login:
        return do_login(args.username, args.password)
    if args.logout:
        return do_logout()
    if args.list_csv:
        return do_list_csv_files()
    if args.ingest_file:
        return _run_admin(lambda: admin.ingest_file(args.ingest_file), title=f"Ingest File — {args.ingest_file}")
    if args.ingest_new:
        return do_ingest_new()
    if args.ingest:
        return do_ingest()
    if args.clean:
        return do_clean()
    if args.ingest_clean:
        return do_ingest_and_clean()
    if args.persist:
        return do_persist()
    if args.pipeline_file:
        return _run_admin(lambda: admin.pipeline_file(args.pipeline_file), title=f"Pipeline File — {args.pipeline_file}")
    if args.pipeline_new:
        return do_pipeline_new()
    if args.pipeline:
        return do_pipeline()
    if args.list_reports:
        return do_list_reports()
    if args.view_report:
        try:
            payload = admin.view_report(args.view_report)
            row = payload["report"]
            print(json.dumps(row, indent=2, default=str))
            return 0
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            return _handle(exc)

    parser.print_help()
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

"""Data cleaning package.

cleaners/
  orchestrator.py, pipeline.py, quality_report.py
  stages/   text → null → duplicate → schema
"""

from cleaners.orchestrator import CleaningOrchestrator, CleaningService
from cleaners.pipeline import CleaningPipeline
from cleaners.quality_report import QualityReport

__all__ = ["CleaningOrchestrator", "CleaningService", "CleaningPipeline", "QualityReport"]


==============================================================================
FILE: cleaners/orchestrator.py
==============================================================================
"""Cleaning orchestration — pipeline run + optional local file snapshot."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pandas as pd

from cleaners.pipeline import CleaningPipeline
from config.runtime import write_processed_files
from utils.helpers import project_path
from utils.logger import get_logger

log = get_logger("cleaning")


class CleaningOrchestrator:
    """Runs cleaners. Report persistence to DB is done by ReportService."""

    def __init__(self, processed_dir: Path | None = None) -> None:
        self.processed_dir = processed_dir or project_path("data", "processed")
        self.processed_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    def clean_content(
        self,
        records: list[dict[str, Any]],
        *,
        write_files: bool | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if write_files is None:
            write_files = write_processed_files()
        pipeline = CleaningPipeline(record_type="content")
        result = pipeline.run(records)
        report = pipeline.report.to_dict()
        report_path = ""
        cleaned_csv = ""
        if write_files:
            report_path_obj = self.processed_dir / "quality_report_content.json"
            report_path_obj.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
            out_csv = self.processed_dir / "cleaned_content.csv"
            pd.DataFrame(result.kept).to_csv(out_csv, index=False)
            report_path = str(report_path_obj)
            cleaned_csv = str(out_csv)
        log.info("Content cleaning kept=%s rejected=%s", len(result.kept), len(result.rejected))
        return {
            "kept": result.kept,
            "rejected": result.rejected,
            "stats": result.stats,
            "report": report,
            "report_path": report_path,
            "cleaned_csv": cleaned_csv,
        }

    def clean_pitches(
        self,
        records: list[dict[str, Any]],
        *,
        write_files: bool | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if write_files is None:
            write_files = write_processed_files()
        pipeline = CleaningPipeline(record_type="pitch")
        result = pipeline.run(records)
        report = pipeline.report.to_dict()
        report_path = ""
        if write_files:
            report_path_obj = self.processed_dir / "quality_report_pitches.json"
            report_path_obj.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
            report_path = str(report_path_obj)
        log.info("Pitch cleaning kept=%s rejected=%s", len(result.kept), len(result.rejected))
        return {
            "kept": result.kept,
            "rejected": result.rejected,
            "stats": result.stats,
            "report": report,
            "report_path": report_path,
        }


CleaningService = CleaningOrchestrator


==============================================================================
FILE: cleaners/pipeline.py
==============================================================================
"""Ordered cleaner chain."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from cleaners.quality_report import QualityReport
from cleaners.stages import (
    BaseCleaner,
    CleanerResult,
    DuplicateCleaner,
    NullCleaner,
    SchemaCleaner,
    TextCleaner,
)


class CleaningPipeline:
    def __init__(self, cleaners: list[BaseCleaner] | None = None, record_type: str = "content") -> None:
        self.cleaners = cleaners or [
            TextCleaner(),
            NullCleaner(),
            DuplicateCleaner(),
            SchemaCleaner(record_type=record_type),
        ]
        self.report = QualityReport()

    def run(self, records: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> CleanerResult:
        from utils.progress import progress

        current = list(records)
        all_rejected: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        input_count = len(records)
        progress(f"Cleaner chain starting ({input_count} row(s), {len(self.cleaners)} stage(s))...")
        for cleaner in self.cleaners:
            progress(f"  Stage: {cleaner.name} ({len(current)} row(s) in)...")
            result = cleaner.clean(current)
            self.report.add_stage(cleaner.name, result.stats, result.rejected)
            all_rejected.extend(result.rejected)
            current = result.kept
            progress(
                f"  Stage {cleaner.name} done: kept={len(result.kept)} "
                f"rejected={len(result.rejected)}"
            )
        self.report.finalize(input_count, len(current))
        progress(
            f"Cleaner chain finished: kept={len(current)} rejected={len(all_rejected)}"
        )
        return CleanerResult(
            kept=current,
            rejected=all_rejected,
            stats={
                "input": input_count,
                "kept": len(current),
                "rejected": len(all_rejected),
            },
        )


==============================================================================
FILE: cleaners/quality_report.py
==============================================================================
"""Before/after quality metrics and reject reasons."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections import Counter
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from utils.helpers import project_path


@dataclass
class QualityReport:
    input_count: int = 0
    output_count: int = 0
    rejected_count: int = 0
    stage_stats: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
    reject_reasons: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def add_stage(self, name: str, stats: dict[str, Any], rejected: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
        self.stage_stats.append({"cleaner": name, **stats})
        reasons = Counter(str(r.get("_reject_reason") or "unknown") for r in rejected)
        for key, value in reasons.items():
            self.reject_reasons[key] = self.reject_reasons.get(key, 0) + value

    def finalize(self, input_count: int, output_count: int) -> None:
        self.input_count = input_count
        self.output_count = output_count
        self.rejected_count = max(0, input_count - output_count)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "generated_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
            "input_count": self.input_count,
            "output_count": self.output_count,
            "rejected_count": self.rejected_count,
            "reject_reasons": self.reject_reasons,
            "stage_stats": self.stage_stats,
        }

    def write(self, filename: str = "quality_report.json") -> Path:
        out_dir = project_path("data", "processed")
        out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path = out_dir / filename
        path.write_text(json.dumps(self.to_dict(), indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        return path


==============================================================================
FILE: cleaners/README.md
==============================================================================
# Learning guide: `cleaners/`

## Why this folder exists

CSV ingestion produces **messy rows**: stray whitespace, duplicate headlines, null titles, invalid status strings. Cleaning is a dedicated stage between **in-memory / raw_ingest records** and **persisted content**.

The `cleaners/` package owns the **pipeline pattern** (ordered stages), **quality reporting**, and **orchestration** that writes optional snapshots to `data/processed/`. It does not own PostgreSQL report rows — `ReportService` persists summaries to `cleaning_report` after admin runs.

---

## How cleaners connect to the app

```
AdminModule.clean / ingest_and_clean / run_pipeline
        ↓
CleaningOrchestrator (orchestrator.py)
        ↓
CleaningPipeline (pipeline.py)
        ↓
stages: TextCleaner → NullCleaner → DuplicateCleaner → SchemaCleaner
```

`IngestionService` supplies `list[dict]` records. `PersistenceService` consumes `kept` records after cleaning. Rejected rows carry `_reject_reason` or stage metadata for raw ingest status updates.

---

## Core files

### `orchestrator.py` — class `CleaningOrchestrator`

Also aliased as `CleaningService` for older tests.

**`__init__(processed_dir)`** — defaults to `data/processed`, creates directory if needed.

**`clean_content(records, write_files=True)`** — builds `CleaningPipeline(record_type="content")`, runs stages, optionally writes `quality_report_content.json` and `cleaned_content.csv` via pandas DataFrame export.

**`clean_pitches(records, write_files=True)`** — same pattern with `record_type="pitch"` and pitch quality JSON path.

Returns dict with `kept`, `rejected`, `stats`, `report`, paths. Logs kept/rejected counts at INFO.

### `pipeline.py` — class `CleaningPipeline`

Constructs default stage list or accepts custom `cleaners` list for tests.

**`run(records)`** — loops cleaners in order; each stage receives output of previous; accumulates rejections; feeds `QualityReport` per stage; returns `CleanerResult` dataclass-like object with `kept`, `rejected`, `stats`.

### `quality_report.py`

Collects per-stage statistics for JSON export and DB report serialization (consumed by orchestrator and ReportService).

### `stages/` package

Individual cleaner implementations — see **`cleaners/stages/README.md`** for Text → Null → Duplicate → Schema deep dive.

---

## Technology notes

- **pandas** — used only for writing cleaned CSV snapshot, not for stage logic.
- **In-memory dict records** — same shape as ingestion normalization; stages mutate copies per row.
- **Config constants** — `SchemaCleaner` imports allowed statuses and content kinds from `config.constants` so desk and pipeline agree.

---

## Exercises

1. Run clean on a file with intentional duplicate titles — inspect `rejected` reasons in quality JSON.
2. Pass a custom cleaner list into `CleaningPipeline` in a REPL — skip `DuplicateCleaner` and observe diff.
3. Trace where `CleaningReportDAO` is written relative to orchestrator return value (in ReportService / AdminModule).
4. Read stage README and explain why Text runs before Schema.

    "BaseCleaner",
    "CleanerResult",
    "DuplicateCleaner",
    "NullCleaner",
    "SchemaCleaner",
    "TextCleaner",
