==============================================================================
FILE: cleaners/stages/base_cleaner.py
==============================================================================
"""Abstract cleaner interface."""

from __future__ import annotations

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any


@dataclass
class CleanerResult:
    kept: list[dict[str, Any]]
    rejected: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
    stats: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)


class BaseCleaner(ABC):
    name: str = "base"

    @abstractmethod
    def clean(self, records: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> CleanerResult:
        raise NotImplementedError


==============================================================================
FILE: cleaners/stages/duplicate_cleaner.py
==============================================================================
"""Remove exact / normalized-title duplicates."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from cleaners.stages.base_cleaner import BaseCleaner, CleanerResult
from utils.helpers import normalize_key


class DuplicateCleaner(BaseCleaner):
    name = "duplicate_cleaner"

    def clean(self, records: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> CleanerResult:
        kept: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        rejected: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        seen: set[str] = set()
        for row in records:
            key = normalize_key(str(row.get("title") or ""))
            if not key:
                rejected.append({**row, "_reject_reason": "empty_title_key"})
                continue
            if key in seen:
                rejected.append({**row, "_reject_reason": "duplicate_title"})
                continue
            seen.add(key)
            kept.append(row)
        return CleanerResult(
            kept=kept,
            rejected=rejected,
            stats={
                "input": len(records),
                "kept": len(kept),
                "rejected": len(rejected),
                "unique_titles": len(seen),
            },
        )


==============================================================================
FILE: cleaners/stages/null_cleaner.py
==============================================================================
"""Drop or reject records missing required fields."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from cleaners.stages.base_cleaner import BaseCleaner, CleanerResult
from utils.helpers import coerce_str


class NullCleaner(BaseCleaner):
    name = "null_cleaner"

    def __init__(self, required_fields: tuple[str, ...] = ("title", "text")) -> None:
        self.required_fields = required_fields

    def clean(self, records: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> CleanerResult:
        kept: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        rejected: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for row in records:
            title = coerce_str(row.get("title") or row.get("headline") or row.get("headline_text"))
            body = coerce_str(
                row.get("text")
                or row.get("content_body")
                or row.get("description")
                or row.get("short_description")
            )
            # Headline-only: allow title to fill body
            if title and not body:
                body = title
                row = {**row, "text": body, "content_body": body}

            if not title:
                rejected.append({**row, "_reject_reason": "missing_title"})
                continue
            if not body:
                rejected.append({**row, "_reject_reason": "missing_body"})
                continue

            out = dict(row)
            out["title"] = title
            out["text"] = body
            out["content_body"] = body
            kept.append(out)
        return CleanerResult(
            kept=kept,
            rejected=rejected,
            stats={"input": len(records), "kept": len(kept), "rejected": len(rejected)},
        )


==============================================================================
FILE: cleaners/stages/README.md
==============================================================================
# Learning guide: `cleaners/stages/`

## Why this folder exists

A monolithic “clean everything” function is hard to test and hard to teach. Each **stage** implements one concern. `CleaningPipeline` runs stages in a fixed order so behavior is predictable: normalize text before checking nulls, drop nulls before duplicate detection, enforce schema last on rows that survived structural checks.

---

## Stage order and rationale

1. **TextCleaner** — strip noise so null/duplicate checks see real content.
2. **NullCleaner** — reject rows missing required fields (e.g. title).
3. **DuplicateCleaner** — collapse or reject duplicate keys within the batch.
4. **SchemaCleaner** — coerce types, map statuses to allow-lists, run `validate_content_record`.

Changing this order in `pipeline.py` without understanding dependencies will produce surprising rejections — treat the order as part of the contract.

---

## Shared abstractions (`base_cleaner.py`)

**`CleanerResult`** — holds `kept` list, `rejected` list (often with reason metadata), and `stats` dict for quality report.

**`BaseCleaner`** — subclasses implement `name` and `clean(records) -> CleanerResult`. Pipeline uses `cleaner.name` as the stage key in reports.

---

## `text_cleaner.py` — class `TextCleaner`

Trims strings, normalizes whitespace on text fields, prepares `title` and body columns for downstream checks. Does not reject rows unless a subclass rule does — primarily mutates in place.

---

## `null_cleaner.py` — class `NullCleaner`

Rejects rows where mandatory fields are empty after text pass. Rejected rows should include enough context for `raw_ingest.reject_reason` when persisted.

---

## `duplicate_cleaner.py` — class `DuplicateCleaner`

Detects duplicates within the **current batch** (typically by normalized title or composite key). Keeps first occurrence or applies project rule; others go to `rejected` with duplicate reason.

---

## `schema_cleaner.py` — class `SchemaCleaner`

**Constructor `record_type`** — `"content"` vs `"pitch"` switches allowed status sets (`ALLOWED_CONTENT_STATUSES` vs `ALLOWED_PITCH_STATUSES`).

Per row:

- Coerces `title`, body (`text` / `content_body`), category fields via `coerce_str`.
- Sets `content_kind` defaulting to `article` if unknown (filtered against `CONTENT_KINDS`).
- Parses dates with `_parse_date` helper trying multiple formats.
- Runs **`validate_content_record`** — same function desk CRUD uses — so a row invalid in the console is invalid in the pipeline.

Rows failing validation move to `rejected`; survivors are normalized dicts ready for `PersistenceService`.

---

## `__init__.py` exports

Re-exports cleaners for `from cleaners.stages import TextCleaner, …` in `pipeline.py`.

---

## Connection to utils and config

- **`utils.helpers.coerce_str`** — safe string extraction from CSV oddities.
- **`utils.validators.validate_content_record`** — single validation brain.
- **`config.constants`** — authoritative allow-lists for status and kind.

---

## Exercises

1. Unit-test one stage in isolation with three rows — read `tests/test_cleaners.py` for patterns.
2. Feed a row with illegal status — confirm SchemaCleaner maps or rejects per `record_type`.
3. Add a temporary debug print in `DuplicateCleaner` and run integration pipeline — verify order of execution.
4. Explain in prose why SchemaCleaner should not run before NullCleaner.


==============================================================================
FILE: cleaners/stages/schema_cleaner.py
==============================================================================
"""Type coercion, enums, date parsing."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date, datetime
from typing import Any, Optional

from cleaners.stages.base_cleaner import BaseCleaner, CleanerResult
from config.constants import ALLOWED_CONTENT_STATUSES, ALLOWED_PITCH_STATUSES, CONTENT_KINDS
from utils.helpers import coerce_str
from utils.validators import validate_content_record


def _parse_date(value: Any) -> Optional[date]:
    if value is None or value == "":
        return None
    if isinstance(value, date) and not isinstance(value, datetime):
        return value
    text = coerce_str(value)
    for fmt in ("%Y-%m-%d", "%d/%m/%Y", "%m/%d/%Y", "%Y/%m/%d"):
        try:
            return datetime.strptime(text[:10], fmt).date()
        except ValueError:
            continue
    return None


class SchemaCleaner(BaseCleaner):
    name = "schema_cleaner"

    def __init__(self, record_type: str = "content") -> None:
        self.record_type = record_type

    def clean(self, records: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> CleanerResult:
        kept: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        rejected: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for row in records:
            out = dict(row)
            out["title"] = coerce_str(out.get("title"))
            body = coerce_str(out.get("text") or out.get("content_body"))
            out["text"] = body
            out["content_body"] = body
            out["category"] = coerce_str(out.get("category") or out.get("category_name") or "City")
            out["category_name"] = out["category"]
            kind = coerce_str(out.get("content_kind") or out.get("kind") or "article").lower()
            out["content_kind"] = kind if kind in CONTENT_KINDS else "article"
            status = coerce_str(out.get("status") or "draft").lower()
            if self.record_type == "pitch":
                out["status"] = status if status in ALLOWED_PITCH_STATUSES else "submitted"
            else:
                out["status"] = status if status in ALLOWED_CONTENT_STATUSES else "draft"
            out["published_date"] = _parse_date(out.get("date") or out.get("published_date"))
            for score_key in ("trend_score", "audience_interest_score", "coverage_gap_score"):
                try:
                    out[score_key] = float(out.get(score_key) or 0.0)
                except (TypeError, ValueError):
                    out[score_key] = 0.0

            if self.record_type != "pitch":
                errors = validate_content_record(out)
                if errors:
                    rejected.append({**out, "_reject_reason": ",".join(errors)})
                    continue
            kept.append(out)
        return CleanerResult(
            kept=kept,
            rejected=rejected,
            stats={"input": len(records), "kept": len(kept), "rejected": len(rejected)},
        )


==============================================================================
FILE: cleaners/stages/text_cleaner.py
==============================================================================
"""Normalize text fields — whitespace, HTML noise."""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from typing import Any

from cleaners.stages.base_cleaner import BaseCleaner, CleanerResult
from utils.helpers import coerce_str


def _scrub(text: str) -> str:
    text = coerce_str(text)
    text = re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", " ", text)
    text = text.replace("\u00a0", " ")
    text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip()
    return text


class TextCleaner(BaseCleaner):
    name = "text_cleaner"

    def clean(self, records: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> CleanerResult:
        kept: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for row in records:
            out = dict(row)
            out["title"] = _scrub(str(out.get("title") or ""))
            body = _scrub(str(out.get("text") or out.get("content_body") or ""))
            out["text"] = body
            out["content_body"] = body
            if out.get("summary"):
                out["summary"] = _scrub(str(out["summary"]))
            if out.get("category"):
                out["category"] = _scrub(str(out["category"]))
            kept.append(out)
        return CleanerResult(
            kept=kept,
            rejected=[],
            stats={"input": len(records), "kept": len(kept), "rejected": 0},
        )

FILE: config/db_config.py

"""Database configuration for the NewsMind console backend (local or hosted)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from dataclasses import dataclass

from config.env_loader import load_dotenv
from config.runtime import db_connect_timeout, db_sslmode


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DatabaseConfig:
    host: str
    port: int
    name: str
    user: str
    password: str
    connect_timeout: int = 10
    sslmode: str = "prefer"

    def dsn_kwargs(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "host": self.host,
            "port": self.port,
            "dbname": self.name,
            "user": self.user,
            "password": self.password,
            "connect_timeout": self.connect_timeout,
            "sslmode": self.sslmode,
            # TCP keepalives for hosted / remote Postgres connections
            "keepalives": 1,
            "keepalives_idle": 30,
            "keepalives_interval": 10,
            "keepalives_count": 3,
        }


def load_db_config() -> DatabaseConfig:
    load_dotenv()
    return DatabaseConfig(
        host=os.environ.get("NEWSROOM_DB_HOST", "localhost"),
        port=int(os.environ.get("NEWSROOM_DB_PORT", "5432")),
        name=os.environ.get("NEWSROOM_DB_NAME", "newsmind_console"),
        user=os.environ.get("NEWSROOM_DB_USER", "postgres"),
        password=os.environ.get("NEWSROOM_DB_PASSWORD", "admin"),
        connect_timeout=db_connect_timeout(),
        sslmode=db_sslmode(),
    )


==============================================================================
FILE: config/env_loader.py
==============================================================================
"""Load key=value pairs from project .env into os.environ (no extra dependency)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
_LOADED = False


def load_dotenv(path: Path | None = None) -> Path | None:
    global _LOADED
    if _LOADED:
        return path
    env_path = path or (ROOT / ".env")
    if not env_path.exists():
        _LOADED = True
        return None
    for raw in env_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
        line = raw.strip()
        if not line or line.startswith("#") or "=" not in line:
            continue
        key, _, value = line.partition("=")
        key = key.strip()
        value = value.strip().strip('"').strip("'")
        if key and key not in os.environ:
            os.environ[key] = value
    _LOADED = True
    return env_path


==============================================================================
FILE: config/README.md
==============================================================================
# Learning guide: `config/`

## Why this folder exists

Every real application needs a place for **settings that are not business logic**: database host, role names, password rules, allowed statuses. If those values were hard-coded inside DAOs or `app.py`, changing one constant would mean hunting through dozens of files.

`config/` is that single place. Other packages **import from here**; they do not redefine “what is a journalist” or “what is draft status”.

### How it connects to the rest of the project

```
.env  →  env_loader / db_config  →  database/connection.py  →  every DAO
constants.py  →  validators, permission_service, auth, cleaners, app menus
```

- When you run `python app.py --init-db`, connection settings come from `.env` via `db_config`.
- When a journalist logs in, `ROLE_CAPABILITIES` decides which pipeline menu items appear.
- When SchemaCleaner checks status, it uses `ALLOWED_CONTENT_STATUSES` from here.

---

## Technology concepts used in this folder

### `from __future__ import annotations`

In Python, a normal import like `from datetime import datetime` loads a real object immediately. Type hints (the bits after `:`) are also expressions — historically Python tried to evaluate them at import time.

`from __future__ import annotations` tells Python: **treat all annotations as postponed (stored as strings / deferred)**. Practical benefits in this project:

1. You can write modern unions like `Path | None` more safely across versions.
2. You can refer to types that are defined later in the file without quoting them.
3. Import-time work is lighter because annotations are not fully evaluated up front.

You will see this line at the top of almost every `.py` file in NewsMind. It is a **language feature**, not a third-party library.

### `os.environ`

Operating systems expose environment variables (name → string). Cloud hosts, Docker, and local shells use them for secrets so passwords are not baked into source code.

`os.environ.get("NEWSROOM_DB_PASSWORD", "admin")` means: use the env value if set, otherwise default to `"admin"` for local learning.

### `dataclasses.dataclass`

Writing a class with `__init__` that only stores fields is repetitive. `@dataclass` auto-builds `__init__`, `__repr__`, etc. With `frozen=True`, instances are **immutable** — once `DatabaseConfig` is created, nothing can change `host` by accident mid-request. That matches the idea of “config is read-only after load”.

### `pathlib.Path`

Older Python used `os.path.join("a", "b")` strings. `Path` objects understand the OS path rules (Windows `\` vs Linux `/`) and give helpers like `.exists()`, `.read_text()`, `.joinpath()`. NewsMind uses Path everywhere for project files.

---

## File-by-file walkthrough

### `__init__.py`

An empty or tiny file that marks the directory as a **Python package**. Without it (on some setups), `import config.db_config` can fail. Its purpose is packaging, not logic.

### `env_loader.py` — loading `.env` without extra packages

**Purpose:** Read a local `.env` file into `os.environ` so `db_config` can stay simple.

**Why not `python-dotenv`?** The project keeps dependencies small (`pandas`, `psycopg2`, `pytest`). A 20-line parser is enough for learning and local runs.

**Function: `load_dotenv(path=None)`**

1. Finds `New folder/.env` (project root next to `config/`).
2. Skips blank lines and `#` comments.
3. Splits each line on the first `=`.
4. Sets `os.environ[key] = value` **only if the key is not already set** — so a PowerShell `$env:NEWSROOM_DB_NAME = "..."` still wins. That is important for CI and overrides.
5. Uses a module-level `_LOADED` flag so repeated calls do not re-read the file every time a DAO opens a connection.

**Connection:** Called first thing inside `load_db_config()`, and also at the top of `app.py` via `load_dotenv()` so the console process sees env early.

### `db_config.py` — database settings object

**Class: `DatabaseConfig`**

Fields: `host`, `port`, `name`, `user`, `password`.

**Method: `dsn_kwargs()`**  
Converts the object into the keyword dict `psycopg2.connect(**...)` expects (`dbname` not `name`). This keeps driver-specific naming in one place.

**Function: `load_db_config()`**

1. `load_dotenv()`
2. Build `DatabaseConfig` from `NEWSROOM_DB_*` variables with defaults (`localhost`, `5432`, `newsmind_console`, …).

**Connection:** `database/connection.py` calls this whenever it needs a connection. You should not scatter `os.environ.get("NEWSROOM_DB_HOST")` inside DAOs.

### `constants.py` — shared vocabulary of the newsroom app

Think of this file as the **dictionary** the whole codebase agrees on.

#### Status allow-lists

- `ALLOWED_CONTENT_STATUSES` — draft → submitted → approved/rejected → published  
- `ALLOWED_PITCH_STATUSES` — pitch workflow  
- `ALLOWED_ACCOUNT_STATUSES` — user account state  

`utils/validators.py` and cleaners import these so invalid statuses are rejected the same way in console create forms and CSV cleaning.

#### Length rules

`MIN_TITLE_LENGTH`, `MIN_BODY_LENGTH`, `MAX_TITLE_LENGTH` keep “news-like” quality: tiny titles/bodies are rejected by SchemaCleaner and validators.

#### Roles and capabilities (RBAC)

**RBAC** = Role-Based Access Control. Instead of checking `if username == "admin"` everywhere:

1. User has a **role** (`journalist`, `editor`, …).
2. Each role has a tuple of **capabilities** (`ingest`, `clean`, `publish`, …).
3. Menus and modules ask: `has_capability(role, "ingest")`?

`ROLE_CAPABILITIES` is the map. Example: publishers get `CAP_PUBLISH` only; journalists get pipeline caps; administrators get everything (and `permission_service` also short-circuits admin to allow all).

**Connection:**

- `services/permission_service.py` reads this map.
- `app.py` builds menus from capabilities.
- `modules/admin` requires `CAP_INGEST` / `CAP_CLEAN` / etc. via `require_capability_session`.

#### Security constants

`PASSWORD_HASH_ITERATIONS`, `PASSWORD_SALT_BYTES`, `SESSION_TTL_HOURS`, lockout settings — used by `modules/authentication/security.py` and auth service so crypto policy is tunable in one file.

#### `DEMO_PASSWORDS`

Only for local learning/demo seed users. In a real deployment you would remove this and force password reset emails.

---

## Learning checklist

1. Change `NEWSROOM_DB_NAME` in `.env`, restart app, run DB status — you are practicing env-based config.
2. Add a new capability string and give it to one role — see how menus change after login.
3. Trace: `constants.ALLOWED_CONTENT_STATUSES` → `validators.require_content_status` → `ContentService.update_content_status` → SQL update.


==============================================================================
FILE: config/runtime.py
==============================================================================
"""Runtime settings loaded from environment (.env).

Controls CSV reading batches, DB insert batches, processed-file snapshots,
multi-file pipeline strategy, and hosted Postgres connect options.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os

from config.env_loader import load_dotenv


def _int(name: str, default: int) -> int:
    load_dotenv()
    raw = os.environ.get(name)
    if raw is None or str(raw).strip() == "":
        return default
    try:
        return int(raw)
    except ValueError:
        return default


def _bool(name: str, default: bool) -> bool:
    load_dotenv()
    raw = os.environ.get(name)
    if raw is None or str(raw).strip() == "":
        return default
    return str(raw).strip().lower() in {"1", "true", "yes", "on"}


def csv_chunk_rows() -> int:
    """Pandas chunk size when reading CSV files."""
    return max(500, _int("NEWSROOM_CSV_CHUNK_ROWS", 2000))


def db_insert_batch() -> int:
    """Rows per batch when bulk-inserting raw ingest rows."""
    return max(50, _int("NEWSROOM_DB_INSERT_BATCH", 200))


def max_csv_mb_warn() -> int:
    """Warn in logs/console when a single CSV is larger than this (MB)."""
    return max(1, _int("NEWSROOM_MAX_CSV_MB_WARN", 15))


def write_processed_files() -> bool:
    """Write optional snapshots under data/processed (DB reports remain source of truth)."""
    return _bool("NEWSROOM_WRITE_PROCESSED_FILES", False)


def pipeline_file_by_file() -> bool:
    """When true, multi-file pipelines run one CSV at a time (ingest→clean→persist)."""
    return _bool("NEWSROOM_PIPELINE_FILE_BY_FILE", True)


def db_connect_timeout() -> int:
    return max(3, _int("NEWSROOM_DB_CONNECT_TIMEOUT", 10))


def db_sslmode() -> str:
    load_dotenv()
    return os.environ.get("NEWSROOM_DB_SSLMODE", "prefer").strip() or "prefer"

"""CAE adaptation tables — requests, drafts, reviews, finals."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from typing import Any, Optional

from dao.base_dao import BaseDAO
from database.connection import db_cursor
from utils.errors import NotFoundError, ValidationError
from utils.validators import require_nonempty, require_positive_id, require_title


def _json(value: Any) -> str:
    return json.dumps(value if value is not None else {})


class AdaptationDAO(BaseDAO):
    table = "adaptation_requests"

    def create_request(
        self,
        *,
        original_title: str,
        original_content: str,
        created_by: int | None,
        status: str = "PROCESSING",
    ) -> int:
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO adaptation_requests (
                    original_title, original_content, created_by, status
                )
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s)
                RETURNING id
                """,
                (
                    require_title(original_title),
                    require_nonempty(original_content, "Original content"),
                    created_by,
                    require_nonempty(status, "Status"),
                ),
            )
            return int(cur.fetchone()["id"])

    def update_request_status(self, request_id: int, status: str) -> int:
        request_id = require_positive_id(request_id, "Request ID")
        return int(
            self.execute(
                """
                UPDATE adaptation_requests
                SET status = %s, updated_at = NOW()
                WHERE id = %s
                """,
                (require_nonempty(status, "Status"), request_id),
            )
        )

    def get_request(self, request_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        request_id = require_positive_id(request_id, "Request ID")
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute("SELECT * FROM adaptation_requests WHERE id = %s", (request_id,))
            row = cur.fetchone()
            return dict(row) if row else None

    def require_request(self, request_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row = self.get_request(request_id)
        if not row:
            raise NotFoundError(f"Adaptation request {request_id} was not found.")
        return row

    def list_drafts_for_request(self, request_id: int) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        request_id = require_positive_id(request_id, "Request ID")
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                SELECT * FROM adaptation_drafts
                WHERE request_id = %s
                ORDER BY platform, version_number
                """,
                (request_id,),
            )
            return [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall() or []]

    def create_draft(
        self,
        *,
        request_id: int,
        platform: str,
        version_number: int,
        adapted_title: str,
        adapted_content: str,
        model_version: str,
        status: str,
        parent_draft_id: int | None = None,
        generation_parameters: dict | None = None,
        validation_flags: dict | None = None,
    ) -> int:
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO adaptation_drafts (
                    request_id, platform, version_number, adapted_title, adapted_content,
                    model_version, status, parent_draft_id, generation_parameters, validation_flags
                )
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s::jsonb, %s::jsonb)
                RETURNING id
                """,
                (
                    require_positive_id(request_id, "Request ID"),
                    require_nonempty(platform, "Platform"),
                    int(version_number),
                    require_title(adapted_title),
                    require_nonempty(adapted_content, "Adapted content"),
                    require_nonempty(model_version, "Model version"),
                    require_nonempty(status, "Status"),
                    parent_draft_id,
                    _json(generation_parameters),
                    _json(validation_flags),
                ),
            )
            return int(cur.fetchone()["id"])

    def get_draft(self, draft_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        draft_id = require_positive_id(draft_id, "Draft ID")
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute("SELECT * FROM adaptation_drafts WHERE id = %s", (draft_id,))
            row = cur.fetchone()
            return dict(row) if row else None

    def require_draft(self, draft_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row = self.get_draft(draft_id)
        if not row:
            raise NotFoundError(f"Adaptation draft {draft_id} was not found.")
        return row

    def update_draft_status(self, draft_id: int, status: str) -> int:
        draft_id = require_positive_id(draft_id, "Draft ID")
        return int(
            self.execute(
                "UPDATE adaptation_drafts SET status = %s WHERE id = %s",
                (require_nonempty(status, "Status"), draft_id),
            )
        )

    def list_versions(self, request_id: int, platform: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                SELECT * FROM adaptation_drafts
                WHERE request_id = %s AND platform = %s
                ORDER BY version_number
                """,
                (require_positive_id(request_id, "Request ID"), require_nonempty(platform, "Platform")),
            )
            return [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall() or []]

    def add_review(
        self,
        *,
        draft_id: int,
        action: str,
        reviewed_by: int | None,
        remarks: str | None = None,
    ) -> int:
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO adaptation_reviews (draft_id, action, remarks, reviewed_by)
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s)
                RETURNING id
                """,
                (
                    require_positive_id(draft_id, "Draft ID"),
                    require_nonempty(action, "Action"),
                    remarks,
                    reviewed_by,
                ),
            )
            return int(cur.fetchone()["id"])

    def save_final(
        self,
        *,
        draft_id: int,
        final_title: str,
        final_content: str,
        finalized_by: int | None,
        remarks: str | None = None,
    ) -> int:
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO final_adaptations (
                    draft_id, final_title, final_content, finalized_by, remarks
                )
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                RETURNING id
                """,
                (
                    require_positive_id(draft_id, "Draft ID"),
                    require_title(final_title),
                    require_nonempty(final_content, "Final content"),
                    finalized_by,
                    remarks,
                ),
            )
            return int(cur.fetchone()["id"])

    def get_final_for_draft(self, draft_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                "SELECT * FROM final_adaptations WHERE draft_id = %s",
                (require_positive_id(draft_id, "Draft ID"),),
            )
            row = cur.fetchone()
            return dict(row) if row else None

FILE: dao/article_dao.py
==============================================================================
"""Article DAO — table article."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Optional

from dao.base_dao import BaseDAO
from database.connection import db_cursor
from entities.content.article import Article
from utils.errors import NotFoundError, ValidationError
from utils.validators import (
    require_article_type,
    require_optional_positive_id,
    require_positive_id,
    require_read_time,
)


class ArticleDAO(BaseDAO):
    table = "article"

    def create(self, article: Article) -> int:
        content_id = require_positive_id(article.content_id, "Content ID")
        article_type = require_article_type(article.article_type or "feature")
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO article (content_id, story_pitch_id, article_type, estimated_read_time)
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s)
                RETURNING article_id
                """,
                (
                    content_id,
                    require_optional_positive_id(article.story_pitch_id, "Story pitch ID"),
                    article_type,
                    require_read_time(article.estimated_read_time),
                ),
            )
            return int(cur.fetchone()["article_id"])

    def read(self, article_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.get_by_id("article_id", require_positive_id(article_id, "Article ID"))

    def require(self, article_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row = self.read(article_id)
        if not row:
            raise NotFoundError(f"Article ID {article_id} was not found.")
        return row

    def update(self, article_id: int, **fields: Any) -> int:
        self.require(article_id)
        allowed = {"story_pitch_id", "article_type", "estimated_read_time"}
        cols = {k: v for k, v in fields.items() if k in allowed}
        if "article_type" in cols:
            cols["article_type"] = require_article_type(cols["article_type"])
        if "estimated_read_time" in cols:
            cols["estimated_read_time"] = require_read_time(cols["estimated_read_time"])
        if "story_pitch_id" in cols:
            cols["story_pitch_id"] = require_optional_positive_id(cols["story_pitch_id"], "Story pitch ID")
        if not cols:
            raise ValidationError("No valid fields to update.")
        sets = ", ".join(f"{k} = %s" for k in cols)
        return int(
            self.execute(
                f"UPDATE article SET {sets} WHERE article_id = %s",
                (*cols.values(), article_id),
            )
        )

    def delete(self, article_id: int) -> int:
        self.require(article_id)
        return self.delete_by_id("article_id", article_id)

FILE: dao/audience_insight_analyst_dao.py
==============================================================================
"""AudienceInsightAnalyst DAO — table audience_insight_analyst."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Optional

from dao.base_dao import BaseDAO
from database.connection import db_cursor
from entities.audience_insight_analyst import AudienceInsightAnalyst
from utils.errors import NotFoundError, ValidationError
from utils.validators import require_desk_status, require_optional_str, require_positive_id


class AudienceInsightAnalystDAO(BaseDAO):
    table = "audience_insight_analyst"

    def create(self, aia: AudienceInsightAnalyst) -> int:
        user_id = require_positive_id(aia.user_id, "User ID")
        status = require_desk_status(aia.status or "active")
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO audience_insight_analyst (user_id, focus_region, status)
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s)
                ON CONFLICT (user_id) DO UPDATE
                SET focus_region = EXCLUDED.focus_region, status = EXCLUDED.status
                RETURNING aia_id
                """,
                (
                    user_id,
                    require_optional_str(aia.focus_region, "Focus region", max_len=128),
                    status,
                ),
            )
            return int(cur.fetchone()["aia_id"])

    def ensure_for_user(self, user_id: int) -> int:
        return self.create(AudienceInsightAnalyst(user_id=user_id, focus_region="National"))

    def read(self, aia_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.get_by_id("aia_id", require_positive_id(aia_id, "AIA ID"))

    def require(self, aia_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row = self.read(aia_id)
        if not row:
            raise NotFoundError(f"AIA ID {aia_id} was not found.")
        return row

    def read_by_user_id(self, user_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        user_id = require_positive_id(user_id, "User ID")
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute("SELECT * FROM audience_insight_analyst WHERE user_id = %s", (user_id,))
            row = cur.fetchone()
            return dict(row) if row else None

    def update(self, aia_id: int, **fields: Any) -> int:
        self.require(aia_id)
        allowed = {"focus_region", "status"}
        cols = {k: v for k, v in fields.items() if k in allowed}
        if "status" in cols:
            cols["status"] = require_desk_status(cols["status"])
        if not cols:
            raise ValidationError("No valid fields to update.")
        sets = ", ".join(f"{k} = %s" for k in cols)
        return int(
            self.execute(
                f"UPDATE audience_insight_analyst SET {sets}, updated_at = NOW() WHERE aia_id = %s",
                (*cols.values(), aia_id),
            )
        )

    def delete(self, aia_id: int) -> int:
        self.require(aia_id)
        return self.delete_by_id("aia_id", aia_id)


==============================================================================
FILE: dao/base_dao.py
==============================================================================
"""Shared DAO helpers."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Optional, Sequence

from database.connection import db_cursor


class BaseDAO:
    table: str = ""

    def execute(self, sql: str, params: Optional[Sequence[Any]] = None, fetch: str = "none") -> Any:
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(sql, params)
            if fetch == "one":
                return cur.fetchone()
            if fetch == "all":
                return cur.fetchall()
            return cur.rowcount

    def get_by_id(self, pk_name: str, pk_value: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        row = self.execute(
            f"SELECT * FROM {self.table} WHERE {pk_name} = %s",
            (pk_value,),
            fetch="one",
        )
        return dict(row) if row else None

    def delete_by_id(self, pk_name: str, pk_value: int) -> int:
        return int(
            self.execute(
                f"DELETE FROM {self.table} WHERE {pk_name} = %s",
                (pk_value,),
            )
        )

    def list_all(self, limit: int = 500) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        rows = self.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {self.table} ORDER BY 1 DESC LIMIT %s", (limit,), fetch="all")
        return [dict(r) for r in rows or []]

"""Category DAO — table category."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Optional

from dao.base_dao import BaseDAO
from database.connection import db_cursor
from entities.content.category import Category
from utils.errors import NotFoundError, ValidationError
from utils.validators import require_category_name, require_optional_str, require_positive_id


class CategoryDAO(BaseDAO):
    table = "category"

    def create(self, category: Category) -> int:
        name = require_category_name(category.category_name)
        description = require_optional_str(category.description, "Description")
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO category (category_name, description, is_active)
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s)
                ON CONFLICT (category_name) DO UPDATE
                SET description = EXCLUDED.description, is_active = EXCLUDED.is_active
                RETURNING category_id
                """,
                (name, description, bool(category.is_active)),
            )
            return int(cur.fetchone()["category_id"])

    def read(self, category_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.get_by_id("category_id", require_positive_id(category_id, "Category ID"))

    def require(self, category_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row = self.read(category_id)
        if not row:
            raise NotFoundError(f"Category ID {category_id} was not found.")
        return row

    def update(self, category_id: int, **fields: Any) -> int:
        self.require(category_id)
        allowed = {"category_name", "description", "is_active"}
        cols = {k: v for k, v in fields.items() if k in allowed}
        if "category_name" in cols:
            cols["category_name"] = require_category_name(cols["category_name"])
        if "description" in cols:
            cols["description"] = require_optional_str(cols["description"], "Description")
        if not cols:
            raise ValidationError("No valid fields to update.")
        sets = ", ".join(f"{k} = %s" for k in cols)
        return int(
            self.execute(
                f"UPDATE category SET {sets}, updated_at = NOW() WHERE category_id = %s",
                (*cols.values(), category_id),
            )
        )

    def delete(self, category_id: int) -> int:
        self.require(category_id)
        return self.delete_by_id("category_id", category_id)


==============================================================================
FILE: dao/cleaning_report_dao.py
==============================================================================
"""DAO for cleaning_report — quality reports in DB."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from typing import Any, Optional

from dao.base_dao import BaseDAO
from database.connection import db_cursor
from utils.validators import require_nonempty, require_optional_positive_id, require_positive_id


class CleaningReportDAO(BaseDAO):
    table = "cleaning_report"

    def create(
        self,
        *,
        report_type: str,
        report: dict[str, Any],
        input_count: int,
        kept_count: int,
        rejected_count: int,
        source_files: str = "",
        created_by: int | None = None,
    ) -> int:
        report_type = require_nonempty(report_type, "Report type").lower()
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO cleaning_report (
                    report_type, source_files, input_count, kept_count, rejected_count,
                    report_json, created_by
                )
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s::jsonb, %s)
                RETURNING cleaning_report_id
                """,
                (
                    report_type,
                    source_files or None,
                    int(input_count),
                    int(kept_count),
                    int(rejected_count),
                    json.dumps(report),
                    require_optional_positive_id(created_by, "Created by"),
                ),
            )
            return int(cur.fetchone()["cleaning_report_id"])

    def read(self, cleaning_report_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.get_by_id(
            "cleaning_report_id",
            require_positive_id(cleaning_report_id, "Cleaning report ID"),
        )

    def list_recent(self, limit: int = 20, report_type: str | None = None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        if report_type:
            rows = self.execute(
                """
                SELECT cleaning_report_id, report_type, source_files, input_count,
                       kept_count, rejected_count, created_by, created_at
                FROM cleaning_report
                WHERE report_type = %s
                ORDER BY cleaning_report_id DESC
                LIMIT %s
                """,
                (report_type, limit),
                fetch="all",
            )
        else:
            rows = self.execute(
                """
                SELECT cleaning_report_id, report_type, source_files, input_count,
                       kept_count, rejected_count, created_by, created_at
                FROM cleaning_report
                ORDER BY cleaning_report_id DESC
                LIMIT %s
                """,
                (limit,),
                fetch="all",
            )
        return [dict(r) for r in rows or []]

FILE: dao/content_dao.py

"""Content DAO — table content."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Optional

from dao.base_dao import BaseDAO
from database.connection import db_cursor
from entities.content.content import Content
from utils.errors import NotFoundError, ValidationError
from utils.validators import (
    require_content_kind,
    require_content_status,
    require_optional_positive_id,
    require_optional_str,
    require_positive_id,
    require_title,
)


class ContentDAO(BaseDAO):
    table = "content"

    def create(self, content: Content) -> int:
        status = require_content_status(content.status or "draft")
        kind = require_content_kind(content.content_kind or "article")
        title = require_title(content.title)
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO content (
                    author_id, title, summary, raw_body, content_body,
                    content_kind, source_type, status, current_version
                )
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                RETURNING content_id
                """,
                (
                    require_optional_positive_id(content.author_id, "Author ID"),
                    title,
                    require_optional_str(content.summary, "Summary"),
                    require_optional_str(content.raw_body, "Raw body"),
                    require_optional_str(content.content_body, "Content body"),
                    kind,
                    require_optional_str(content.source_type, "Source type", max_len=128),
                    status,
                    int(content.current_version or 1),
                ),
            )
            return int(cur.fetchone()["content_id"])

    def read(self, content_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.get_by_id("content_id", content_id)

    def require(self, content_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row = self.read(content_id)
        if not row:
            raise NotFoundError(f"Content ID {content_id} was not found.")
        return row

    def update(self, content_id: int, **fields: Any) -> int:
        self.require(content_id)
        allowed = {
            "author_id",
            "title",
            "summary",
            "raw_body",
            "content_body",
            "content_kind",
            "source_type",
            "status",
            "current_version",
        }
        cols = {k: v for k, v in fields.items() if k in allowed}
        if "title" in cols:
            cols["title"] = require_title(cols["title"])
        if "status" in cols:
            cols["status"] = require_content_status(str(cols["status"]))
        if "content_kind" in cols:
            cols["content_kind"] = require_content_kind(str(cols["content_kind"]))
        if not cols:
            raise ValidationError("No valid fields to update.")
        sets = ", ".join(f"{k} = %s" for k in cols)
        return int(
            self.execute(
                f"UPDATE content SET {sets}, updated_at = NOW() WHERE content_id = %s",
                (*cols.values(), content_id),
            )
        )

    def update_status(self, content_id: int, status: str) -> int:
        return self.update(content_id, status=status)

    def list_by_status(self, status: str, limit: int = 100) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        status = require_content_status(status)
        rows = self.execute(
            """
            SELECT * FROM content
            WHERE status = %s
            ORDER BY content_id DESC
            LIMIT %s
            """,
            (status, max(1, min(int(limit), 500))),
            fetch="all",
        )
        return [dict(r) for r in rows or []]

    def delete(self, content_id: int) -> int:
        self.require(content_id)
        return self.delete_by_id("content_id", content_id)

"""Employee DAO — table employee."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Optional

from dao.base_dao import BaseDAO
from database.connection import db_cursor
from entities.employee import Employee
from utils.errors import NotFoundError, ValidationError
from utils.validators import (
    require_email,
    require_employment_status,
    require_nonempty,
    require_optional_str,
    require_positive_id,
)


class EmployeeDAO(BaseDAO):
    table = "employee"

    def create(self, employee: Employee) -> int:
        code = require_nonempty(employee.employee_code, "Employee code")
        first_name = require_nonempty(employee.first_name, "First name")
        last_name = require_nonempty(employee.last_name, "Last name")
        email = require_email(employee.email)
        status = require_employment_status(employee.employment_status or "active")
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO employee (
                    employee_code, first_name, last_name, email,
                    mobile_number, department, designation, location, employment_status, joining_date
                )
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                ON CONFLICT (employee_code) DO UPDATE
                SET first_name = EXCLUDED.first_name,
                    last_name = EXCLUDED.last_name,
                    email = EXCLUDED.email
                RETURNING employee_id
                """,
                (
                    code,
                    first_name,
                    last_name,
                    email,
                    require_optional_str(employee.mobile_number, "Mobile"),
                    require_optional_str(employee.department, "Department", max_len=128),
                    require_optional_str(employee.designation, "Designation", max_len=128),
                    require_optional_str(employee.location, "Location", max_len=128),
                    status,
                    employee.joining_date,
                ),
            )
            return int(cur.fetchone()["employee_id"])

    def read(self, employee_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.get_by_id("employee_id", require_positive_id(employee_id, "Employee ID"))

    def require(self, employee_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row = self.read(employee_id)
        if not row:
            raise NotFoundError(f"Employee ID {employee_id} was not found.")
        return row

    def update(self, employee_id: int, **fields: Any) -> int:
        self.require(employee_id)
        allowed = {
            "first_name",
            "last_name",
            "email",
            "mobile_number",
            "department",
            "designation",
            "location",
            "employment_status",
        }
        cols = {k: v for k, v in fields.items() if k in allowed}
        if "first_name" in cols:
            cols["first_name"] = require_nonempty(cols["first_name"], "First name")
        if "last_name" in cols:
            cols["last_name"] = require_nonempty(cols["last_name"], "Last name")
        if "email" in cols:
            cols["email"] = require_email(cols["email"])
        if "employment_status" in cols:
            cols["employment_status"] = require_employment_status(cols["employment_status"])
        if not cols:
            raise ValidationError("No valid fields to update.")
        sets = ", ".join(f"{k} = %s" for k in cols)
        return int(
            self.execute(
                f"UPDATE employee SET {sets}, updated_at = NOW() WHERE employee_id = %s",
                (*cols.values(), employee_id),
            )
        )

    def delete(self, employee_id: int) -> int:
        self.require(employee_id)
        return self.delete_by_id("employee_id", employee_id)

"""Evidence DAO — table evidence."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Optional

from dao.base_dao import BaseDAO
from database.connection import db_cursor
from entities.content.evidence import Evidence
from utils.errors import NotFoundError, ValidationError
from utils.validators import (
    require_nonempty,
    require_optional_positive_id,
    require_optional_str,
    require_positive_id,
    require_title,
)


class EvidenceDAO(BaseDAO):
    table = "evidence"

    def create(self, evidence: Evidence) -> int:
        title = require_title(evidence.title)
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO evidence (
                    content_id, title, evidence_type, source_url, file_path, ocr_text, uploaded_by
                )
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                RETURNING evidence_id
                """,
                (
                    require_optional_positive_id(evidence.content_id, "Content ID"),
                    title,
                    require_optional_str(evidence.evidence_type, "Evidence type", max_len=64) or "document",
                    require_optional_str(evidence.source_url, "Source URL"),
                    require_optional_str(evidence.file_path, "File path"),
                    require_optional_str(evidence.ocr_text, "OCR text"),
                    require_optional_positive_id(evidence.uploaded_by, "Uploaded by"),
                ),
            )
            return int(cur.fetchone()["evidence_id"])

    def read(self, evidence_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.get_by_id("evidence_id", require_positive_id(evidence_id, "Evidence ID"))

    def require(self, evidence_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row = self.read(evidence_id)
        if not row:
            raise NotFoundError(f"Evidence ID {evidence_id} was not found.")
        return row

    def update(self, evidence_id: int, **fields: Any) -> int:
        self.require(evidence_id)
        allowed = {"title", "evidence_type", "source_url", "file_path", "ocr_text", "uploaded_by"}
        cols = {k: v for k, v in fields.items() if k in allowed}
        if "title" in cols:
            cols["title"] = require_title(cols["title"])
        if "evidence_type" in cols:
            cols["evidence_type"] = require_nonempty(cols["evidence_type"], "Evidence type")
        if not cols:
            raise ValidationError("No valid fields to update.")
        sets = ", ".join(f"{k} = %s" for k in cols)
        return int(
            self.execute(
                f"UPDATE evidence SET {sets} WHERE evidence_id = %s",
                (*cols.values(), evidence_id),
            )
        )

    def update_ocr_text(self, evidence_id: int, ocr_text: str) -> int:
        return self.update(evidence_id, ocr_text=require_nonempty(ocr_text, "OCR text"))

    def delete(self, evidence_id: int) -> int:
        self.require(evidence_id)
        return self.delete_by_id("evidence_id", evidence_id)

"""DAO for ingest_file — CSV file registry."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Optional

from dao.base_dao import BaseDAO
from database.connection import db_cursor
from utils.validators import require_nonempty


class IngestFileDAO(BaseDAO):
    table = "ingest_file"

    def upsert(
        self,
        *,
        source_file: str,
        file_hash: str,
        row_count: int,
        usable_row_count: int,
        last_status: str = "ingested",
    ) -> int:
        source_file = require_nonempty(source_file, "Source file")
        file_hash = require_nonempty(file_hash, "File hash")
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO ingest_file (
                    source_file, file_hash, row_count, usable_row_count, last_status, last_ingested_at
                )
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, NOW())
                ON CONFLICT (source_file) DO UPDATE
                SET file_hash = EXCLUDED.file_hash,
                    row_count = EXCLUDED.row_count,
                    usable_row_count = EXCLUDED.usable_row_count,
                    last_status = EXCLUDED.last_status,
                    last_ingested_at = NOW()
                RETURNING ingest_file_id
                """,
                (source_file, file_hash, int(row_count), int(usable_row_count), last_status),
            )
            return int(cur.fetchone()["ingest_file_id"])

    def get_by_source(self, source_file: str) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        row = self.execute(
            "SELECT * FROM ingest_file WHERE source_file = %s",
            (source_file,),
            fetch="one",
        )
        return dict(row) if row else None

    def list_all(self, limit: int = 200) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        rows = self.execute(
            """
            SELECT * FROM ingest_file
            ORDER BY last_ingested_at DESC
            LIMIT %s
            """,
            (limit,),
            fetch="all",
        )
        return [dict(r) for r in rows or []]

"""Journalist DAO — table journalist."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Optional

from dao.base_dao import BaseDAO
from database.connection import db_cursor
from entities.journalist import Journalist
from utils.errors import NotFoundError, ValidationError
from utils.validators import (
    require_availability,
    require_desk_status,
    require_optional_str,
    require_positive_id,
)


class JournalistDAO(BaseDAO):
    table = "journalist"

    def create(self, journalist: Journalist) -> int:
        user_id = require_positive_id(journalist.user_id, "User ID")
        availability = require_availability(journalist.availability_status or "available")
        status = require_desk_status(journalist.status or "active")
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO journalist (
                    user_id, primary_specialization, secondary_specialization,
                    availability_status, status
                )
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                ON CONFLICT (user_id) DO UPDATE
                SET primary_specialization = EXCLUDED.primary_specialization,
                    secondary_specialization = EXCLUDED.secondary_specialization,
                    availability_status = EXCLUDED.availability_status,
                    status = EXCLUDED.status
                RETURNING journalist_id
                """,
                (
                    user_id,
                    require_optional_str(journalist.primary_specialization, "Primary specialization", max_len=128),
                    require_optional_str(journalist.secondary_specialization, "Secondary specialization", max_len=128),
                    availability,
                    status,
                ),
            )
            return int(cur.fetchone()["journalist_id"])

    def ensure_for_user(self, user_id: int) -> int:
        return self.create(
            Journalist(
                user_id=user_id,
                primary_specialization="General",
                availability_status="available",
            )
        )

    def read(self, journalist_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.get_by_id("journalist_id", require_positive_id(journalist_id, "Journalist ID"))

    def require(self, journalist_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row = self.read(journalist_id)
        if not row:
            raise NotFoundError(f"Journalist ID {journalist_id} was not found.")
        return row

    def read_by_user_id(self, user_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        user_id = require_positive_id(user_id, "User ID")
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute("SELECT * FROM journalist WHERE user_id = %s", (user_id,))
            row = cur.fetchone()
            return dict(row) if row else None

    def update(self, journalist_id: int, **fields: Any) -> int:
        self.require(journalist_id)
        allowed = {
            "primary_specialization",
            "secondary_specialization",
            "availability_status",
            "status",
        }
        cols = {k: v for k, v in fields.items() if k in allowed}
        if "availability_status" in cols:
            cols["availability_status"] = require_availability(cols["availability_status"])
        if "status" in cols:
            cols["status"] = require_desk_status(cols["status"])
        if not cols:
            raise ValidationError("No valid fields to update.")
        sets = ", ".join(f"{k} = %s" for k in cols)
        return int(
            self.execute(
                f"UPDATE journalist SET {sets}, updated_at = NOW() WHERE journalist_id = %s",
                (*cols.values(), journalist_id),
            )
        )

    def delete(self, journalist_id: int) -> int:
        self.require(journalist_id)
        return self.delete_by_id("journalist_id", journalist_id)

"""News DAO — table news."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Optional

from dao.base_dao import BaseDAO
from database.connection import db_cursor
from entities.content.news import News
from utils.errors import NotFoundError, ValidationError
from utils.validators import (
    require_nonempty,
    require_optional_str,
    require_positive_id,
    require_severity,
)


class NewsDAO(BaseDAO):
    table = "news"

    def create(self, news: News) -> int:
        content_id = require_positive_id(news.content_id, "Content ID")
        severity = require_severity(news.severity or "low")
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO news (
                    content_id, event_title, event_type, event_location,
                    event_date, breaking_news_flag, severity
                )
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                RETURNING news_id
                """,
                (
                    content_id,
                    require_optional_str(news.event_title, "Event title", max_len=512),
                    require_optional_str(news.event_type, "Event type", max_len=128) or "city",
                    require_optional_str(news.event_location, "Event location", max_len=255),
                    news.event_date,
                    bool(news.breaking_news_flag),
                    severity,
                ),
            )
            return int(cur.fetchone()["news_id"])

    def read(self, news_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.get_by_id("news_id", require_positive_id(news_id, "News ID"))

    def require(self, news_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row = self.read(news_id)
        if not row:
            raise NotFoundError(f"News ID {news_id} was not found.")
        return row

    def update(self, news_id: int, **fields: Any) -> int:
        self.require(news_id)
        allowed = {
            "event_title",
            "event_type",
            "event_location",
            "event_date",
            "breaking_news_flag",
            "severity",
        }
        cols = {k: v for k, v in fields.items() if k in allowed}
        if "severity" in cols:
            cols["severity"] = require_severity(cols["severity"])
        if "event_type" in cols:
            cols["event_type"] = require_nonempty(cols["event_type"], "Event type")
        if not cols:
            raise ValidationError("No valid fields to update.")
        sets = ", ".join(f"{k} = %s" for k in cols)
        return int(
            self.execute(
                f"UPDATE news SET {sets} WHERE news_id = %s",
                (*cols.values(), news_id),
            )
        )

    def delete(self, news_id: int) -> int:
        self.require(news_id)
        return self.delete_by_id("news_id", news_id)

"""NewsroomManager DAO — table newsroom_manager."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Optional

from dao.base_dao import BaseDAO
from database.connection import db_cursor
from entities.newsroom_manager import NewsroomManager
from utils.errors import NotFoundError, ValidationError
from utils.validators import require_desk_status, require_optional_str, require_positive_id


class NewsroomManagerDAO(BaseDAO):
    table = "newsroom_manager"

    def create(self, manager: NewsroomManager) -> int:
        user_id = require_positive_id(manager.user_id, "User ID")

            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO newsroom_manager (user_id, managed_region, status)
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s)
                ON CONFLICT (user_id) DO UPDATE
                SET managed_region = EXCLUDED.managed_region, status = EXCLUDED.status

        )

    def require(self, newsroom_manager_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row = self.read(newsroom_manager_id)
        if not row:
            raise NotFoundError(f"Newsroom manager ID {newsroom_manager_id} was not found.")
        return row

    def read_by_user_id(self, user_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        user_id = require_positive_id(user_id, "User ID")
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute("SELECT * FROM newsroom_manager WHERE user_id = %s", (user_id,))
            row = cur.fetchone()
            return dict(row) if row else None

    def update(self, newsroom_manager_id: int, **fields: Any) -> int:
        self.require(newsroom_manager_id)
        allowed = {"managed_region", "status"}
        cols = {k: v for k, v in fields.items() if k in allowed}
        if "status" in cols:
            cols["status"] = require_desk_status(cols["status"])
        if not cols:
            raise ValidationError("No valid fields to update.")
        sets = ", ".join(f"{k} = %s" for k in cols)
        return int(
            self.execute(
                f"UPDATE newsroom_manager SET {sets}, updated_at = NOW() WHERE newsroom_manager_id = %s",
                (*cols.values(), newsroom_manager_id),
            )
        )

        return self.delete_by_id("newsroom_manager_id", newsroom_manager_id)

"""Publisher publication queue — table publication."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Optional

from dao.base_dao import BaseDAO
from database.connection import db_cursor
from utils.errors import NotFoundError, ValidationError
from utils.validators import require_optional_str, require_positive_id

ALLOWED_PUBLICATION_STATUSES = ("ready", "approved", "scheduled", "published")


class PublicationDAO(BaseDAO):
    table = "publication"

    def ensure_for_content(self, content_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        content_id = require_positive_id(content_id, "Content ID")
        existing = self.read_by_content_id(content_id)
        if existing:
            return existing
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO publication (content_id, status)
                VALUES (%s, 'ready')
                ON CONFLICT (content_id) DO UPDATE
                SET updated_at = NOW()
                RETURNING *
                """,
                (content_id,),
            )
            return dict(cur.fetchone())

    def read(self, publication_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.get_by_id(
            "publication_id",
            require_positive_id(publication_id, "Publication ID"),
        )

    def require(self, publication_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row = self.read(publication_id)
        if not row:
            raise NotFoundError(f"Publication ID {publication_id} was not found.")
        return row

    def read_by_content_id(self, content_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        content_id = require_positive_id(content_id, "Content ID")
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute("SELECT * FROM publication WHERE content_id = %s", (content_id,))
            row = cur.fetchone()
            return dict(row) if row else None

    def list_ready_articles(self, limit: int = 100) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                SELECT
                    c.content_id,
                    c.title,
                    c.summary,
                    c.content_body,
                    c.content_kind,
                    c.status AS content_status,
                    p.publication_id,
                    p.template_code,
                    p.status AS publication_status,
                    p.scheduled_at,
                    p.published_at
                FROM content c
                LEFT JOIN publication p ON p.content_id = c.content_id
                WHERE c.status = 'approved'
                  AND (p.publication_id IS NULL OR p.status IN ('ready', 'approved', 'scheduled'))
                ORDER BY c.content_id DESC
                LIMIT %s
                """,
                (max(1, min(int(limit), 500)),),
            )
            return [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall() or []]

    def update_fields(self, publication_id: int, **fields: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
        self.require(publication_id)
        allowed = {"template_code", "status", "scheduled_at", "published_at"}
        cols = {k: v for k, v in fields.items() if k in allowed}
        if "status" in cols:
            status = str(cols["status"]).strip().lower()
            if status not in ALLOWED_PUBLICATION_STATUSES:
                raise ValidationError(
                    f"Invalid publication status. Allowed: {', '.join(ALLOWED_PUBLICATION_STATUSES)}"
                )
            cols["status"] = status
        if "template_code" in cols and cols["template_code"] is not None:
            cols["template_code"] = require_optional_str(
                cols["template_code"], "Template code", max_len=64
            )
        if not cols:
            raise ValidationError("No valid fields to update.")
        sets = ", ".join(f"{k} = %s" for k in cols)
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                f"""
                UPDATE publication
                SET {sets}, updated_at = NOW()
                WHERE publication_id = %s
                RETURNING *
                """,
                (*cols.values(), publication_id),
            )
            return dict(cur.fetchone())

    def analytics(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                SELECT status, COUNT(*) AS count
                FROM publication
                GROUP BY status
                """
            )
            by_status = {str(r["status"]): int(r["count"]) for r in cur.fetchall() or []}
            cur.execute(
                """
                SELECT COUNT(*) AS count
                FROM content
                WHERE status = 'approved'
                """
            )
            approved_content = int(cur.fetchone()["count"])
        return {
            "approved_content": approved_content,
            "publications_by_status": by_status,
            "published": by_status.get("published", 0),
            "scheduled": by_status.get("scheduled", 0),
            "ready": by_status.get("ready", 0),
            "approved": by_status.get("approved", 0),
        }

"""Publisher DAO — table publisher."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Optional

from dao.base_dao import BaseDAO
from database.connection import db_cursor
from entities.publisher import Publisher
from utils.errors import NotFoundError, ValidationError
from utils.validators import require_desk_status, require_optional_str, require_positive_id


class PublisherDAO(BaseDAO):
    table = "publisher"

    def create(self, publisher: Publisher) -> int:
        user_id = require_positive_id(publisher.user_id, "User ID")
        status = require_desk_status(publisher.status or "active")
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO publisher (user_id, publishing_group, status)
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s)
                ON CONFLICT (user_id) DO UPDATE
                SET publishing_group = EXCLUDED.publishing_group, status = EXCLUDED.status
                RETURNING publisher_id
                """,
                (
                    user_id,
                    require_optional_str(publisher.publishing_group, "Publishing group", max_len=128),
                    status,
                ),
            )
            return int(cur.fetchone()["publisher_id"])

    def ensure_for_user(self, user_id: int) -> int:
        return self.create(Publisher(user_id=user_id, publishing_group="Primary"))

    def read(self, publisher_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.get_by_id("publisher_id", require_positive_id(publisher_id, "Publisher ID"))

    def require(self, publisher_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row = self.read(publisher_id)
        if not row:
            raise NotFoundError(f"Publisher ID {publisher_id} was not found.")
        return row

    def read_by_user_id(self, user_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        user_id = require_positive_id(user_id, "User ID")
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute("SELECT * FROM publisher WHERE user_id = %s", (user_id,))
            row = cur.fetchone()
            return dict(row) if row else None

    def update(self, publisher_id: int, **fields: Any) -> int:
        self.require(publisher_id)
        allowed = {"publishing_group", "status"}
        cols = {k: v for k, v in fields.items() if k in allowed}
        if "status" in cols:
            cols["status"] = require_desk_status(cols["status"])
        if not cols:
            raise ValidationError("No valid fields to update.")
        sets = ", ".join(f"{k} = %s" for k in cols)
        return int(
            self.execute(
                f"UPDATE publisher SET {sets}, updated_at = NOW() WHERE publisher_id = %s",
                (*cols.values(), publisher_id),
            )
        )

    def delete(self, publisher_id: int) -> int:
        self.require(publisher_id)
        return self.delete_by_id("publisher_id", publisher_id)
