==============================================================================
FILE: ml/common/entities.py
==============================================================================
"""Named-entity helpers.

Uses spaCy as a supporting library with a **blank** English pipeline + EntityRuler
(no trained model download, no model training). Falls back to regex if spaCy
is not installed.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from functools import lru_cache
from typing import Any

_PERSON = re.compile(r"\b([A-Z][a-z]+(?:\s+[A-Z][a-z]+)+)\b")
_DATE = re.compile(
    r"\b(\d{1,2}[/-]\d{1,2}[/-]\d{2,4}|"
    r"(?:Jan|Feb|Mar|Apr|May|Jun|Jul|Aug|Sep|Oct|Nov|Dec)[a-z]*\s+\d{1,2},?\s+\d{4}|"
    r"\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})\b",
    re.IGNORECASE,
)
_ORG = re.compile(
    r"\b([A-Z][A-Za-z]+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z]+)*\s+"
    r"(?:Inc|Corp|Company|Ltd|LLC|University|Ministry|Commission|Agency))\b"
)
_LOCATION = re.compile(
    r"\b(?:in|at|from|near)\s+([A-Z][a-z]+(?:\s+[A-Z][a-z]+)?)\b"
)

_EMPTY: dict[str, list[str]] = {
    "people": [],
    "organizations": [],
    "locations": [],
    "dates": [],
    "other": [],
}


def _regex_entities(text: str) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    return {
        "people": list(dict.fromkeys(_PERSON.findall(text))),
        "organizations": list(dict.fromkeys(_ORG.findall(text))),
        "locations": list(dict.fromkeys(_LOCATION.findall(text))),
        "dates": list(dict.fromkeys(_DATE.findall(text))),
        "other": [],
    }


@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
def _spacy_ruler():
    """Blank spaCy English + pattern ruler. Never downloads a model."""
    import spacy
    from spacy.pipeline import EntityRuler

    nlp = spacy.blank("en")
    ruler: EntityRuler = nlp.add_pipe("entity_ruler")
    patterns: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
        {"label": "DATE", "pattern": [{"SHAPE": "dd/dd/dddd"}]},
        {"label": "DATE", "pattern": [{"SHAPE": "dddd-dd-dd"}]},
        {
            "label": "ORG",
            "pattern": [
                {"IS_TITLE": True, "OP": "+"},
                {"LOWER": {"IN": ["inc", "corp", "company", "ltd", "llc", "university", "ministry", "commission", "agency"]}},
            ],
        },
        {
            "label": "PERSON",
            "pattern": [{"IS_TITLE": True}, {"IS_TITLE": True}],
        },
        {
            "label": "GPE",
            "pattern": [
                {"LOWER": {"IN": ["in", "at", "from", "near"]}},
                {"IS_TITLE": True},
            ],
        },
    ]
    ruler.add_patterns(patterns)
    return nlp


def _spacy_entities(text: str) -> dict[str, list[str]] | None:
    try:
        nlp = _spacy_ruler()
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — spaCy missing or pipe setup failed
        return None

    doc = nlp(text)
    result: dict[str, list[str]] = {
        "people": [],
        "organizations": [],
        "locations": [],
        "dates": [],
        "other": [],
    }
    seen: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()
    for ent in doc.ents:
        value = ent.text.strip()
        # EntityRuler GPE pattern includes the preposition — keep the place name only
        if ent.label_ == "GPE" and " " in value:
            value = value.split(None, 1)[-1]
        key = (ent.label_, value.lower())
        if not value or key in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        if ent.label_ == "PERSON":
            result["people"].append(value)
        elif ent.label_ == "ORG":
            result["organizations"].append(value)
        elif ent.label_ in {"GPE", "LOC"}:
            result["locations"].append(value)
        elif ent.label_ == "DATE":
            result["dates"].append(value)
        else:
            result["other"].append(value)

    # Merge regex hits so month-name dates and similar still appear
    regex = _regex_entities(text)
    for field in ("people", "organizations", "locations", "dates"):
        merged = list(dict.fromkeys([*result[field], *regex[field]]))
        result[field] = merged
    return result


def extract_entities(text: str) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    """Extract people, orgs, locations, dates. Empty text → empty lists."""
    raw = "" if text is None else str(text).strip()
    if not raw:
        return dict(_EMPTY)

    spacy_result = _spacy_entities(raw)
    if spacy_result is not None:
        return spacy_result
    return _regex_entities(raw)


==============================================================================
FILE: ml/common/similarity.py
==============================================================================
"""One shared place for TF-style ranking and near-duplicate checks.

Uses plain term counts + cosine (numpy). No separate copy in each service.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections import Counter
from typing import Any, Mapping, Sequence

import numpy as np

from ml.common.text import normalize, word_ngrams


def _tokens(text: str) -> list[str]:
    return normalize(text).split()


def _tf_vector(tokens: Sequence[str], vocab: dict[str, int]) -> np.ndarray:
    vec = np.zeros(len(vocab), dtype=float)
    if not tokens:
        return vec
    counts = Counter(tokens)
    for term, count in counts.items():
        idx = vocab.get(term)
        if idx is not None:
            vec[idx] = float(count)
    return vec


def _cosine(a: np.ndarray, b: np.ndarray) -> float:
    denom = float(np.linalg.norm(a) * np.linalg.norm(b))
    if denom == 0.0:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.dot(a, b) / denom)


def _doc_text(doc: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    return str(doc.get("text") or doc.get("content_body") or doc.get("title") or "")


def rank(
    query: str,
    documents: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    *,
    limit: int = 10,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Rank documents by cosine similarity to query.

    Each document may include id, title, text (or content_body).
    Empty corpus → [].
    """
    docs = list(documents or [])
    if not docs or not normalize(query):
        return []

    query_tokens = _tokens(query)
    doc_tokens = [_tokens(_doc_text(d)) for d in docs]
    vocab_terms = sorted({t for tokens in [query_tokens, *doc_tokens] for t in tokens})
    if not vocab_terms:
        return []

    vocab = {term: i for i, term in enumerate(vocab_terms)}
    q_vec = _tf_vector(query_tokens, vocab)

    scored: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for doc, tokens in zip(docs, doc_tokens):
        score = _cosine(q_vec, _tf_vector(tokens, vocab))
        if score <= 0:
            continue
        scored.append(
            {
                "id": doc.get("id") or doc.get("content_id") or doc.get("evidence_id"),
                "title": doc.get("title") or "",
                "score": round(score, 4),
            }
        )

    scored.sort(key=lambda row: row["score"], reverse=True)
    return scored[: max(0, int(limit))]


def _minhash_signature(text: str, *, num_hashes: int = 32) -> set[int]:
    """Simple hash-shingle signature for near-duplicate detection."""
    shingles = word_ngrams(text, n=3) or _tokens(text)
    if not shingles:
        return set()
    sig: set[int] = set()
    for i in range(num_hashes):
        best = min(hash((i, s)) & 0xFFFFFFFF for s in shingles)
        sig.add(best)
    return sig


def _jaccard(a: set[int], b: set[int]) -> float:
    if not a and not b:
        return 0.0
    union = a | b
    if not union:
        return 0.0
    return len(a & b) / len(union)


def similar_pairs(
    documents: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    *,
    threshold: float = 0.5,
    limit: int = 20,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Find near-duplicate pairs using MinHash-style Jaccard on shingles.

    Empty / single-doc corpus → [].
    """
    docs = list(documents or [])
    if len(docs) < 2:
        return []

    sigs = [_minhash_signature(_doc_text(d)) for d in docs]
    pairs: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for i in range(len(docs)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(docs)):
            score = _jaccard(sigs[i], sigs[j])
            if score < threshold:
                continue
            pairs.append(
                {
                    "left_id": docs[i].get("id") or docs[i].get("content_id"),
                    "right_id": docs[j].get("id") or docs[j].get("content_id"),
                    "left_title": docs[i].get("title") or "",
                    "right_title": docs[j].get("title") or "",
                    "score": round(score, 4),
                }
            )

    pairs.sort(key=lambda row: row["score"], reverse=True)
    return pairs[: max(0, int(limit))]


==============================================================================
FILE: ml/common/text.py
==============================================================================
"""Simple text cleanup and n-grams."""

from __future__ import annotations

import re

_WS = re.compile(r"\s+")
_NON_WORD = re.compile(r"[^\w\s]", re.UNICODE)


def normalize(text: str) -> str:
    """Lowercase, strip punctuation-ish noise, collapse spaces."""
    raw = "" if text is None else str(text)
    cleaned = _NON_WORD.sub(" ", raw.lower())
    return _WS.sub(" ", cleaned).strip()


def word_ngrams(text: str, n: int = 2) -> list[str]:
    """Return word n-grams from normalized text."""
    tokens = normalize(text).split()
    if n < 1 or len(tokens) < n:
        return tokens[:]
    return [" ".join(tokens[i : i + n]) for i in range(len(tokens) - n + 1)]

==============================================================================
FILE: ml/editor/events.py
==============================================================================
"""Event grouping — cluster related content for Editor / Manager desks.

Uses TF cosine on title+body plus title-token Jaccard. Not a joblib model.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections import defaultdict
from typing import Any, Mapping, Sequence

from ml.common.similarity import _cosine, _doc_text, _tf_vector, _tokens
from ml.common.text import normalize


def _title_jaccard(a: str, b: str) -> float:
    ta, tb = set(_tokens(a)), set(_tokens(b))
    if not ta and not tb:
        return 0.0
    union = ta | tb
    if not union:
        return 0.0
    return len(ta & tb) / len(union)


def _pair_similarity(a: Mapping[str, Any], b: Mapping[str, Any]) -> float:
    title_a = str(a.get("title") or "")
    title_b = str(b.get("title") or "")
    text_a = f"{title_a}. {_doc_text(a)}"
    text_b = f"{title_b}. {_doc_text(b)}"
    ta, tb = _tokens(text_a), _tokens(text_b)
    vocab_terms = sorted(set(ta) | set(tb))
    body_score = 0.0
    if vocab_terms:
        vocab = {term: i for i, term in enumerate(vocab_terms)}
        body_score = _cosine(_tf_vector(ta, vocab), _tf_vector(tb, vocab))
    title_score = _title_jaccard(title_a, title_b)
    return max(body_score, title_score * 0.85 + body_score * 0.15)


def group_related_content(
    documents: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    *,
    min_similarity: float = 0.42,
    neighbor_window: int = 50,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Union-find cluster of related stories. Returns groups with 2+ members."""
    docs = [d for d in documents if normalize(str(d.get("title") or _doc_text(d)))]
    if len(docs) < 2:
        return []

    ids = [int(d.get("content_id") or d.get("id") or i) for i, d in enumerate(docs)]
    parent = {cid: cid for cid in ids}

    def find(x: int) -> int:
        while parent[x] != x:
            parent[x] = parent[parent[x]]
            x = parent[x]
        return x

    def union(a: int, b: int) -> None:
        ra, rb = find(a), find(b)
        if ra != rb:
            parent[rb] = ra

    by_id = {cid: doc for cid, doc in zip(ids, docs)}
    for i, left in enumerate(docs):
        left_id = ids[i]
        for j in range(i + 1, min(len(docs), i + 1 + neighbor_window)):
            right = docs[j]
            right_id = ids[j]
            score = _pair_similarity(left, right)
            if score >= min_similarity:
                union(left_id, right_id)

    buckets: dict[int, list[dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(list)
    for cid in ids:
        buckets[find(cid)].append(by_id[cid])

    groups: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for root, members in buckets.items():
        if len(members) < 2:
            continue
        titles = [str(m.get("title") or "") for m in members]
        lead = titles[0] if titles else f"Event {root}"
        groups.append(
            {
                "event_key": f"eg-{root}",
                "group_title": lead[:180],
                "member_count": len(members),
                "members": [
                    {
                        "content_id": m.get("content_id") or m.get("id"),
                        "title": m.get("title"),
                        "status": m.get("status"),
                        "content_kind": m.get("content_kind"),
                    }
                    for m in members
                ],
                "method": "tf_cosine_plus_title_jaccard",
                "min_similarity": min_similarity,
            }
        )
    groups.sort(key=lambda g: g["member_count"], reverse=True)
    return groups


==============================================================================
FILE: ml/editor/queue_urgency.py
==============================================================================
"""Editor queue urgency model.

Tells the editor how urgent a story is for the review queue:
  urgent > high > medium > low

This is NOT the same as AIA "pitch importance" (that one is a simple formula).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from functools import lru_cache
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix, hstack

from utils.logger import get_logger

log = get_logger("ml.editor.queue_urgency")

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
MODEL_PATHS = (
    ROOT / "artifacts" / "models" / "editor_queue_urgency.joblib",
    ROOT / "ML Project" / "editor_queue_urgency" / "model" / "editor_queue_urgency.joblib",
)

# Model training labels → simple desk levels used in the app
LABEL_TO_URGENCY = {
    "very low": "low",
    "low": "low",
    "medium": "medium",
    "high": "high",
    "very high": "urgent",
}


@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
def _load_model() -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    path = next((p for p in MODEL_PATHS if p.exists()), None)
    if path is None:
        log.warning("editor_queue_urgency.joblib not found")
        return None
    try:
        import joblib

        bundle = joblib.load(path)
        if not {"model", "tfidf", "labels", "urg_regex"}.issubset(bundle):
            log.warning("Urgency model file is missing required keys: %s", path)
            return None
        log.info("Loaded editor queue urgency model from %s", path)
        return bundle
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        log.warning("Could not load urgency model: %s", exc)
        return None


def model_available() -> bool:
    return _load_model() is not None


def clear_cache() -> None:
    _load_model.cache_clear()


def _extra_features(texts: list[str], urg_regex: str) -> np.ndarray:
    """Simple extra signals: length, urgent words, digits, ALLCAPS runs."""
    rows = []
    urg = re.compile(urg_regex, re.I)
    digit = re.compile(r"\d")
    caps = re.compile(r"[A-Z]{4,}")
    for text in texts:
        raw = text or ""
        rows.append(
            [
                len(raw) / 500.0,
                len(urg.findall(raw)),
                1 if digit.search(raw) else 0,
                len(caps.findall(raw)),
            ]
        )
    return np.asarray(rows, dtype=float)


def predict_editor_queue_urgency(headline: str, body: str = "") -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Return how urgent this story is for the editor queue."""
    bundle = _load_model()
    text = f"{headline or ''}. {body or ''}".strip()

    if bundle is None:
        return {
            "urgency": "medium",
            "model_label": None,
            "confidence": None,
            "probabilities": {},
            "source": "fallback",
        }

    labels: list[str] = list(bundle["labels"])
    features = hstack(
        [
            bundle["tfidf"].transform([text]),
            csr_matrix(_extra_features([text], str(bundle["urg_regex"]))),
        ]
    ).tocsr()

    probs = bundle["model"].predict_proba(features)[0]
    best = int(np.argmax(probs))
    model_label = labels[best]

    return {
        "urgency": LABEL_TO_URGENCY.get(model_label, "medium"),
        "model_label": model_label,
        "confidence": round(float(probs[best]), 4),
        "probabilities": {label: round(float(p), 4) for label, p in zip(labels, probs)},
        "source": "model",
    }

# `ml/` — scoring helpers

```
ml/common/       shared text + similarity + NER
ml/aia/          engagement/interest formulas + news category
ml/editor/       editor queue urgency + event groups
ml/adaptation/   CAE platform adaptations
```

| Package | Main functions | Meaning |
|---------|----------------|---------|
| `ml.editor` | `predict_editor_queue_urgency` | How urgent for editor queue |
| `ml.aia` | `predict_category`, `predict_engagement`, … | Topic + audience scores |
| `ml.common` | `rank`, `similar_pairs`, … | Shared text helpers |

Full naming notes: `docs/NAMING.md`

==============================================================================
FILE: modules/admin/admin_module.py
==============================================================================
"""Admin / pipeline feature module."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Optional

from cleaners.orchestrator import CleaningOrchestrator
from config.constants import CAP_CLEAN, CAP_INGEST, CAP_PERSIST, CAP_PIPELINE
from config.runtime import pipeline_file_by_file
from services.access import require_capability_session
from services.ingestion_service import IngestionService
from services.persistence_service import PersistenceService
from services.report_service import ReportService
from utils.logger import get_logger
from utils.progress import progress

log = get_logger("admin")


class AdminModule:
    def __init__(
        self,
        ingestion: IngestionService | None = None,
        cleaning: CleaningOrchestrator | None = None,
        persistence: PersistenceService | None = None,
        reports: ReportService | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.ingestion = ingestion or IngestionService()
        self.cleaning = cleaning or CleaningOrchestrator()
        self.persistence = persistence or PersistenceService()
        self.reports = reports or ReportService()

    def list_csv_files(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = require_capability_session(CAP_INGEST)
        progress("Scanning data/raw for CSV files...")
        files = self.ingestion.list_csv_files()
        new_files = self.ingestion.list_new_or_changed_files()
        progress(
            f"Found {len(files)} CSV file(s); {len(new_files)} new or changed."
        )
        return {
            "actor": user["username"],
            "role": user.get("role_name") or user["role_code"],
            "all_files": files,
            "new_or_changed": new_files,
            "registered": self.ingestion.registered_files(),
        }

    def ingest(self, *, only_new: bool = False, store_raw: bool = True) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = require_capability_session(CAP_INGEST)
        mode = "new/changed only" if only_new else "all CSV files"
        progress(f"Ingest started ({mode})...")
        payload = self.ingestion.ingest_all_content(only_new=only_new)
        pitches = self.ingestion.ingest_pitches() if not only_new else []
        raw_ids: list[int] = []
        if store_raw and payload["records"]:
            progress(f"Storing {len(payload['records'])} raw row(s) in database...")
            raw_ids = self.persistence.persist_raw_records(
                payload["records"], cleaning_status="pending"
            )
            progress(f"Raw ingest stored: {len(raw_ids)} row(s).")
        progress(
            f"Ingest finished: {len(payload['records'])} content row(s), "
            f"{len(pitches)} pitch row(s), {len(payload.get('source_files') or [])} file(s)."
        )
        return {
            "actor": user["username"],
            "role": user.get("role_name") or user["role_code"],
            "content_rows": len(payload["records"]),
            "pitch_rows": len(pitches),
            "source_files": payload.get("source_files") or [],
            "collections": payload["collections"],
            "raw_ingest_ids": raw_ids,
            "message": payload.get("message"),
            "records": payload["records"],
            "pitches": pitches,
        }

    def ingest_file(self, source_file: str, *, store_raw: bool = True) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = require_capability_session(CAP_INGEST)
        progress(f"Ingesting file: {source_file}")
        payload = self.ingestion.ingest_one(source_file)
        raw_ids: list[int] = []
        if store_raw and payload["records"]:
            progress(f"Storing {len(payload['records'])} raw row(s) in database...")
            raw_ids = self.persistence.persist_raw_records(
                payload["records"], cleaning_status="pending"
            )
            progress(f"Raw ingest stored: {len(raw_ids)} row(s).")
        progress(
            f"File ingest finished: {source_file} → "
            f"{payload.get('usable_row_count', len(payload['records']))} usable row(s)."
        )
        return {
            "actor": user["username"],
            "role": user.get("role_name") or user["role_code"],
            "content_rows": len(payload["records"]),
            "pitch_rows": 0,
            "source_files": payload["source_files"],
            "collections": payload["collections"],
            "raw_ingest_ids": raw_ids,
            "records": payload["records"],
            "pitches": [],
        }

    def clean(
        self,
        records: Optional[list[dict[str, Any]]] = None,
        pitches: Optional[list[dict[str, Any]]] = None,
        *,
        source_files: list[str] | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = require_capability_session(CAP_CLEAN)
        if records is None:
            progress("Loading CSV data for cleaning...")
            loaded = self.ingestion.ingest_all_content(only_new=False)
            records = loaded["records"]
            source_files = source_files or loaded.get("source_files")
        if pitches is None:
            pitches = self.ingestion.ingest_pitches()

        progress(f"Cleaning {len(records)} content row(s)...")
        content_result = self.cleaning.clean_content(records)
        progress(
            f"Content clean done: kept={content_result['stats'].get('kept')} "
            f"rejected={content_result['stats'].get('rejected')}"
        )
        progress(f"Cleaning {len(pitches)} pitch row(s)...")
        pitch_result = self.cleaning.clean_pitches(pitches)
        progress(
            f"Pitch clean done: kept={pitch_result['stats'].get('kept')} "
            f"rejected={pitch_result['stats'].get('rejected')}"
        )
        progress("Saving cleaning report to database...")
        report_id = self.reports.save_cleaning_report(
            report_type="content",
            result=content_result,
            source_files=source_files or [],
            created_by=user.get("user_id"),
        )
        pitch_report_id = None
        if pitches:
            pitch_report_id = self.reports.save_cleaning_report(
                report_type="pitch",
                result=pitch_result,
                source_files=source_files or ["pitches.csv"],
                created_by=user.get("user_id"),
            )
        progress(f"Cleaning report saved (id={report_id}).")
        return {
            "actor": user["username"],
            "role": user.get("role_name") or user["role_code"],
            "content_stats": content_result["stats"],
            "pitch_stats": pitch_result["stats"],
            "content_report": content_result.get("report_path"),
            "cleaned_csv": content_result.get("cleaned_csv"),
            "cleaning_report_id": report_id,
            "pitch_report_id": pitch_report_id,
            "content": content_result,
            "pitches": pitch_result,
        }

    def ingest_and_clean(self, *, only_new: bool = False, store_raw: bool = True) -> dict[str, Any]:
        require_capability_session(CAP_CLEAN)
        loaded = self.ingest(only_new=only_new, store_raw=store_raw)
        cleaned = self.clean(
            records=loaded["records"],
            pitches=loaded["pitches"],
            source_files=loaded.get("source_files"),
        )
        return {
            "actor": cleaned["actor"],
            "role": cleaned["role"],
            "ingest": {
                "content_rows": loaded["content_rows"],
                "pitch_rows": loaded["pitch_rows"],
                "source_files": loaded["source_files"],
                "collections": loaded["collections"],
                "raw_stored": len(loaded.get("raw_ingest_ids") or []),
                "message": loaded.get("message"),
            },
            "content_stats": cleaned["content_stats"],
            "pitch_stats": cleaned["pitch_stats"],
            "content_report": cleaned["content_report"],
            "cleaned_csv": cleaned["cleaned_csv"],
            "cleaning_report_id": cleaned["cleaning_report_id"],
            "content": cleaned["content"],
            "pitches": cleaned["pitches"],
        }

    def pipeline_file(self, source_file: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        require_capability_session(CAP_PIPELINE)
        progress(f"Pipeline for file: {source_file}")
        loaded = self.ingest_file(source_file, store_raw=True)
        cleaned = self.clean(
            records=loaded["records"],
            pitches=[],
            source_files=loaded["source_files"],
        )
        persisted = self.persist(cleaned)
        progress(
            f"Pipeline finished for {source_file}: "
            f"persisted={persisted['persisted_content']} "
            f"rejected_raw={persisted['rejected_raw_stored']}"
        )
        return {
            "actor": persisted["actor"],
            "role": persisted["role"],
            "source_files": loaded["source_files"],
            "ingest": {
                "content_rows": loaded["content_rows"],
                "source_files": loaded["source_files"],
                "collections": loaded["collections"],
            },
            "content_stats": cleaned["content_stats"],
            "cleaning_report_id": cleaned["cleaning_report_id"],
            "persisted_content": persisted["persisted_content"],
            "rejected_raw_stored": persisted["rejected_raw_stored"],
        }

    def persist(self, cleaned: Optional[dict[str, Any]] = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = require_capability_session(CAP_PERSIST)
        if cleaned is None:
            cleaned = self.ingest_and_clean(store_raw=True)
        kept = cleaned["content"]["kept"]
        rejected = cleaned["content"]["rejected"]
        pitches_kept = cleaned["pitches"]["kept"]
        progress(f"Persisting {len(kept)} cleaned content row(s)...")
        content_ids = self.persistence.persist_content(kept)
        progress(f"Persisting {len(rejected)} rejected row(s) to raw_ingest...")
        rejected_ids = self.persistence.persist_rejected(rejected)
        progress(f"Persisting {len(pitches_kept)} pitch row(s)...")
        pitch_ids = self.persistence.persist_pitches(pitches_kept)
        progress(
            f"Persist finished: content={len(content_ids)} "
            f"pitches={len(pitch_ids)} rejected_raw={len(rejected_ids)}"
        )
        return {
            "actor": user["username"],
            "role": user.get("role_name") or user["role_code"],
            "content_ids": content_ids,
            "pitch_ids": pitch_ids,
            "persisted_content": len(content_ids),
            "persisted_pitches": len(pitch_ids),
            "rejected_raw_stored": len(rejected_ids),
            "cleaning_report_id": cleaned.get("cleaning_report_id"),
        }

    def run_pipeline(self, *, only_new: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Full ETL. By default processes one CSV at a time with console progress."""
        require_capability_session(CAP_PIPELINE)
        if pipeline_file_by_file():
            files = (
                self.ingestion.list_new_or_changed_files()
                if only_new
                else self.ingestion.list_csv_files()
            )
            if only_new and not files:
                user = require_capability_session(CAP_PIPELINE)
                progress("No new or changed CSV files to process.")
                return {
                    "actor": user["username"],
                    "role": user.get("role_name") or user["role_code"],
                    "ingest": {
                        "content_rows": 0,
                        "pitch_rows": 0,
                        "source_files": [],
                        "message": "No new or changed CSV files to ingest.",
                    },
                    "content_stats": {"input": 0, "kept": 0, "rejected": 0},
                    "pitch_stats": {"input": 0, "kept": 0, "rejected": 0},
                    "quality_report": "",
                    "cleaning_report_id": None,
                    "persisted_content": 0,
                    "persisted_pitches": 0,
                    "rejected_raw_stored": 0,
                    "persisted_content_ids": [],
                    "persisted_pitch_ids": [],
                    "message": "No new or changed CSV files to ingest.",
                }
            totals = {
                "content_rows": 0,
                "kept": 0,
                "rejected": 0,
                "persisted_content": 0,
                "rejected_raw_stored": 0,
                "persisted_pitches": 0,
                "source_files": [],
                "content_ids": [],
                "pitch_ids": [],
                "cleaning_report_id": None,
            }
            last_actor = ""
            last_role = ""
            total_files = len(files)
            progress(f"Full pipeline starting for {total_files} file(s) (one file at a time)...")
            for index, rel in enumerate(files, start=1):
                progress(f"--- File {index}/{total_files}: {rel} ---")
                one = self.pipeline_file(rel)
                last_actor = one.get("actor") or last_actor
                last_role = one.get("role") or last_role
                totals["source_files"].append(rel)
                totals["content_rows"] += int((one.get("ingest") or {}).get("content_rows") or 0)
                stats = one.get("content_stats") or {}
                totals["kept"] += int(stats.get("kept") or 0)
                totals["rejected"] += int(stats.get("rejected") or 0)
                totals["persisted_content"] += int(one.get("persisted_content") or 0)
                totals["rejected_raw_stored"] += int(one.get("rejected_raw_stored") or 0)
                totals["cleaning_report_id"] = one.get("cleaning_report_id") or totals["cleaning_report_id"]
            pitches = self.ingestion.ingest_pitches() if not only_new else []
            pitch_ids: list[int] = []
            pitch_stats = {"input": 0, "kept": 0, "rejected": 0}
            if pitches:
                progress(f"Processing pitches.csv ({len(pitches)} row(s))...")
                pitch_clean = self.cleaning.clean_pitches(pitches)
                pitch_stats = pitch_clean["stats"]
                pitch_ids = self.persistence.persist_pitches(pitch_clean["kept"])
                totals["persisted_pitches"] = len(pitch_ids)
                progress(f"Pitches persisted: {len(pitch_ids)}")
            progress(
                f"Full pipeline complete: files={len(totals['source_files'])} "
                f"persisted_content={totals['persisted_content']} "
                f"rejected_raw={totals['rejected_raw_stored']}"
            )
            return {
                "actor": last_actor,
                "role": last_role,
                "ingest": {
                    "content_rows": totals["content_rows"],
                    "pitch_rows": len(pitches),
                    "source_files": totals["source_files"],
                    "message": f"Processed {len(totals['source_files'])} file(s) one at a time.",
                },
                "content_stats": {
                    "input": totals["content_rows"],
                    "kept": totals["kept"],
                    "rejected": totals["rejected"],
                },
                "pitch_stats": pitch_stats,
                "quality_report": "",
                "cleaning_report_id": totals["cleaning_report_id"],
                "persisted_content": totals["persisted_content"],
                "persisted_pitches": totals["persisted_pitches"],
                "rejected_raw_stored": totals["rejected_raw_stored"],
                "persisted_content_ids": totals["content_ids"],
                "persisted_pitch_ids": pitch_ids,
            }

        progress("Full pipeline started (combined mode)...")
        cleaned = self.ingest_and_clean(only_new=only_new, store_raw=True)
        persisted = self.persist(cleaned)
        progress("Full pipeline complete.")
        return {
            "actor": persisted["actor"],
            "role": persisted["role"],
            "ingest": cleaned["ingest"],
            "content_stats": cleaned["content_stats"],
            "pitch_stats": cleaned["pitch_stats"],
            "quality_report": cleaned["content_report"],
            "cleaning_report_id": cleaned.get("cleaning_report_id"),
            "persisted_content": persisted["persisted_content"],
            "persisted_pitches": persisted["persisted_pitches"],
            "rejected_raw_stored": persisted["rejected_raw_stored"],
            "persisted_content_ids": persisted["content_ids"],
            "persisted_pitch_ids": persisted["pitch_ids"],
        }

    def list_reports(self, limit: int = 15) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = require_capability_session(CAP_CLEAN)
        rows = self.reports.list_recent(limit=limit)
        return {
            "actor": user["username"],
            "role": user.get("role_name") or user["role_code"],
            "reports": rows,
        }

    def view_report(self, report_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = require_capability_session(CAP_CLEAN)
        row = self.reports.get(report_id)
        return {
            "actor": user["username"],
            "role": user.get("role_name") or user["role_code"],
            "report": row,
        }


==============================================================================
FILE: modules/admin/README.md
==============================================================================
# Learning guide: `modules/admin/`

## Why this folder exists

Operators with ingest/clean/persist capabilities need one place to **list CSV files**, **ingest**, **clean**, **persist**, and run the **full pipeline** without learning four separate service APIs. `AdminModule` is that façade: it checks the session capability, delegates to services, and returns a single dictionary the console can render.

Despite the name, this is not only for the `admin` role — any role with `CAP_INGEST`, `CAP_CLEAN`, `CAP_PERSIST`, or `CAP_PIPELINE` in `ROLE_CAPABILITIES` sees the corresponding menu items. The class name reflects “newsroom administration of data,” not “must be role admin.”

---

## How this module connects

```
app.do_list_csv_files / do_ingest / do_clean / …
        ↓
AdminModule
        ↓
require_capability_session(CAP_*)
        ↓
IngestionService | CleaningOrchestrator | PersistenceService | ReportService
```

`CleaningOrchestrator` lives in `cleaners/` but is constructed inside `AdminModule` like a service peer. Report persistence to PostgreSQL goes through `ReportService`, not the orchestrator itself.

---

## Class `AdminModule`

**Constructor** accepts optional injected services (useful in tests): `IngestionService`, `CleaningOrchestrator`, `PersistenceService`, `ReportService`. Defaults construct fresh instances.

### Discovery

**`list_csv_files()`** — requires `CAP_INGEST`; returns `all_files`, `new_or_changed` (hash-based), `registered` paths from DB, plus `actor`/`role` strings for the header.

### Ingest family

**`ingest(only_new=False, store_raw=True)`** — full or new-only bulk ingest; optional pitch ingest when not `only_new`; when `store_raw`, calls `persistence.persist_raw_records` with `cleaning_status="pending"`. Returns counts, `source_files`, `collections` summary, `raw_ingest_ids`.

**`ingest_file(source_file, store_raw=True)`** — single-file variant via `ingestion.ingest_one`.

**`ingest_and_clean(only_new)`** — chains ingest logic with cleaning (see implementation for pitch handling and merged return shape).

### Cleaning

**`clean()`** — loads records from ingestion memory/DB paths per implementation, runs `cleaning.clean_content` (and pitches if applicable), returns kept/rejected stats, local JSON/CSV paths under `data/processed`.

### Persist

**`persist()`** — takes cleaned records, `persistence.persist_content` / pitch persist, may store rejected rows back to raw ingest.

### Pipeline orchestration

**`run_pipeline(only_new)`** — ingest → clean → persist in one call.

**`pipeline_file(source_file)`** — single-file full pipeline.

These are what `--pipeline` and `--pipeline-new` CLI flags ultimately exercise through `app.py`.

### Reports

**`list_reports(limit)`** — DB cleaning reports for menu table.

**`view_report(report_id)`** — fetch one report row including `report_json` for detail view.

Each public method starts with the appropriate `require_capability_session` constant so unauthorized roles fail fast with `PermissionDeniedError`.

---

## Return payloads and `app._run_admin`

`app.py` function `_run_admin` knows how to print common keys: `all_files`, `new_or_changed`, `content_rows`, `ingest` sub-dict, `content_stats`, `persisted_content`, etc. When you add a new admin operation, either reuse those keys or extend `_run_admin` once.

---

## Exercises

1. Run **List CSV files** before and after copying a new file into `data/raw` — observe `new_or_changed`.
2. Run **Ingest one** then **Clean** then **Persist** separately; compare to **Full pipeline** on the same file.
3. Open `data/processed/quality_report_content.json` after clean — relate fields to `QualityReport` in cleaners.
4. Grep `AdminModule` for `CAP_` constants and map each to your role’s menu visibility.

==============================================================================
FILE: modules/authentication/auth_service.py
==============================================================================
"""Login / logout / session resolve.

Login: **username** + password (email on employee only).
Sessions are application-layer (no user_session table in your ERD).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Optional

from config.constants import SESSION_TTL_HOURS
from dao.user_dao import UserDAO
from modules.authentication.security import generate_session_token, hash_password, verify_password
from repository.user_repository import UserRepository
from services.permission_service import capabilities_for_role
from utils.errors import ValidationError
from utils.helpers import project_path
from utils.validators import require_password, require_username

SESSION_FILE = project_path("logs", ".session_token")

# API sessions: token → {user_id, expires_at} (simple in-memory store)
_API_SESSIONS: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}


class AuthenticationError(Exception):
    pass


def _expires_at() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(hours=SESSION_TTL_HOURS)


def _parse_expires(expires_raw: str) -> Optional[datetime]:
    if not expires_raw:
        return None
    expires = datetime.fromisoformat(expires_raw)
    if expires.tzinfo is None:
        expires = expires.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    return expires


def _is_expired(expires: Optional[datetime]) -> bool:
    if expires is None:
        return True
    return expires <= datetime.now(timezone.utc)


def _save_session(user_id: int, token: str) -> None:
    """CLI file session (single active console user) + API token map."""
    expires = _expires_at()
    SESSION_FILE.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    SESSION_FILE.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "user_id": user_id,
                "token": token,
                "expires_at": expires.isoformat(),
            }
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    _API_SESSIONS[token] = {"user_id": user_id, "expires_at": expires.isoformat()}


def _load_session() -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
    if not SESSION_FILE.exists():
        return None
    try:
        data = json.loads(SESSION_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):
        return None
    expires = _parse_expires(str(data.get("expires_at") or ""))
    if _is_expired(expires):
        return None
    return data


def _load_api_session(token: str) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
    data = _API_SESSIONS.get(token)
    if not data:
        # Fall back to CLI file if token matches
        file_data = _load_session()
        if file_data and file_data.get("token") == token:
            return file_data
        return None
    expires = _parse_expires(str(data.get("expires_at") or ""))
    if _is_expired(expires):
        _API_SESSIONS.pop(token, None)
        return None
    return data


def _clear_session() -> None:
    if SESSION_FILE.exists():
        SESSION_FILE.unlink()
    _API_SESSIONS.clear()


def _user_payload(user: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    role_code = str(user.get("role_code") or "")
    profiles = UserRepository().get_profile_ids(int(user["user_id"]))
    return {
        "user_id": int(user["user_id"]),
        "username": user["username"],
        "email": user.get("email"),
        "full_name": f"{user.get('first_name', '')} {user.get('last_name', '')}".strip(),
        "role_code": role_code,
        "role_name": user.get("role_name"),
        "capabilities": list(capabilities_for_role(role_code)),
        "profiles": profiles,
    }


def authenticate(
    username: str,
    password: str,
    *,
    ip_address: Optional[str] = None,
    user_agent: Optional[str] = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    del ip_address, user_agent  # reserved for future audit; no DB session row
    username = (username or "").strip()
    if not username or not password:
        raise AuthenticationError("Username and password are required.")

    users = UserDAO()
    user = users.read_by_username(username)
    if user is None:
        raise AuthenticationError("Invalid username or password.")

    if user.get("account_status") != "active":
        raise AuthenticationError("This account is inactive.")

    if not verify_password(password, user["password_hash"]):
        raise AuthenticationError("Invalid username or password.")

    users.record_login_success(int(user["user_id"]))
    token = generate_session_token()
    _save_session(int(user["user_id"]), token)
    payload = _user_payload(user)
    payload["session_token"] = token
    return payload


def resolve_session(session_token: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if session_token:
        data = _load_api_session(session_token)
    else:
        data = _load_session()
    if not data:
        raise AuthenticationError("Invalid or expired session.")
    user = UserDAO().read_by_id(int(data["user_id"]))
    if not user or user.get("account_status") != "active":
        raise AuthenticationError("Invalid or expired session.")
    return _user_payload(user)


def logout(session_token: str | None = None) -> None:
    if session_token:
        _API_SESSIONS.pop(session_token, None)
        file_data = _load_session()
        if file_data and file_data.get("token") == session_token:
            if SESSION_FILE.exists():
                SESSION_FILE.unlink()
        return
    _clear_session()


def change_password(user_id: int, current_password: str, new_password: str) -> None:
    """Logged-in user changes their own password (must know current password)."""
    if not current_password:
        raise ValidationError("Current password cannot be empty.")
    new_password = require_password(new_password)
    if current_password == new_password:
        raise ValidationError("New password must be different from the current password.")

    users = UserDAO()
    user = users.require(int(user_id))
    if not verify_password(current_password, user["password_hash"]):
        raise AuthenticationError("Current password is incorrect.")
    users.update_password_hash(int(user["user_id"]), hash_password(new_password))


def admin_reset_password(username: str, new_password: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Administrator sets a new password for any user (no old password required)."""
    username = require_username(username)
    new_password = require_password(new_password)
    users = UserDAO()
    user = users.read_by_username(username)
    if user is None:
        raise AuthenticationError(f"User '{username}' was not found.")
    users.update_password_hash(int(user["user_id"]), hash_password(new_password))
    return {"user_id": int(user["user_id"]), "username": user["username"]}


==============================================================================
FILE: modules/authentication/README.md
==============================================================================
# Learning guide: `modules/authentication/`

## Why this folder exists

Every console action that touches the pipeline or a newsroom desk assumes someone is logged in and has the right role. Authentication is the boundary: it turns a username and password into a **session** the rest of the app can trust, without building a full web stack or a `user_session` table in PostgreSQL.

NewsMind is a **local console** application. Sessions live in a small JSON file under `logs/.session_token`, not in Redis or cookies. That is a deliberate teaching choice: you can open the file, read `expires_at`, and see exactly how `resolve_session()` decides whether you are still logged in.

Password policy in this project is also intentional and documented here so you do not hunt for features that were never built:

- **Logged out:** only Login (plus DB status from the system menu). There is **no** public “forgot password” flow, no email reset, and no self-service account recovery while locked out.
- **Logged in (any role):** **Change password** — you must supply the current password; the new password is validated and re-hashed.
- **Admin desk only (after admin login):** **Add user** (via `UserService`) and **Reset user password** — the administrator sets a new password for another username without knowing the old one.

Account creation is always an admin action (`UserService.add_user`), not open registration.

---

## How this package connects to the rest of the project

When you choose **Login** in `app.py`, the menu handler `do_login` calls `authenticate(username, password)`. That function loads the row from PostgreSQL through `UserDAO.read_by_username`, checks `account_status`, and runs `verify_password` against the stored `password_hash`. On success it calls `UserDAO.record_login_success`, generates a token with `generate_session_token()`, and writes the session file via `_save_session`.

From that moment, anything that needs the current actor goes through `resolve_session()` — either directly (for example `do_change_password`) or indirectly through `services.access.require_capability_session`, which wraps resolve + capability checks for pipeline and admin modules.

`UserService.reset_password` is the admin path into `admin_reset_password` in this package; it first calls `require_capability_session(CAP_ADMIN)` so a journalist cannot reset someone else’s password through the service layer.

`database/seed_demo_users.py` imports `hash_password` from `security.py` so demo accounts in `seed_data.sql` get the same PBKDF2 format as runtime password changes.

```
app.do_login / do_logout / do_change_password / do_admin_reset_password
        ↓
modules.authentication (auth_service + security)
        ↓
dao.UserDAO  +  repository.UserRepository (profiles in session payload)
        ↓
database.db_cursor  →  PostgreSQL users + employee + role
```

---

## Technology concepts used here

### PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256 (`security.hash_password`)

**PBKDF2** (Password-Based Key Derivation Function 2) stretches a password through many iterations of HMAC. NewsMind uses the stdlib `hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac` with SHA-256. The iteration count and salt size come from `config.constants` (`PASSWORD_HASH_ITERATIONS`, `PASSWORD_SALT_BYTES`).

**Why not store plaintext passwords?** If the database leaks, attackers get hashes, not passwords. PBKDF2 is slow on purpose so guessing millions of passwords becomes expensive.

**Salt:** A random byte string so two users with the same password do not share the same hash. The ERD has a single `password_hash` column, so this project encodes salt and digest in one string: `salt_hex$digest_hex` (see `hash_password` docstring in `security.py`).

### `hmac.compare_digest` (`verify_password`)

After re-deriving the digest with the stored salt, verification uses `hmac.compare_digest` instead of `==`. That avoids **timing attacks** where a naive string compare might leak how much of a guess was correct.

### Session token (`secrets.token_urlsafe`)

The token in the session file is not the password. It is a random URL-safe string from `secrets.token_urlsafe(32)`. The file stores `user_id`, `token`, and `expires_at` (UTC ISO timestamp). `SESSION_TTL_HOURS` from config defines lifetime.

### Application-layer session (no DB session table)

`resolve_session` reloads the user from `UserDAO` on each call so role or `account_status` changes take effect. Expired JSON sessions return `AuthenticationError`, which `app._current_user` treats as logged out.

---

## File-by-file walkthrough

### `security.py` — cryptography only, no I/O

| Function | What it does |
|----------|----------------|
| `hash_password(password, salt=None)` | Generates salt (if needed), runs PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256, returns `salt$digest` string for `users.password_hash`. |
| `verify_password(password, password_hash)` | Parses salt from stored value, re-hashes, compares with `compare_digest`. |
| `generate_session_token()` | Random session token for `_save_session`. |

**Called by:** `auth_service.authenticate`, `change_password`, `admin_reset_password`; `UserService.add_user` (via `hash_password` import); seed scripts.

### `auth_service.py` — login, session, passwords

**Module constant:** `SESSION_FILE = project_path("logs", ".session_token")`.

**Session helpers (private):**

- `_save_session(user_id, token)` — creates `logs/` if needed, writes JSON with expiry = now + `SESSION_TTL_HOURS`.
- `_load_session()` — reads file; returns `None` if missing, corrupt, or past `expires_at`.
- `_clear_session()` — deletes the file on logout.

**`_user_payload(user)`** builds the dict menus need: ids, name, email, `role_code`, `role_name`, `capabilities` from `capabilities_for_role`, and desk `profiles` from `UserRepository.get_profile_ids`.

**`authenticate(username, password)`** — full login path: validate inputs, load user, check active, verify password, stamp `last_login`, save session, return payload including `session_token`. Raises `AuthenticationError` with generic messages for bad credentials (does not reveal whether username exists).

**`resolve_session(session_token=None)`** — loads session file, optionally checks token match, reloads user by id, rebuilds payload. Used everywhere “who is logged in?” matters.

**`logout(session_token=None)`** — clears session file.

**`change_password(user_id, current_password, new_password)`** — logged-in self-service: `require_password` on new password, verify current via `verify_password`, `UserDAO.update_password_hash` with new `hash_password` result.

**`admin_reset_password(username, new_password)`** — admin sets password without old password; raises if username not found.

**`AuthenticationError`** — typed failure for auth boundaries; `services.access` maps it to `AuthError` for the console.

Parameters `ip_address` and `user_agent` on `authenticate` are reserved for future audit logging and are intentionally unused today.

### `__init__.py`

Re-exports the public API: `authenticate`, `logout`, `resolve_session`, `change_password`, `admin_reset_password`, `AuthenticationError`. There is **no** `forgot_password` export — that flow does not exist in the codebase.

---

## Call graph (password-related)

**Change password (any logged-in role):**

```
app.do_change_password
  → resolve_session()
  → change_password(user_id, current, new)
      → UserDAO.require + verify_password(current)
      → hash_password(new) → UserDAO.update_password_hash
```

**Admin reset (admin menu only):**

```
app.do_admin_reset_password
  → UserService.reset_password(username, new)
      → require_capability_session(CAP_ADMIN)
      → admin_reset_password(username, new)
          → UserDAO.read_by_username + update_password_hash
```

---

## Learning exercises

1. Log in as `journalist`, use **Change password**, log out, log in with the new password, then restore the demo password from `config.constants.DEMO_PASSWORDS` if tests need it.
2. As `admin`, use **Reset user password** for `publisher`, then log in as publisher with the new password.
3. Try `UserService().reset_password` while logged in as `editor` — expect `PermissionDeniedError` (see `tests/test_password_flows.py`).
4. After login, open `logs/.session_token`, note `expires_at`, delete the file, and watch the next menu rebuild as logged-out.
5. Read `UserDAO.update_password_hash` and see how `password_changed_at` is stamped in SQL.


==============================================================================
FILE: modules/authentication/security.py
==============================================================================
"""Password hashing (stdlib). Salt embedded in password_hash (ERD has no salt column)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import hmac
import os
import secrets
from typing import Tuple

from config.constants import PASSWORD_HASH_ITERATIONS, PASSWORD_SALT_BYTES


def hash_password(password: str, salt: bytes | None = None) -> str:
    """Return stored form: salt_hex$digest_hex (single ERD password_hash field)."""
    if not password:
        raise ValueError("Password must not be empty")
    salt_bytes = salt if salt is not None else os.urandom(PASSWORD_SALT_BYTES)
    digest = hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac(
        "sha256",
        password.encode("utf-8"),
        salt_bytes,
        PASSWORD_HASH_ITERATIONS,
    )
    return f"{salt_bytes.hex()}${digest.hex()}"


def verify_password(password: str, password_hash: str) -> bool:
    try:
        salt_hex, digest_hex = password_hash.split("$", 1)
        candidate = hash_password(password, salt=bytes.fromhex(salt_hex))
        return hmac.compare_digest(candidate, password_hash)
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return False


def generate_session_token() -> str:
    return secrets.token_urlsafe(32)

"""Content desk operations (news/article/pitch/category/list/view)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from services.content import ContentService


class ContentModule:
    def __init__(self, service: ContentService | None = None) -> None:
        self.service = service or ContentService()

    def add_category(self, name: str, description: str = "") -> int:
        return self.service.add_category(name, description)

    def add_pitch(self, title: str, summary: str, status: str = "submitted") -> int:
        return self.service.add_pitch(title, summary, status=status)

    def add_news(self, **kwargs: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.service.add_news(**kwargs)

    def add_article(self, **kwargs: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.service.add_article(**kwargs)

    def list_content(self, limit: int = 10) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.service.list_content(limit=limit)

    def list_news(self, limit: int = 10) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.service.list_news(limit=limit)

    def list_raw(self, limit: int = 15) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.service.list_raw_ingest(limit=limit)

    def raw_counts(self) -> dict[str, int]:
        return self.service.raw_ingest_counts()

    def get_content(self, content_id: int | str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if isinstance(content_id, str):
            return self.service.get_content_by_input(content_id)
        return self.service.get_content(content_id)

    def update_status(self, content_id: int, status: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.service.update_content_status(content_id, status)

# Learning guide: `modules/content/`

## Why this folder exists

Desk journalists and editors think in terms of **add news**, **list content**, **update status** — not `ContentService` method names. `ContentModule` is a thin, stable API surface the console menu can depend on while validation and SQL stay in `services/content`.

Keeping the module small makes it obvious what the UI layer needs: one class delegating to `ContentService` with no capability logic inside (role gating is in `app._build_menu` today).

---

## How this module connects

```
app.do_add_news / do_list_content / do_view_content / …
        ↓
ContentModule
        ↓
ContentService
        ↓
ContentRepository, CategoryDAO
```

Pipeline operators may also view raw ingest through this module (`list_raw`, `raw_counts`) even though ingestion itself lives under `AdminModule`.

---

## Class `ContentModule`

**Constructor:** optional `ContentService` for testing.

### Delegates (desk operations)

| Method | Forwards to |
|--------|-------------|
| `add_category` | `service.add_category` |
| `add_pitch` | `service.add_pitch` |
| `add_news` | `service.add_news` |
| `add_article` | `service.add_article` |
| `list_content` | `service.list_content` |
| `list_news` | `service.list_news` |
| `list_raw` | `service.list_raw_ingest` |
| `raw_counts` | `service.raw_ingest_counts` |
| `get_content` | `service.get_content` or `get_content_by_input` if str |
| `update_status` | `service.update_content_status` |

**`get_content` id coercion:** if the console passes a string from a prompt, the module routes to `get_content_by_input` so users can paste non-numeric identifiers when supported by the service.

No method in this file touches PostgreSQL directly — that is intentional layering.

---

## Role visibility (in `app.py`, not here)

Admin desk gets the full set of content actions. Journalist gets add news/article, view, lists, raw ingest. Editor/publisher get review/publish status updates. Analyst gets pitch/category adds.

If you add a new desk action, add a `ContentModule` method only when `ContentService` already exposes the workflow; otherwise implement service first, then delegate.

---

## Exercises

1. Compare file length: `content_module.py` vs `content_service.py` — list behaviors only in the service.
2. Add news, then use **View content by ID** — confirm module → service → DAO path.
3. Reason about where you would add `require_capability_session` if desk actions must be enforced below the menu layer.
4. Read `services/content/README.md` for validation detail this module intentionally omits.

"""Journalist feature module."""

from modules.journalist.journalist_module import JournalistModule

==============================================================================
FILE: modules/journalist/journalist_module.py
==============================================================================
"""Journalist tools façade — capability check, then call a service."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Mapping, Sequence

from config.constants import CAP_JOURNALIST_TOOLS
from dao.journalist_dao import JournalistDAO
from services.access import require_capability_session
from services.content import ContentService
from services.journalist import (
    assess_credibility,
    extract_metadata,
    find_duplicates,
    search_evidence,
    search_patents,
    search_related_news,
    validate_article,
)


class JournalistModule:
    def __init__(
        self,
        session_token: str | None = None,
        content: ContentService | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.session_token = session_token
        self.content = content or ContentService()

    def _user(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return require_capability_session(CAP_JOURNALIST_TOOLS, self.session_token)

    def _author_id(self, user: dict[str, Any]) -> int | None:
        """content.author_id FK → journalist.journalist_id (not users.user_id)."""
        profiles = user.get("profiles") or {}
        journalist_id = profiles.get("journalist_id")
        if journalist_id:
            return int(journalist_id)
        return JournalistDAO().ensure_for_user(int(user["user_id"]))

    def research(
        self,
        query: str,
        documents: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]] | None = None,
        *,
        limit: int = 10,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._user()
        hits = search_related_news(query, documents, limit=limit)
        return {"actor": user["username"], "results": hits}

    def evidence_search(
        self,
        query: str,
        documents: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]] | None = None,
        *,
        limit: int = 10,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._user()
        hits = search_evidence(query, documents, limit=limit)
        return {"actor": user["username"], "results": hits}

    def metadata(self, text: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._user()
        return {"actor": user["username"], "entities": extract_metadata(text)}

    def duplicates(
        self,
        documents: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]] | None = None,
        *,
        threshold: float = 0.5,
        limit: int = 20,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._user()
        pairs = find_duplicates(documents, threshold=threshold, limit=limit)
        return {"actor": user["username"], "pairs": pairs}

    def validate(self, record: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._user()
        return {"actor": user["username"], **validate_article(record)}

    def credibility(
        self,
        text: str,
        evidence_docs: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]] | None = None,
        *,
        limit: int = 5,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._user()
        report = assess_credibility(text, evidence_docs, limit=limit)
        return {"actor": user["username"], **report}

    def patents(
        self,
        query: str,
        documents: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]] | None = None,
        *,
        limit: int = 10,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._user()
        hits = search_patents(query, documents, limit=limit)
        return {"actor": user["username"], "results": hits}

    # ── Desk content (parity with console journalist menu) ─────────────────

    def add_news(self, **kwargs: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._user()
        kwargs.setdefault("author_id", self._author_id(user))
        created = self.content.add_news(**kwargs)
        return {"actor": user["username"], **created}

    def add_article(self, **kwargs: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._user()
        kwargs.setdefault("author_id", self._author_id(user))
        created = self.content.add_article(**kwargs)
        return {"actor": user["username"], **created}

    def list_content(self, limit: int = 20, status: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._user()
        return {
            "actor": user["username"],
            "items": self.content.list_content(limit=limit, status=status),
        }

    def suggest_category(self, title: str, summary: str = "", body: str = "") -> dict[str, Any]:
        from ml.aia import predict_category

        user = self._user()
        prediction = predict_category(title, f"{summary or ''} {body or ''}".strip())
        return {"actor": user["username"], **prediction}

    def get_content(self, content_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._user()
        return {"actor": user["username"], "content": self.content.get_content(content_id)}

    def list_news(self, limit: int = 20) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._user()
        return {"actor": user["username"], "items": self.content.list_news(limit=limit)}

    def list_raw_ingest(self, limit: int = 20) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._user()
        return {
            "actor": user["username"],
            "items": self.content.list_raw_ingest(limit=limit),
            "counts": self.content.raw_ingest_counts(),
        }

    def add_evidence(self, **kwargs: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._user()
        evidence_id = self.content.add_evidence(**kwargs)
        return {"actor": user["username"], "evidence_id": evidence_id}

    def update_content_status(self, content_id: int, status: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._user()
        updated = self.content.update_content_status(content_id, status)
        return {"actor": user["username"], **updated}

from modules.journalist import JournalistModule

==============================================================================
FILE: modules/README.md
==============================================================================
# Learning guide: `modules/`

## Why this folder exists

The console application (`app.py`) would become unmaintainable if every menu action embedded SQL, pandas, and cleaning logic. **Feature modules** group user-visible capabilities into packages that mirror how product owners think: authenticate, run the data pipeline as admin, work the content desk.

`modules/` is **not** the same as `services/`:

| Layer | Role | Example |
|-------|------|---------|
| **`api/schemas/`** | JSON shapes + validation for HTTP only | `SuggestCategoryRequest` |
| **`modules/`** | Thin façade for console / some routers (session, actor, capability) | `JournalistModule.suggest_category` |
| **`services/`** | Real domain workflows (SQL, ML calls, business rules) | `ContentService`, `AIAService` |
| **`ml/`** | Pure predictors / ranking helpers (no HTTP, minimal SQL) | `ml.aia.predict_category` |

- **Modules** = “desk button” layer: check who is logged in, call a service, return a dict with `actor`.
- **Services** = reusable business logic used by modules **and** FastAPI routers (editor/AIA/manager often call services directly).

NewsMind follows the same packaging idea as `entities/`: real features get folders; content-related code lives under `modules/content/`, not one folder per job title.

---

## Package map

| Package | Responsibility | Primary services |
|---------|----------------|------------------|
| `modules/authentication/` | Login, session, password change, admin reset helper | UserDAO, UserRepository, security |
| `modules/admin/` | CSV pipeline, clean, persist, reports | IngestionService, CleaningOrchestrator, PersistenceService, ReportService |
| `modules/content/` | Desk CRUD and listings | ContentService |
| `modules/journalist/` | Research, evidence, metadata, duplicates, validation, credibility, patents | `services/journalist` + `ml/` |

Each subdirectory has its own learning README with function-level detail.

---

## Cross-cutting pattern: capability gates

Pipeline and sensitive admin paths call **`services.access.require_capability_session`** at the start of `AdminModule` methods. Content desk paths currently rely on **menu visibility** in `app._build_menu` (role-specific desk sections); services still validate data.

Authentication module functions are called directly from `app.py` for login/logout/password — no separate `AuthenticationModule` class, just `auth_service.py` functions.

---

## How `app.py` wires modules

At startup:

```python
admin = AdminModule()
content = ContentModule()
users = UserService()  # service, not under modules/
```

Menu builder `_build_menu`:

- Logged out: Login + system DB status (password reset is **not** offered while logged out).
- Logged in: Logout, change password, role-based pipeline block (capabilities), role desk block.

`_run_admin` pretty-prints dict payloads from `AdminModule` (file lists, ingest counts, cleaning stats).

Desk handlers call `content.*` or `users.*` directly.

---

## Dependency direction

```
app.py  →  modules.*  →  services.*  →  repository / cleaners  →  dao  →  database
                ↓
         modules.authentication  →  dao (also used by services.user)
```

Modules should **not** import `app.py`. Modules may import authentication (admin does not; access imports auth).

---

## When to add a new module

Add a subpackage when a new **menu area** appears with multiple actions and its own vocabulary — for example a future “analytics” desk. Keep one-off scripts out of `modules/`; they belong in tools or tests.

---

## Exercises

1. Read `AdminModule.ingest` return dict keys and match them to `_run_admin` display branches in `app.py`.
2. Compare journalist vs admin desk entries in `_build_menu` — list which `ContentModule` methods each can reach.
3. Trace one pipeline action from menu number to `require_capability_session` capability constant name in `config.constants`.
4. Skim all three child READMEs and draw your own dependency sketch on paper.

# NewsMind

Console newsroom backend plus FastAPI role APIs (Swagger at `/docs`).

**Learning docs:** each package has a detailed `README.md` (technologies, why imports exist, how folders connect, function-by-function learning notes — not just a short reference).

## Architecture

| Layer | Path | Responsibility |
|---|---|---|
| Console CLI | `app.py` | Role menus, human-readable output |
| FastAPI + Swagger | `api/` | Auth, journalist, AIA, CAE, publisher, editor, manager |
| Feature modules | `modules/` | Auth, admin pipeline, content desk, journalist tools |
| Services | `services/` | Pipeline + desks (`journalist/`, `aia/`, `adaptation/`, `publisher/`, `editor/`, `manager/`) |
| ML helpers | `ml/` | `common` + `aia` + `editor` + `adaptation` |
| API schemas | `api/schemas/` | Pydantic HTTP request/response shapes (not business logic) |
| Naming guide | `docs/NAMING.md` | What “urgency” vs “pitch importance” means |
| Cleaners | `cleaners/` | Ingest cleaning stages |
| Repositories | `repository/` | Aggregate DAO access |
| DAO / Entities | `dao/`, `entities/` | Table access + domain objects |

Modules follow the same idea as `entities/`: real features get folders; content grouped under `content/`.

Flow: **ingest → clean → persist**

| Store | Table |
|---|---|
| Raw CSV rows | `raw_ingest` |
| Cleaned news | `content` (`raw_body` + `content_body`) |
| File registry | `ingest_file` |
| Quality reports | `cleaning_report` |

---

## Config

Copy `ENV.example` → `.env` (a `.env` is already present for local use).  
`config/db_config.py` + `config/runtime.py` load it automatically.

Hosted DB and runtime tuning: see **`DEPLOYMENT.md`**.  
Defaults include chunked CSV reads, file-by-file pipeline, DB SSL/timeouts, and **console progress logs** for ingest/clean/persist steps. Prefer **Pipeline one CSV** or **new files only** for large folders.

---

## Start

```powershell
cd "G:\NewsMind AI\NewsMindAI V2"
$env:PYTHONPATH = "G:\NewsMind AI\NewsMindAI V2"
python -m pip install -r requirements.txt
python app.py --init-db
python app.py
```

FastAPI + Swagger (~71 endpoints across auth / journalist / AIA / CAE / publisher / editor / manager):

```powershell
$env:PYTHONPATH = "G:\NewsMind AI\NewsMindAI V2"
uvicorn api.main:app --reload
```

Open **http://127.0.0.1:8000/docs** → login → Authorize with Bearer `session_token`.  
Demo users: `adapter` (CAE), `publisher`, `editor`, `manager`, `analyst`, `journalist`, `admin`.
### Passwords and accounts

| Situation | What the console offers |
|-----------|-------------------------|
| **Logged out** | **Login** only (plus **DB status** under System). There is no forgot-password, email reset, or self-service recovery while logged out. |
| **Logged in** (any role) | **Change password** — you must enter the current password; the new password is validated (`require_password`) and stored as PBKDF2 hash. |
| **Admin desk** (admin logged in) | **Add user** (`UserService.add_user`) and **Reset user password** (`UserService.reset_password` / `admin_reset_password`). Administrators set another user’s new password; they do not need the old one. |

New accounts are created only through admin **Add user**, not open self-registration.

After **Login**, the menu also shows pipeline + desk actions for that role (capabilities from `ROLE_CAPABILITIES`).

---

## Pipeline options (after login with caps)

- List CSV files (new/changed marked)
- Ingest one / new-only / all
- Clean, ingest+clean, persist
- Pipeline one file / new-only / all
- List / view cleaning reports (DB)

```powershell
python app.py --login --username admin --password "Admin@NewsMind2026!"
python app.py --list-csv
python app.py --ingest-file "my_new_dataset.csv"
python app.py --pipeline-new
python app.py --list-reports
```

---

## Demo logins

| Username | Password |
|---|---|
| `admin` | `Admin@NewsMind2026!` |
| `journalist` | `Journalist@NewsMind2026!` |
| `editor` | `Editor@NewsMind2026!` |
| `publisher` | `Publisher@NewsMind2026!` |
| `manager` | `Manager@NewsMind2026!` |
| `analyst` | `Analyst@NewsMind2026!` |
| `adapter` | `Adapter@NewsMind2026!` |

---

## Datasets

`data/raw/**/*.csv` — news feed for the pipeline.

- No CSV row caps
- Add a new file after others are done → **Ingest one** or **new/changed only**
- Large dumps can stay in `data/archive/` until moved into `data/raw`

## Exit / cancel

After you choose a menu option, a tip is shown before that step starts: `q` / `exit` cancels prompts; `Ctrl+C` cancels a running step. Menu `0` exits.

from repository.content_repository import ContentRepository

==============================================================================
FILE: repository/content_repository.py
==============================================================================
"""Content-side repository: content, article, news, evidence, pitches, raw ingest."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from dao.article_dao import ArticleDAO
from dao.content_dao import ContentDAO
from dao.evidence_dao import EvidenceDAO
from dao.news_dao import NewsDAO
from dao.raw_ingest_dao import RawIngestDAO
from dao.story_pitch_dao import StoryPitchDAO
from entities.content.article import Article
from entities.content.content import Content
from entities.content.evidence import Evidence
from entities.content.news import News
from entities.content.story_pitch import StoryPitch


class ContentRepository:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.content = ContentDAO()
        self.articles = ArticleDAO()
        self.news = NewsDAO()
        self.evidence = EvidenceDAO()
        self.pitches = StoryPitchDAO()
        self.raw = RawIngestDAO()

    def save_pitch(self, pitch: StoryPitch) -> int:
        return self.pitches.create(pitch)

    def save_content_bundle(self, content: Content, *, event_location: str = "") -> int:
        content_id = self.content.create(content)
        if (content.content_kind or "article") == "news":
            self.news.create(
                News(
                    content_id=content_id,
                    event_title=content.title,
                    event_location=event_location or None,
                )
            )
        else:
            self.articles.create(Article(content_id=content_id, article_type="feature"))
        return content_id

    def save_evidence(self, evidence: Evidence) -> int:
        return self.evidence.create(evidence)

    def get_content(self, content_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.content.require(content_id)

FILE: repository/newsroom_repository.py

"""Compatibility façade over ContentRepository + UserRepository."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from entities.content.content import Content
from entities.content.evidence import Evidence
from entities.content.story_pitch import StoryPitch
from repository.content_repository import ContentRepository
from repository.user_repository import UserRepository


class NewsroomRepository:
    """Thin façade kept for older call sites; prefer Content/User repositories."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.content_repo = ContentRepository()
        self.user_repo = UserRepository()
        self.users = self.user_repo.users
        self.journalists = self.user_repo.journalists
        self.editors = self.user_repo.editors
        self.analysts = self.user_repo.analysts
        self.adapters = self.user_repo.adapters
        self.publishers = self.user_repo.publishers
        self.managers = self.user_repo.managers
        self.pitches = self.content_repo.pitches
        self.content = self.content_repo.content
        self.articles = self.content_repo.articles
        self.news = self.content_repo.news
        self.evidence = self.content_repo.evidence

    def ensure_demo_user(self, username: str = "journalist") -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.user_repo.ensure_demo_user(username)

    def get_profile_ids(self, user_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.user_repo.get_profile_ids(user_id)

    def save_pitch(self, pitch: StoryPitch) -> int:
        return self.content_repo.save_pitch(pitch)

    def save_content_bundle(self, content: Content, *, event_location: str = "") -> int:
        return self.content_repo.save_content_bundle(content, event_location=event_location)

    def save_evidence(self, evidence: Evidence) -> int:
        return self.content_repo.save_evidence(evidence)
