FILE: repository/README.md
==============================================================================
# Learning guide: `repository/`

## Why this folder exists

A single DAO answers “how do I read row X from table Y?” A **repository** answers “how do I perform this **newsroom use case** that might touch several tables?” Repositories sit between **services** (validation, session, orchestration) and **DAOs** (SQL).

NewsMind uses two repository classes — not one repository per table — because the domain splits cleanly into **people** (`UserRepository`) and **content** (`ContentRepository`).

---

## How repositories connect to callers

**`UserRepository`** is constructed inside `UserService`, `PersistenceService` (for `ensure_demo_user`), and `auth_service._user_payload` (for desk profile ids in the session).

**`ContentRepository`** is the default backing store for `ContentService` and `PersistenceService`. Cleaning and ingestion services do not use repositories directly; they pass dict records into services that eventually call `ContentRepository` or raw DAOs through it.

```
ContentService.add_news
  → ContentRepository.content.create + .news.create

UserService.add_user
  → UserRepository.create_employee + create_user + ensure_desk

PersistenceService.persist_content
  → ContentRepository.content.create (+ news/article split)
  → UserRepository.ensure_demo_user (default author)
```

Repositories **do not** call `resolve_session`; session and capability checks stay in `services.access`, modules, or `app.py`.

---

## `UserRepository` — people, roles, desks

**Construction:** eagerly builds DAO handles: `UserDAO`, `EmployeeDAO`, `RoleDAO`, and one DAO per desk profile (journalist, editor, publisher, audience analyst, content adaptation editor, newsroom manager).

**`ensure_demo_user(username="journalist")`** — used when pipeline persist needs an author; raises `NotFoundError` with a hint to run `--init-db` if seed data is missing.

**`get_profile_ids(user_id)`** — loops desk DAOs, returns a small dict like `{"journalist_id": 3}` for keys that exist. Fed into login session payload so future features know which desk row belongs to the user.

**`ensure_desk(user_id, role_code)`** — dispatches on `role_code` string to the correct desk DAO’s `ensure_for_user`. Called after admin **Add user** so the database has both `users` row and desk profile row.

**`create_employee` / `create_user`** — thin wrappers around entity `create` on employee and user DAOs.

**`find_by_username`** — delegates to `UserDAO.read_by_username`.

**`role_id_by_code`** — maps `journalist` → `role_id` for inserts.

The repository never hashes passwords; `UserService` passes `password_hash` on the `User` entity.

---

## `ContentRepository` — stories, articles, raw staging

**Construction:** `ContentDAO`, `ArticleDAO`, `NewsDAO`, `EvidenceDAO`, `StoryPitchDAO`, `RawIngestDAO`.

**`save_pitch`** — single-table create for analyst desk flows.

**`save_content_bundle`** — creates base `content`, then branches on `content_kind`: `news` → `News` row, else → `Article` row. This encodes the ERD pattern “content is the base; news/article are extensions keyed by `content_id`.”

**`save_evidence`** — evidence attachment create.

**`get_content(content_id)`** — `content.require` for fail-fast reads.

**Raw ingest:** exposed as `self.raw` for `PersistenceService` bulk inserts without wrapping every method.

Repositories here are intentionally smaller than “generic DDD repositories” — they aggregate DAOs the services already need, not every possible query.

---

## Why not skip repositories?

You *could* call DAOs from `ContentService` directly. NewsMind keeps repositories so:

- Service files stay focused on validation and console rules.
- Multi-table creates (`save_content_bundle`) have one home.
- Callers get one façade instead of wiring several DAOs by hand.

When you add a feature that only needs one table, a service may still call a DAO directly (e.g. `ContentService` uses `CategoryDAO` directly for categories). That is acceptable; repositories are for **coordination**, not dogma.

---

## Exercises

1. Follow `UserService.add_user` through all three repository steps (employee, user, desk).
2. Compare `ContentService.add_news` (explicit content + news creates) with `save_content_bundle` — when would you use each?
3. Sketch what would break if `get_profile_ids` stopped running at login — would menus fail, or only future profile features?
4. Add a mental diagram: which repository methods are hit during `admin.run_pipeline` end-to-end?

"""User-side repository: users, employees, roles, desk profiles."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Optional

from dao.audience_insight_analyst_dao import AudienceInsightAnalystDAO
from dao.content_adaptation_editor_dao import ContentAdaptationEditorDAO
from dao.editor_dao import EditorDAO
from dao.employee_dao import EmployeeDAO
from dao.journalist_dao import JournalistDAO
from dao.newsroom_manager_dao import NewsroomManagerDAO
from dao.publisher_dao import PublisherDAO
from dao.role_dao import RoleDAO
from dao.user_dao import UserDAO
from entities.employee import Employee
from entities.user import User
from utils.errors import NotFoundError


class UserRepository:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.users = UserDAO()
        self.employees = EmployeeDAO()
        self.roles = RoleDAO()
        self.journalists = JournalistDAO()
        self.editors = EditorDAO()
        self.analysts = AudienceInsightAnalystDAO()
        self.adapters = ContentAdaptationEditorDAO()
        self.publishers = PublisherDAO()
        self.managers = NewsroomManagerDAO()

    def ensure_demo_user(self, username: str = "journalist") -> dict[str, Any]:
        existing = self.users.read_by_username(username)
        if existing:
            return existing
        raise NotFoundError(f"User '{username}' missing — run python app.py --init-db")

    def get_profile_ids(self, user_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        out: dict[str, Any] = {}
        for key, row in (
            ("aia_id", self.analysts.read_by_user_id(user_id)),
            ("editor_id", self.editors.read_by_user_id(user_id)),
            ("journalist_id", self.journalists.read_by_user_id(user_id)),
            ("cae_id", self.adapters.read_by_user_id(user_id)),
            ("publisher_id", self.publishers.read_by_user_id(user_id)),
            ("newsroom_manager_id", self.managers.read_by_user_id(user_id)),
        ):
            if row:
                out[key] = int(row[key])
        return out

    def ensure_desk(self, user_id: int, role_code: str) -> None:
        if role_code == "audience_insight_analyst":
            self.analysts.ensure_for_user(user_id)
        elif role_code == "editor":
            self.editors.ensure_for_user(user_id)
        elif role_code == "journalist":
            self.journalists.ensure_for_user(user_id)
        elif role_code == "content_adaptation_editor":
            self.adapters.ensure_for_user(user_id)
        elif role_code == "publisher":
            self.publishers.ensure_for_user(user_id)
        elif role_code == "newsroom_manager":
            self.managers.ensure_for_user(user_id)

    def create_employee(self, employee: Employee) -> int:
        return self.employees.create(employee)

    def create_user(self, user: User) -> int:
        return self.users.create(user)

    def find_by_username(self, username: str) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.users.read_by_username(username)

    def role_id_by_code(self, role_code: str) -> Optional[int]:
        return self.roles.get_id_by_code(role_code)

"""Live smoke test: login each demo role and hit its endpoints."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import httpx

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from config.constants import DEMO_PASSWORDS  # noqa: E402

BASE = "http://127.0.0.1:8000"
SAMPLE_BODY = (
    "The city council approved a housing plan covering several wards after public review. "
    "Officials said funding will begin next quarter for the first phase."
)


def login(client: httpx.Client, username: str) -> str:
    r = client.post(
        "/auth/login",
        json={"username": username, "password": DEMO_PASSWORDS[username]},
    )
    r.raise_for_status()
    return r.json()["session_token"]


def call(
    client: httpx.Client,
    method: str,
    path: str,
    *,
    token: str | None = None,
    json_body: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    expect: set[int] | None = None,
) -> tuple[str, int, str]:
    headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"} if token else {}
    r = client.request(method, path, headers=headers, json=json_body)
    ok_codes = expect or {200}
    status = "PASS" if r.status_code in ok_codes else "FAIL"
    detail = ""
    if status == "FAIL":
        detail = (r.text or "")[:180].replace("\n", " ")
    return f"{method} {path}", r.status_code, f"{status}{(' | ' + detail) if detail else ''}"


def run() -> int:
    results: list[tuple[str, str, int, str]] = []
    with httpx.Client(base_url=BASE, timeout=60.0) as client:
        # Health / docs
        for method, path in (("GET", "/docs"), ("GET", "/openapi.json")):
            name, code, status = call(client, method, path)
            results.append(("public", name, code, status))

        openapi = client.get("/openapi.json").json()
        paths = sorted(openapi.get("paths") or {})

        # ---- auth ----
        token_admin = login(client, "admin")
        for item in (
            call(client, "GET", "/auth/me", token=token_admin),
            call(
                client,
                "POST",
                "/auth/change-password",
                token=token_admin,
                json_body={
                    "current_password": DEMO_PASSWORDS["admin"],
                    "new_password": DEMO_PASSWORDS["admin"],
                },
                expect={200, 400, 422},
            ),
        ):
            results.append(("admin/auth", *item))

        # ---- journalist ----
        j = login(client, "journalist")
        news = client.post(
            "/journalist/news",
            headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {j}"},
            json={
                "title": "Smoke test housing plan advances",
                "body": SAMPLE_BODY,
                "summary": "Council housing update",
                "status": "draft",
            },
        )
        results.append(("journalist", "POST /journalist/news", news.status_code, "PASS" if news.status_code == 200 else f"FAIL | {news.text[:160]}"))
        content_id = int((news.json() or {}).get("content_id") or 0) if news.status_code == 200 else 0

        article = client.post(
            "/journalist/articles",
            headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {j}"},
            json={
                "title": "Smoke feature on civic housing reform",
                "body": SAMPLE_BODY,
                "article_type": "feature",
                "status": "draft",
            },
        )
        results.append(("journalist", "POST /journalist/articles", article.status_code, "PASS" if article.status_code == 200 else f"FAIL | {article.text[:160]}"))
        article_id = int((article.json() or {}).get("content_id") or 0) if article.status_code == 200 else content_id

        for item in (
            call(client, "GET", "/journalist/content?limit=10", token=j),
            call(client, "GET", f"/journalist/content/{content_id or 1}", token=j, expect={200, 404}),
            call(client, "GET", "/journalist/news?limit=10", token=j),
            call(client, "GET", "/journalist/raw-ingest?limit=10", token=j),
            call(
                client,
                "POST",
                "/journalist/research",
                token=j,
                json_body={"query": "housing plan", "limit": 5},
            ),
            call(
                client,
                "POST",
                "/journalist/evidence-search",
                token=j,
                json_body={"query": "housing funding", "limit": 5},
            ),
            call(
                client,
                "POST",
                "/journalist/metadata",
                token=j,
                json_body={"text": SAMPLE_BODY},
            ),
            call(
                client,
                "POST",
                "/journalist/duplicates",
                token=j,
                json_body={
                    "documents": [
                        {"id": "a", "title": "Housing plan", "text": SAMPLE_BODY},
                        {"id": "b", "title": "Housing plan update", "text": SAMPLE_BODY},
                    ]
                },
            ),
            call(
                client,
                "POST",
                "/journalist/credibility",
                token=j,
                json_body={"text": SAMPLE_BODY, "limit": 3},
            ),
            call(
                client,
                "POST",
                "/journalist/patents",
                token=j,
                json_body={"query": "housing construction materials", "limit": 3},
            ),
            call(
                client,
                "POST",
                "/journalist/validate",
                token=j,
                json_body={"title": "Long enough headline for validation", "text": SAMPLE_BODY, "status": "draft"},
            ),
            call(
                client,
                "POST",
                "/journalist/suggest-category",
                token=j,
                json_body={"title": "Lakers win NBA finals thriller", "summary": "Sports championship coverage"},
            ),
            call(
                client,
                "PUT",
                f"/journalist/content/{content_id or 1}/status",
                token=j,
                json_body={"status": "submitted"},
                expect={200, 404},
            ),
            call(
                client,
                "POST",
                "/journalist/evidence",
                token=j,
                json_body={
                    "content_id": content_id or article_id or 1,
                    "title": "Smoke evidence doc",
                    "evidence_type": "link",
                    "source_url": "https://example.com/housing",
                },
                expect={200, 404, 422},
            ),
            # forbidden desk
            call(client, "GET", "/aia/performance", token=j, expect={403}),
        ):
            results.append(("journalist", *item))

        # ---- analyst / AIA ----
        a = login(client, "analyst")
        for item in (
            call(
                client,
                "POST",
                "/aia/analyze-engagement",
                token=a,
                json_body={"views": 500, "likes": 40, "shares": 12, "comments": 8},
            ),
            call(
                client,
                "POST",
                "/aia/analyze-interest",
                token=a,
                json_body={"views": 500, "likes": 40, "shares": 12, "comments": 8},
            ),
            call(
                client,
                "POST",
                "/aia/analyze-trends",
                token=a,
                json_body={"title": "Sports finals draw record TV audience", "summary": "Ratings surge overnight"},
            ),
            call(
                client,
                "POST",
                "/aia/predict-category",
                token=a,
                json_body={"title": "Parliament debates new budget bill", "summary": "Politics from the capital"},
            ),
            call(
                client,
                "POST",
                "/aia/generate-story-pitch",
                token=a,
                json_body={
                    "title": "Smoke AIA pitch on civic sports funding",
                    "summary": "City may expand youth sports programs next year.",
                    "trend_score": 70,
                    "audience_interest_score": 65,
                    "coverage_gap_score": 40,
                },
            ),
            call(client, "GET", "/aia/story-pitches", token=a),
            call(client, "GET", "/aia/story-pitches/top", token=a),
            call(client, "GET", "/aia/performance", token=a),
            call(client, "GET", "/editor/queue", token=a, expect={403}),
        ):
            results.append(("analyst", *item))

        # ---- editor ----
        e = login(client, "editor")
        for item in (
            call(client, "GET", "/editor/queue", token=e),
            call(
                client,
                "POST",
                "/editor/queue/score",
                token=e,
                json_body={
                    "title": "BREAKING plant fire halts production",
                    "summary": "Emergency shutdown after a factory fire.",
                },
            ),
            call(client, "GET", "/editor/pitches", token=e),
            call(
                client,
                "POST",
                "/editor/pitches/duplicates",
                token=e,
                json_body={"title": "Smoke AIA pitch on civic sports funding"},
            ),
            call(
                client,
                "POST",
                "/editor/assign/suggest",
                token=e,
                json_body={"topic": "Sports city funding", "title": "Youth sports budget"},
            ),
            call(client, "GET", "/editor/content?status=submitted", token=e),
            call(client, "GET", "/editor/event-groups", token=e),
        ):
            results.append(("editor", *item))

        # content approve/rework if we have submitted content
        if content_id:
            rework = call(
                client,
                "PUT",
                f"/editor/content/{content_id}/rework",
                token=e,
                json_body={"remarks": "Add one more source"},
            )
            results.append(("editor", *rework))
            # journalist resubmit
            resub = call(
                client,
                "PUT",
                f"/journalist/content/{content_id}/status",
                token=j,
                json_body={"status": "submitted"},
            )
            results.append(("journalist", *resub))
            appr = call(client, "PUT", f"/editor/content/{content_id}/approve", token=e)
            results.append(("editor", *appr))

        # ---- CAE / adapter ----
        c = login(client, "adapter")
        adapt_body = {
            "original_title": "Smoke feature on civic housing reform",
            "original_content": SAMPLE_BODY,
            "target_platforms": ["SOCIAL_MEDIA", "WEBSITE"],
        }
        created = client.post(
            "/cae/adaptations",
            headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {c}"},
            json=adapt_body,
        )
        results.append(
            (
                "adapter",
                "POST /cae/adaptations",
                created.status_code,
                "PASS" if created.status_code in {200, 201} else f"FAIL | {created.text[:180]}",
            )
        )
        draft_id = None
        adaptation_id = None
        if created.status_code in {200, 201}:
            payload = created.json()
            adaptation_id = payload.get("id")
            drafts = payload.get("drafts") or []
            if drafts:
                draft_id = drafts[0].get("id")

        for item in (
            call(
                client,
                "GET",
                f"/cae/adaptations/{adaptation_id or 1}",
                token=c,
                expect={200, 404},
            ),
            call(client, "GET", f"/cae/adaptation-drafts/{draft_id or 1}", token=c, expect={200, 404}),
        ):
            results.append(("adapter", *item))
        if draft_id:
            for item in (
                call(
                    client,
                    "POST",
                    f"/cae/adaptation-drafts/{draft_id}/regenerate",
                    token=c,
                    json_body={"remarks": "Try a shorter social cut"},
                    expect={200, 201},
                ),
                call(
                    client,
                    "POST",
                    f"/cae/adaptation-drafts/{draft_id}/approve",
                    token=c,
                    json_body={"remarks": "Looks good"},
                    expect={200, 400, 422},
                ),
                call(
                    client,
                    "GET",
                    f"/cae/adaptation-drafts/{draft_id}/versions",
                    token=c,
                ),
            ):
                results.append(("adapter", *item))

        # ---- publisher ----
        p = login(client, "publisher")
        for item in (
            call(client, "GET", "/publisher/articles", token=p),
            call(client, "GET", "/publisher/analytics", token=p),
        ):
            results.append(("publisher", *item))
        if content_id:
            for item in (
                call(
                    client,
                    "PUT",
                    f"/publisher/articles/{content_id}/template",
                    token=p,
                    json_body={"template_code": "web_standard"},
                    expect={200, 404, 400},
                ),
                call(client, "GET", f"/publisher/articles/{content_id}/preview", token=p, expect={200, 404, 400}),
                call(client, "POST", f"/publisher/articles/{content_id}/publish", token=p, expect={200, 404, 400}),
            ):
                results.append(("publisher", *item))

        # ---- manager ----
        m = login(client, "manager")
        for item in (
            call(client, "GET", "/manager/overview", token=m),
            call(client, "GET", "/manager/queue", token=m),
            call(client, "GET", "/manager/bottlenecks", token=m),
            call(client, "GET", "/manager/analytics", token=m),
            call(client, "GET", "/manager/workload", token=m),
            call(client, "GET", "/manager/deadline-risk", token=m),
            call(client, "GET", "/manager/resources", token=m),
            call(client, "GET", "/manager/event-groups", token=m),
            call(client, "GET", "/manager/pitches", token=m),
            call(client, "GET", "/manager/content?status=submitted", token=m),
        ):
            results.append(("manager", *item))

        # logout
        results.append(("admin/auth", *call(client, "POST", "/auth/logout", token=token_admin)))

    # Print report
    failed = [r for r in results if r[3].startswith("FAIL")]
    print(f"OpenAPI path count: {len(paths)}")
    print(f"Checked calls: {len(results)}  PASS={len(results)-len(failed)}  FAIL={len(failed)}")
    print("-" * 80)
    by_role: dict[str, list] = {}
    for role, name, code, status in results:
        by_role.setdefault(role, []).append((name, code, status))
    for role, rows in by_role.items():
        print(f"\n## {role}")
        for name, code, status in rows:
            mark = "OK" if status.startswith("PASS") else "X "
            print(f"  {mark} {code:>3}  {name}  {'' if status=='PASS' else status}")

    out = ROOT / "scripts" / "smoke_report.json"
    out.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            [{"role": r, "endpoint": n, "status_code": c, "result": s} for r, n, c, s in results],
            indent=2,
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    print("\nWrote", out)
    return 1 if failed else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(run())

"""Shared session + capability gate for modules/services."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from modules.authentication.auth_service import AuthenticationError, resolve_session
from services.permission_service import PermissionError, require_capability
from utils.errors import AuthError, PermissionDeniedError


def require_capability_session(
    capability: str,
    session_token: str | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        user = resolve_session(session_token)
    except AuthenticationError as exc:
        raise AuthError(str(exc)) from exc
    try:
        require_capability(user["role_code"], capability)
    except PermissionError as exc:
        raise PermissionDeniedError(str(exc)) from exc
    return user

"""Content Adaptation Editor (CAE) services."""

from services.adaptation.adaptation_service import AdaptationService
from services.adaptation.review_service import ReviewService

__all__ = ["AdaptationService", "ReviewService"]


==============================================================================
FILE: services/adaptation/adaptation_service.py
==============================================================================
"""Create multi-platform adaptation requests + drafts."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from config.constants import ADAPTATION_PLATFORMS
from dao.adaptation_dao import AdaptationDAO
from ml.adaptation import format_output, predict_sentences, validate_facts
from utils.errors import ValidationError
from utils.validators import require_nonempty, require_title


class AdaptationService:
    def __init__(self, dao: AdaptationDAO | None = None) -> None:
        self.dao = dao or AdaptationDAO()

    def create(
        self,
        *,
        original_title: str,
        original_content: str,
        target_platforms: list[str],
        created_by: int | None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        title = require_title(original_title)
        content = require_nonempty(original_content, "Original content")
        if len(content) < 20:
            raise ValidationError("Original content must be at least 20 characters.")
        if not target_platforms:
            raise ValidationError("Select at least one target platform.")

        platforms: list[str] = []
        for raw in target_platforms:
            platform = str(raw).strip().upper()
            if platform not in ADAPTATION_PLATFORMS:
                raise ValidationError(
                    f"Unsupported platform '{raw}'. Allowed: {', '.join(ADAPTATION_PLATFORMS)}"
                )
            if platform in platforms:
                raise ValidationError("target_platforms must not contain duplicates.")
            platforms.append(platform)

        request_id = self.dao.create_request(
            original_title=title,
            original_content=content,
            created_by=created_by,
            status="PROCESSING",
        )
        try:
            for platform in platforms:
                selected, meta, threshold = predict_sentences(title, content, platform)
                adapted_title, adapted_content = format_output(platform, title, selected)
                flags = validate_facts(content, adapted_content)
                status = (
                    "PENDING_REVIEW" if flags.get("passed") else "REQUIRES_MANUAL_REVIEW"
                )
                self.dao.create_draft(
                    request_id=request_id,
                    platform=platform,
                    version_number=1,
                    adapted_title=adapted_title,
                    adapted_content=adapted_content,
                    model_version=str(meta.get("version", "heuristic-1.0.0")),
                    status=status,
                    generation_parameters={
                        "threshold": threshold,
                        "selected_sentence_indexes": [row[0] for row in selected],
                    },
                    validation_flags=flags,
                )
            self.dao.update_request_status(request_id, "COMPLETED")
        except Exception:
            self.dao.update_request_status(request_id, "FAILED")
            raise

        return self.get_request(request_id)

    def get_request(self, request_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        request = self.dao.require_request(request_id)
        drafts = self.dao.list_drafts_for_request(request_id)
        return {**request, "drafts": drafts}


==============================================================================
FILE: services/adaptation/review_service.py
==============================================================================
"""Editor review actions on adaptation drafts."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from dao.adaptation_dao import AdaptationDAO
from ml.adaptation import format_output, predict_sentences, validate_facts
from utils.errors import ValidationError
from utils.validators import require_nonempty, require_title


class ReviewService:
    def __init__(self, dao: AdaptationDAO | None = None) -> None:
        self.dao = dao or AdaptationDAO()

    def require_draft(self, draft_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.dao.require_draft(draft_id)

    def approve(self, draft_id: int, reviewer_id: int | None, remarks: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
        draft = self.dao.require_draft(draft_id)
        self.dao.update_draft_status(draft_id, "APPROVED")
        self.dao.add_review(
            draft_id=draft_id,
            action="APPROVE",
            reviewed_by=reviewer_id,
            remarks=remarks,
        )
        return self.dao.require_draft(draft_id)

    def reject(self, draft_id: int, reviewer_id: int | None, remarks: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        remarks = require_nonempty(remarks, "Remarks")
        if len(remarks) < 3:
            raise ValidationError("Remarks must be at least 3 characters.")
        self.dao.require_draft(draft_id)
        self.dao.update_draft_status(draft_id, "REJECTED")
        self.dao.add_review(
            draft_id=draft_id,
            action="REJECT",
            reviewed_by=reviewer_id,
            remarks=remarks,
        )
        return self.dao.require_draft(draft_id)

    def regenerate(
        self,
        draft_id: int,
        reviewer_id: int | None,
        remarks: str,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        remarks = require_nonempty(remarks, "Remarks")
        draft = self.dao.require_draft(draft_id)
        request = self.dao.require_request(int(draft["request_id"]))
        text = remarks.lower()
        threshold_delta = 0.10 if "shorter" in text else (-0.10 if ("longer" in text or "more detail" in text) else 0.0)
        sentence_delta = -1 if "shorter" in text else (2 if ("longer" in text or "more detail" in text) else 0)

        selected, meta, threshold = predict_sentences(
            str(request["original_title"]),
            str(request["original_content"]),
            str(draft["platform"]),
            threshold_delta,
            sentence_delta,
            "quote" in text,
            ("number" in text or "figure" in text),
        )
        new_title, content = format_output(str(draft["platform"]), str(request["original_title"]), selected)
        flags = validate_facts(str(request["original_content"]), content)
        versions = self.dao.list_versions(int(draft["request_id"]), str(draft["platform"]))
        next_version = max((int(v["version_number"]) for v in versions), default=0) + 1
        child_id = self.dao.create_draft(
            request_id=int(draft["request_id"]),
            platform=str(draft["platform"]),
            version_number=next_version,
            adapted_title=new_title,
            adapted_content=content,
            model_version=str(meta.get("version", "heuristic-1.0.0")),
            status="PENDING_REVIEW" if flags.get("passed") else "REQUIRES_MANUAL_REVIEW",
            parent_draft_id=draft_id,
            generation_parameters={
                "threshold": threshold,
                "remarks": remarks,
                "selected_sentence_indexes": [row[0] for row in selected],
            },
            validation_flags=flags,
        )
        self.dao.update_draft_status(draft_id, "REGENERATION_REQUESTED")
        self.dao.add_review(
            draft_id=draft_id,
            action="REGENERATE",
            reviewed_by=reviewer_id,
            remarks=remarks,
        )
        return self.dao.require_draft(child_id)

    def finalize(
        self,
        draft_id: int,
        reviewer_id: int | None,
        final_title: str,
        final_content: str,
        remarks: str | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        draft = self.dao.require_draft(draft_id)
        if str(draft.get("status")) == "FINALIZED":
            raise ValidationError("Draft is already finalized.")
        existing = self.dao.get_final_for_draft(draft_id)
        if existing:
            raise ValidationError("Draft is already finalized.")

        request = self.dao.require_request(int(draft["request_id"]))
        title = require_title(final_title)
        content = require_nonempty(final_content, "Final content")
        flags = validate_facts(str(request["original_content"]), content)
        if not flags.get("passed"):
            raise ValidationError(
                "Final content contains unsupported numeric facts: "
                + str(flags)
            )

        self.dao.update_draft_status(draft_id, "FINALIZED")
        self.dao.add_review(
            draft_id=draft_id,
            action="FINALIZE",
            reviewed_by=reviewer_id,
            remarks=remarks,
        )
        self.dao.save_final(
            draft_id=draft_id,
            final_title=title,
            final_content=content,
            finalized_by=reviewer_id,
            remarks=remarks,
        )
        return self.dao.require_draft(draft_id)

    def versions(self, draft_id: int) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        draft = self.dao.require_draft(draft_id)
        return self.dao.list_versions(int(draft["request_id"]), str(draft["platform"]))

"""Audience Insight Analyst services."""

from services.aia.aia_service import AIAService

__all__ = ["AIAService"]


==============================================================================
FILE: services/aia/aia_service.py
==============================================================================
"""Audience Insight Analyst — scores, category, story pitches.

Pitch importance = simple weighted formula (NOT the editor queue urgency model).
Editor queue urgency lives in ml/editor/queue_urgency.py.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from database.connection import db_cursor
from entities.content.story_pitch import StoryPitch
from ml.aia import predict_category, predict_engagement, predict_interest, predict_trend
from repository.content_repository import ContentRepository


def _pitch_importance(
    trend_score: float,
    audience_interest_score: float,
    coverage_gap_score: float,
) -> str:
    """Combine scores into low / medium / high pitch importance."""
    score = (trend_score * 0.4) + (audience_interest_score * 0.4) + (coverage_gap_score * 0.2)
    if score >= 80:
        return "high"
    if score >= 50:
        return "medium"
    return "low"


class AIAService:
    def __init__(self, repo: ContentRepository | None = None) -> None:
        self.repo = repo or ContentRepository()

    def analyze_engagement(
        self,
        *,
        views: float = 120,
        likes: float = 25,
        shares: float = 8,
        comments: float = 4,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return predict_engagement(views=views, likes=likes, shares=shares, comments=comments)

    def analyze_interest(
        self,
        *,
        views: float = 120,
        likes: float = 25,
        shares: float = 8,
        comments: float = 4,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return predict_interest(views=views, likes=likes, shares=shares, comments=comments)

    def analyze_trends(self, title: str = "", summary: str = "") -> dict[str, Any]:
        headline = title or "Audience trend scan across recent newsroom coverage"
        return predict_trend(headline, summary)

    def predict_news_category(self, title: str, summary: str = "") -> dict[str, Any]:
        return predict_category(title, summary)

    def generate_story_pitch(self, payload: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
        title = str(payload.title)
        summary = str(payload.summary or "")
        category = predict_category(title, summary)

        trend_score = float(payload.trend_score or 0)
        interest_score = float(payload.audience_interest_score or 0)
        coverage_gap = float(payload.coverage_gap_score or 0)

        if trend_score <= 0:
            trend_score = float(predict_trend(title, summary)["trend_score"])
        if interest_score <= 0:
            interest_score = float(
                predict_interest(
                    views=float(getattr(payload, "views", 120) or 120),
                    likes=float(getattr(payload, "likes", 25) or 25),
                    shares=float(getattr(payload, "shares", 8) or 8),
                    comments=float(getattr(payload, "comments", 4) or 4),
                )["audience_interest_score"]
            )
        if coverage_gap <= 0:
            conf = float(category.get("confidence") or 0.5)
            coverage_gap = round(min(100.0, max(10.0, (1.0 - conf) * 100.0)), 2)

        pitch_importance = _pitch_importance(trend_score, interest_score, coverage_gap)

        # DB column is still named "priority"; API uses clearer "pitch_importance"
        pitch = StoryPitch(
            title=title,
            summary=summary,
            priority=pitch_importance,
            source_type="AI_GENERATED",
            trend_score=trend_score,
            audience_interest_score=interest_score,
            coverage_gap_score=coverage_gap,
            status="submitted",
        )
        pitch_id = self.repo.save_pitch(pitch)
        return {
            "story_pitch_id": pitch_id,
            "title": title,
            "summary": summary,
            "pitch_importance": pitch_importance,
            "priority": pitch_importance,
            "importance_source": "formula",
            "source_type": "AI_GENERATED",
            "created_by_desk": "AIA",
            "category": category.get("predicted_category") or payload.category or None,
            "trend_score": trend_score,
            "audience_interest_score": interest_score,
            "coverage_gap_score": coverage_gap,
        }

    def get_story_pitches(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        pitches = self.repo.pitches.list_all()
        return {"pitches": [_annotate_pitch_source(p) for p in pitches]}

    def get_top_story_pitches(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        pitches = self.repo.pitches.list_all()
        pitches = sorted(
            pitches,
            key=lambda row: (
                float(row.get("trend_score") or 0)
                + float(row.get("audience_interest_score") or 0)
            ),
            reverse=True,
        )
        return {"pitches": [_annotate_pitch_source(p) for p in pitches[:10]]}

    def performance_analytics(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Close the diagram loop: publication/content outcomes back to AIA."""
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                SELECT status, COUNT(*) AS count
                FROM publication
                GROUP BY status
                """
            )
            publication_by_status = {
                str(r["status"]): int(r["count"]) for r in cur.fetchall() or []
            }

            cur.execute(
                """
                SELECT status, COUNT(*) AS count
                FROM content
                GROUP BY status
                """
            )
            content_by_status = {str(r["status"]): int(r["count"]) for r in cur.fetchall() or []}

            cur.execute(
                """
                SELECT status, COUNT(*) AS count,
                       ROUND(AVG(COALESCE(trend_score, 0))::numeric, 2) AS avg_trend,
                       ROUND(AVG(COALESCE(audience_interest_score, 0))::numeric, 2) AS avg_interest
                FROM story_pitch
                GROUP BY status
                """
            )
            pitch_outcomes = [
                {
                    "status": str(r["status"]),
                    "count": int(r["count"]),
                    "avg_trend": float(r["avg_trend"] or 0),
                    "avg_interest": float(r["avg_interest"] or 0),
                }
                for r in cur.fetchall() or []
            ]

            cur.execute(
                """
                SELECT source_type, COUNT(*) AS count
                FROM story_pitch
                GROUP BY source_type
                """
            )
            pitches_by_source = {
                str(r["source_type"] or "UNKNOWN"): int(r["count"]) for r in cur.fetchall() or []
            }

            cur.execute(
                """
                SELECT c.title, p.status AS publication_status, p.published_at
                FROM publication p
                JOIN content c ON c.content_id = p.content_id
                WHERE p.status IN ('published', 'scheduled')
                ORDER BY COALESCE(p.published_at, p.updated_at) DESC NULLS LAST
                LIMIT 10
                """
            )
            recent_published = [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall() or []]

        published = publication_by_status.get("published", 0)
        scheduled = publication_by_status.get("scheduled", 0)
        approved_content = content_by_status.get("approved", 0)
        insights = []
        if published + scheduled > 0:
            insights.append(
                f"{published} published and {scheduled} scheduled items — reuse winning topics in new pitches."
            )
        if approved_content:
            insights.append(
                f"{approved_content} approved pieces awaiting adaptation/publish — check coverage gaps."
            )
        aia_pitches = pitches_by_source.get("AI_GENERATED", 0)
        manual_pitches = pitches_by_source.get("MANUAL", 0) + pitches_by_source.get("UNKNOWN", 0)
        insights.append(
            f"Pitch mix: {aia_pitches} AIA-generated vs {manual_pitches} manual/other."
        )

        return {
            "publication_by_status": publication_by_status,
            "content_by_status": content_by_status,
            "pitch_outcomes": pitch_outcomes,
            "pitches_by_source": pitches_by_source,
            "recent_published": recent_published,
            "insights": insights,
            "loop": "publication/content outcomes → AIA performance → story pitch workspace",
        }


def _annotate_pitch_source(pitch: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    source = str(pitch.get("source_type") or "").strip().upper() or "UNKNOWN"
    if source in {"AI_GENERATED", "AIA", "AI"}:
        created_by = "AIA"
        source_type = "AI_GENERATED"
    elif source in {"MANUAL", "EDITOR", "HUMAN"}:
        created_by = "MANUAL"
        source_type = "MANUAL"
    else:
        created_by = "UNKNOWN"
        source_type = source
    return {
        **pitch,
        "source_type": source_type,
        "created_by_desk": created_by,
    }


==============================================================================
FILE: services/aia/README.md

# `services/aia/`

Audience Insight Analyst domain logic used by `api/routers/aia.py`.

| Method | Purpose |
|--------|---------|
| `analyze_engagement` | Heuristic engagement score (no joblib) |
| `analyze_interest` | Heuristic audience interest (no joblib) |
| `analyze_trends` | Trend score from category RF |
| `generate_story_pitch` | Create pitch (`source_type=AI_GENERATED`; formula priority) |
| `get_story_pitches` | List pitches (+ desk source label) |
| `get_top_story_pitches` | Top 10 by trend + interest |
| `performance_analytics` | Publication/content/pitch rollups + insights |

Requires capability `analytics` (audience analyst / admin).
Pretrained: `news_category_rf` only.

from services.content.content_service import ContentService

__all__ = ["ContentService"]

FILE: services/content/content_service.py

"""Content domain service — news/article/pitch/category CRUD with validation."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from config.constants import ALLOWED_CONTENT_STATUSES
from dao.category_dao import CategoryDAO
from entities.content.article import Article
from entities.content.category import Category
from entities.content.content import Content
from entities.content.evidence import Evidence
from entities.content.news import News
from entities.content.story_pitch import StoryPitch
from repository.content_repository import ContentRepository
from utils.errors import NotFoundError, ValidationError
from utils.validators import (
    require_article_type,
    require_body,
    require_category_name,
    require_content_status,
    require_nonempty,
    require_optional_str,
    require_pitch_status,
    require_positive_id,
    require_priority,
    require_read_time,
    require_severity,
    require_title,
    validate_content_record,
)


class ContentService:
    def __init__(self, repo: ContentRepository | None = None) -> None:
        self.repo = repo or ContentRepository()
        self.categories = CategoryDAO()

    def add_category(self, name: str, description: str = "") -> int:
        return self.categories.create(
            Category(
                category_name=require_category_name(name),
                description=require_optional_str(description, "Description"),
            )
        )

    def add_pitch(self, title: str, summary: str, status: str = "submitted", priority: str = "medium") -> int:
        return self.repo.save_pitch(
            StoryPitch(
                title=require_title(title),
                summary=require_nonempty(summary, "Summary"),
                status=require_pitch_status(status),
                priority=require_priority(priority),
            )
        )

    def add_news(
        self,
        *,
        title: str,
        body: str,
        summary: str = "",
        event_title: str = "",
        event_type: str = "city",
        event_location: str = "",
        severity: str = "low",
        breaking: bool = False,
        status: str = "draft",
        author_id: int | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        status = require_content_status(status)
        title = require_title(title)
        body = require_body(body)
        errors = validate_content_record(
            {"title": title, "text": body, "content_body": body, "status": status}
        )
        if errors:
            raise ValidationError(
                "Invalid news fields: "
                + ", ".join(errors)
                + f". Allowed status: {', '.join(ALLOWED_CONTENT_STATUSES)}."
            )
        content_id = self.repo.content.create(
            Content(
                author_id=author_id,
                title=title,
                summary=require_optional_str(summary, "Summary"),
                raw_body=body,
                content_body=body,
                status=status,
                content_kind="news",
                source_type="api",
            )
        )
        news_id = self.repo.news.create(
            News(
                content_id=content_id,
                event_title=require_optional_str(event_title, "Event title") or title,
                event_type=require_nonempty(event_type, "Event type"),
                event_location=require_optional_str(event_location, "Event location", max_len=255),
                severity=require_severity(severity),
                breaking_news_flag=bool(breaking),
            )
        )
        return {"content_id": content_id, "news_id": news_id}

    def add_article(
        self,
        *,
        title: str,
        body: str,
        summary: str = "",
        article_type: str = "feature",
        read_time: int = 3,
        status: str = "draft",
        author_id: int | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        status = require_content_status(status)
        title = require_title(title)
        body = require_body(body)
        errors = validate_content_record(
            {"title": title, "text": body, "content_body": body, "status": status}
        )
        if errors:
            raise ValidationError("Invalid article fields: " + ", ".join(errors))
        content_id = self.repo.content.create(
            Content(
                author_id=author_id,
                title=title,
                summary=require_optional_str(summary, "Summary"),
                raw_body=body,
                content_body=body,
                status=status,
                content_kind="article",
                source_type="api",
            )
        )
        article_id = self.repo.articles.create(
            Article(
                content_id=content_id,
                article_type=require_article_type(article_type),
                estimated_read_time=require_read_time(read_time),
            )
        )
        return {"content_id": content_id, "article_id": article_id}

    def add_evidence(
        self,
        *,
        content_id: int,
        title: str,
        evidence_type: str = "document",
        source_url: str = "",
    ) -> int:
        self.repo.get_content(content_id)
        return self.repo.save_evidence(
            Evidence(
                content_id=content_id,
                title=require_title(title),
                evidence_type=require_nonempty(evidence_type, "Evidence type"),
                source_url=require_optional_str(source_url, "Source URL"),
            )
        )

    def get_content(self, content_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.repo.get_content(require_positive_id(content_id, "Content ID"))

    def get_content_by_input(self, raw_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.get_content(require_positive_id(raw_id, "Content ID"))

    def list_content(self, limit: int = 10, status: str | None = None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        limit = max(1, min(int(limit), 500))
        if status:
            return self.repo.content.list_by_status(require_content_status(status), limit=limit)
        return self.repo.content.list_all(limit=limit)

    def list_news(self, limit: int = 10) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.repo.news.list_all(limit=max(1, min(int(limit), 500)))

    def list_raw_ingest(self, limit: int = 15) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.repo.raw.list_recent(limit=max(1, min(int(limit), 500)))

    def raw_ingest_counts(self) -> dict[str, int]:
        return self.repo.raw.count_by_status()

    def update_content_status(self, content_id: int, status: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        content_id = require_positive_id(content_id, "Content ID")
        status = require_content_status(status)
        self.repo.get_content(content_id)
        changed = self.repo.content.update_status(content_id, status)
        if changed != 1:
            raise NotFoundError(f"Content ID {content_id} was not updated.")
        row = self.repo.get_content(content_id)
        return {"content_id": content_id, "status": row["status"], "title": row.get("title")}

FILE: services/content/README.md

# Learning guide: `services/content/`

## Why this folder exists

Journalists, editors, publishers, and analysts work with **categories, pitches, news, articles, and raw ingest listings** through the content desk menus. Those operations share validation rules with the CSV pipeline but are not the same as bulk ingest/clean/persist.

`ContentService` centralizes desk CRUD: it validates input, builds `entities.content.*` objects, and delegates persistence to `ContentRepository` and `CategoryDAO`.

---

## How this package connects

```
modules.content.ContentModule  (thin delegate)
        ↓
ContentService
        ↓
ContentRepository + CategoryDAO
        ↓
dao.content_dao, news_dao, article_dao, story_pitch_dao, raw_ingest_dao, ...
```

`app.py` desk handlers (`do_add_news`, `do_list_content`, …) call `ContentModule`, not `ContentService` directly — so capability/session rules can grow in the module layer later without rewriting every menu function.

`PersistenceService` also uses `ContentRepository` but for **pipeline** dict rows, not interactive desk forms — two entry points, one repository.

---

## `content_service.py` — class `ContentService`

**Constructor:** optional `ContentRepository`; always constructs `CategoryDAO` for category creates.

### Categories

**`add_category(name, description)`** — wraps `Category` entity with `require_category_name` and optional description.

### Pitches

**`add_pitch(title, summary, status, priority)`** — builds `StoryPitch` with pitch status and priority validators; `repo.save_pitch`.

### News

**`add_news(**kwargs)`** — desk-created breaking news path:

- Validates title, body, content status, runs `validate_content_record`.
- Creates `Content` with `content_kind="news"`, `source_type="console"`, both `raw_body` and `content_body` set from input (desk entry is already “clean” text).
- Creates linked `News` row with event fields, severity, breaking flag.
- Returns `content_id` and `news_id`.

### Articles

**`add_article(**kwargs)`** — same pattern with `content_kind="article"`, `Article` subtype row (`article_type`, `read_time`).

### Reads and lists

**`list_content`, `list_news`** — paginated-style limits for console tables.

**`list_raw_ingest`, `raw_ingest_counts`** — pipeline staging visibility for journalists/admins.

**`get_content` / `get_content_by_input`** — resolve numeric id or string input from prompts.

**`update_content_status`** — workflow transitions using `require_content_status`; used by editor/publisher/manager desks.

---

## Validation reuse

Desk creates use the same **`validate_content_record`** and **`ALLOWED_CONTENT_STATUSES`** as `SchemaCleaner` and `PersistenceService`. That is how a story typed at the console cannot enter `published` with an empty body if the rule forbids it.

---

## `__init__.py`

Re-exports `ContentService` only.

---

## Exercises

1. Add news from journalist menu; `View content by ID` and compare `raw_body` vs `content_body`.
2. Ingest CSV, list raw ingest — relate rows to `RawIngestDAO` columns.
3. Change status as publisher; trace `update_content_status` SQL in `ContentDAO`.
4. Compare `ContentModule` line count to `ContentService` — note what the module does *not* duplicate.


==============================================================================
FILE: services/editor/__init__.py

"""Editor desk services."""

from services.editor.editor_service import EditorService

__all__ = ["EditorService"]


==============================================================================
FILE: services/editor/editor_service.py
==============================================================================
"""Editor desk — review queue, pitch duplicates, assign suggest, rework."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any

from dao.content_dao import ContentDAO
from dao.story_pitch_dao import StoryPitchDAO
from database.connection import db_cursor
from ml.common import normalize, similar_pairs
from ml.editor import predict_editor_queue_urgency
from utils.validators import require_optional_str, require_positive_id, require_title

# Lower number = show earlier in the editor queue
URGENCY_ORDER = {"urgent": 0, "high": 1, "medium": 2, "low": 3}
def _urgency_rank(level: str | None) -> int:
    return URGENCY_ORDER.get(str(level or "medium").lower(), 4)


def _annotate_pitch_source(pitch: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    source = str(pitch.get("source_type") or "").strip().upper() or "UNKNOWN"
    if source in {"AI_GENERATED", "AIA", "AI"}:
        created_by = "AIA"
        source_type = "AI_GENERATED"
    elif source in {"MANUAL", "EDITOR", "HUMAN"}:
        created_by = "MANUAL"
        source_type = "MANUAL"
    else:
        created_by = "UNKNOWN"
        source_type = source
    return {**pitch, "source_type": source_type, "created_by_desk": created_by}


class EditorService:
    def __init__(
        self,
        pitches: StoryPitchDAO | None = None,
        content: ContentDAO | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.pitches = pitches or StoryPitchDAO()
        self.content = content or ContentDAO()

    def score_editor_queue_urgency(self, title: str, summary: str = "") -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Score how urgent a headline is for the editor review queue."""
        headline = require_title(title)
        body = require_optional_str(summary, "Summary") or ""
        return predict_editor_queue_urgency(headline, body)

    def list_pitches(self, status: str = "submitted", limit: int = 100) -> dict[str, Any]:
        rows = self.pitches.list_by_status(status, limit=limit)
        return {"pitches": [_annotate_pitch_source(r) for r in rows]}

    def find_duplicate_pitches(
        self,
        *,
        title: str | None = None,
        summary: str = "",
        status: str = "submitted",
        threshold: float = 0.45,
        limit: int = 20,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Near-duplicate pitches among open queue (shows AIA vs manual source)."""
        open_pitches = [_annotate_pitch_source(p) for p in self.pitches.list_by_status(status, limit=300)]
        docs = [
            {
                "id": p.get("story_pitch_id"),
                "title": p.get("title") or "",
                "text": f"{p.get('title') or ''}. {p.get('summary') or ''}",
                "source_type": p.get("source_type"),
                "created_by_desk": p.get("created_by_desk"),
                "pitch_importance": p.get("priority"),
            }
            for p in open_pitches
        ]
        by_id = {d["id"]: d for d in docs}

        if title:
            query_doc = {
                "id": "query",
                "title": title,
                "text": f"{title}. {summary or ''}",
            }
            corpus = [query_doc, *docs]
            pairs = similar_pairs(corpus, threshold=threshold, limit=limit * 3)
            matches = []
            for pair in pairs:
                left, right = pair.get("left_id"), pair.get("right_id")
                other_id = right if left == "query" else left if right == "query" else None
                if other_id is None or other_id == "query":
                    continue
                other = by_id.get(other_id) or {}
                matches.append(
                    {
                        "story_pitch_id": other_id,
                        "title": other.get("title"),
                        "score": pair.get("score"),
                        "source_type": other.get("source_type"),
                        "created_by_desk": other.get("created_by_desk"),
                        "pitch_importance": other.get("pitch_importance"),
                    }
                )
            matches = matches[:limit]
            return {
                "query_title": title,
                "matches": matches,
                "match_count": len(matches),
                "scanned": len(docs),
            }

        pairs = similar_pairs(docs, threshold=threshold, limit=limit)
        enriched = []
        for pair in pairs:
            left = by_id.get(pair.get("left_id")) or {}
            right = by_id.get(pair.get("right_id")) or {}
            enriched.append(
                {
                    **pair,
                    "left_source_type": left.get("source_type"),
                    "left_created_by_desk": left.get("created_by_desk"),
                    "right_source_type": right.get("source_type"),
                    "right_created_by_desk": right.get("created_by_desk"),
                }
            )
        return {"pairs": enriched, "pair_count": len(enriched), "scanned": len(docs)}

    def suggest_journalists(
        self,
        *,
        topic: str = "",
        title: str = "",
        limit: int = 10,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Rank journalists by specialty match + inverse workload + availability."""
        needle = normalize(f"{topic} {title}")
        needle_tokens = set(needle.split()) if needle else set()

        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                SELECT j.journalist_id, j.user_id, j.primary_specialization,
                       j.secondary_specialization, j.availability_status, j.status,
                       u.username,
                       COALESCE(NULLIF(TRIM(e.first_name || ' ' || e.last_name), ''), u.username) AS full_name,
                       (
                         SELECT COUNT(*) FROM content c
                         WHERE c.author_id = j.journalist_id
                           AND c.status IN ('draft', 'submitted', 'rework')
                       ) AS open_workload
                FROM journalist j
                JOIN users u ON u.user_id = j.user_id
                LEFT JOIN employee e ON e.employee_id = u.employee_id
                WHERE j.status = 'active'
                ORDER BY j.journalist_id
                """,
            )
            rows = [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall() or []]

        ranked: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for row in rows:
            primary = normalize(str(row.get("primary_specialization") or ""))
            secondary = normalize(str(row.get("secondary_specialization") or ""))
            specialty_tokens = set((primary + " " + secondary).split())
            overlap = len(needle_tokens & specialty_tokens) if needle_tokens else 0
            specialty_score = 0.0
            if needle and primary and primary in needle:
                specialty_score = 1.0
            elif needle and secondary and secondary in needle:
                specialty_score = 0.7
            elif overlap:
                specialty_score = min(1.0, overlap / max(len(needle_tokens), 1))
            elif not needle_tokens:
                specialty_score = 0.35

            workload = int(row.get("open_workload") or 0)
            workload_score = 1.0 / (1.0 + workload)
            availability = str(row.get("availability_status") or "available").lower()
            avail_score = {
                "available": 1.0,
                "busy": 0.45,
                "away": 0.15,
                "leave": 0.0,
            }.get(availability, 0.3)

            score = round(specialty_score * 0.55 + workload_score * 0.30 + avail_score * 0.15, 4)
            ranked.append(
                {
                    "journalist_id": row["journalist_id"],
                    "user_id": row["user_id"],
                    "username": row.get("username"),
                    "full_name": row.get("full_name"),
                    "primary_specialization": row.get("primary_specialization"),
                    "secondary_specialization": row.get("secondary_specialization"),
                    "availability_status": availability,
                    "open_workload": workload,
                    "specialty_score": round(specialty_score, 4),
                    "score": score,
                    "reason": (
                        f"specialty={specialty_score:.2f}, "
                        f"workload={workload}, availability={availability}"
                    ),
                }
            )

        ranked.sort(key=lambda r: (-r["score"], r["open_workload"], r["journalist_id"]))
        return {
            "suggestions": ranked[: max(1, min(int(limit), 50))],
            "topic": topic or title,
            "method": "specialty_match + inverse_workload + availability",
        }

    def list_content(self, status: str = "submitted", limit: int = 100) -> dict[str, Any]:
        items = self.content.list_by_status(status, limit=limit)
        scored: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for row in items:
            title = str(row.get("title") or "")
            body = str(row.get("summary") or row.get("content_body") or row.get("raw_body") or "")
            prediction = predict_editor_queue_urgency(title, body)
            scored.append(
                {
                    **row,
                    "editor_queue_urgency": prediction["urgency"],
                    "urgency_confidence": prediction.get("confidence"),
                    "urgency_source": prediction.get("source"),
                    "model_label": prediction.get("model_label"),
                }
            )
        scored.sort(
            key=lambda row: (
                _urgency_rank(row.get("editor_queue_urgency")),
                -int(row.get("content_id") or 0),
            )
        )
        return {"items": scored}

    def review_queue(
        self,
        *,
        pitch_status: str = "submitted",
        content_status: str = "submitted",
        limit: int = 100,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        pitches = self.list_pitches(status=pitch_status, limit=limit)["pitches"]
        content = self.list_content(status=content_status, limit=limit)["items"]

        tasks: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for pitch in pitches:
            # Pitch importance comes from AIA formula / DB column "priority"
            tasks.append(
                {
                    "task_type": "pitch",
                    "task_id": pitch.get("story_pitch_id"),
                    "title": pitch.get("title"),
                    "summary": pitch.get("summary"),
                    "urgency": pitch.get("priority") or "medium",
                    "urgency_kind": "pitch_importance",
                    "status": pitch.get("status"),
                    "source_type": pitch.get("source_type"),
                    "created_by_desk": pitch.get("created_by_desk"),
                    "item": pitch,
                }
            )
        for item in content:
            # Content urgency comes from the editor queue urgency model
            tasks.append(
                {
                    "task_type": "content",
                    "task_id": item.get("content_id"),
                    "title": item.get("title"),
                    "summary": item.get("summary"),
                    "urgency": item.get("editor_queue_urgency") or "medium",
                    "urgency_kind": "editor_queue_urgency",
                    "status": item.get("status"),
                    "source": item.get("urgency_source") or "model",
                    "confidence": item.get("urgency_confidence"),
                    "item": item,
                }
            )
        tasks.sort(
            key=lambda row: (
                _urgency_rank(str(row.get("urgency"))),
                0 if row.get("task_type") == "pitch" else 1,
                -int(row.get("task_id") or 0),
            )
        )
        return {
            "tasks": tasks,
            "pitch_count": len(pitches),
            "content_count": len(content),
        }

    def approve_pitch(self, story_pitch_id: int, reviewer_id: int | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
        pitch_id = require_positive_id(story_pitch_id, "Story pitch ID")
        self.pitches.require(pitch_id)
        self.pitches.update(
            pitch_id,
            status="approved",
            approved_by=reviewer_id,
            approved_at=datetime.utcnow(),
        )
        return _annotate_pitch_source(self.pitches.require(pitch_id))

    def reject_pitch(self, story_pitch_id: int, remarks: str = "") -> dict[str, Any]:
        pitch_id = require_positive_id(story_pitch_id, "Story pitch ID")
        self.pitches.require(pitch_id)
        self.pitches.update(pitch_id, status="rejected")
        row = _annotate_pitch_source(self.pitches.require(pitch_id))
        return {**row, "remarks": require_optional_str(remarks, "Remarks") or ""}

    def approve_content(self, content_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        content_id = require_positive_id(content_id, "Content ID")
        self.content.require(content_id)
        self.content.update_status(content_id, "approved")
        return self.content.require(content_id)

    def reject_content(self, content_id: int, remarks: str = "") -> dict[str, Any]:
        content_id = require_positive_id(content_id, "Content ID")
        self.content.require(content_id)
        self.content.update_status(content_id, "rejected")
        row = self.content.require(content_id)
        return {**row, "remarks": require_optional_str(remarks, "Remarks") or ""}

    def send_content_rework(self, content_id: int, remarks: str = "") -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Editor → journalist rework loop."""
        content_id = require_positive_id(content_id, "Content ID")
        self.content.require(content_id)
        self.content.update_status(content_id, "rework")
        row = self.content.require(content_id)
        return {
            **row,
            "remarks": require_optional_str(remarks, "Remarks") or "",
            "next_step": "Journalist updates content then resubmits (status=submitted)",
        }

    def assign_content(
        self,
        content_id: int,
        assignee_user_id: int,
        note: str = "",
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        content_id = require_positive_id(content_id, "Content ID")
        assignee_user_id = require_positive_id(assignee_user_id, "Assignee user ID")
        row = self.content.require(content_id)

        journalist_id = None
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                "SELECT journalist_id FROM journalist WHERE user_id = %s",
                (assignee_user_id,),
            )
            hit = cur.fetchone()
            if hit:
                journalist_id = int(hit["journalist_id"])

        if journalist_id is not None:
            self.content.update(content_id, author_id=journalist_id)
        elif row.get("author_id") is None:
            # Fallback: store user_id only if no journalist desk row (legacy paths).
            self.content.update(content_id, author_id=assignee_user_id)

        updated = self.content.require(content_id)
        return {
            "content_id": content_id,
            "assignee_user_id": assignee_user_id,
            "journalist_id": journalist_id,
            "note": require_optional_str(note, "Note") or "",
            "content": updated,
        }


==============================================================================
FILE: services/ingestion_service.py
==============================================================================
"""CSV ingestion — full files, single file, or new/changed files only."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pandas as pd

from config.runtime import csv_chunk_rows, max_csv_mb_warn
from dao.ingest_file_dao import IngestFileDAO
from utils.csv_fields import normalize_row
from utils.errors import NotFoundError, ValidationError
from utils.helpers import project_path
from utils.logger import get_logger

log = get_logger("ingestion")


def summarize_records(rows: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    by_category: dict[str, int] = {}
    titles: set[str] = set()
    for row in rows:
        cat = str(row.get("category") or "Unknown")
        by_category[cat] = by_category.get(cat, 0) + 1
        title = str(row.get("title") or "").strip().lower()
        if title:
            titles.add(title)
    return {
        "list_count": len(rows),
        "categories": sorted(by_category.keys()),
        "unique_titles": len(titles),
        "by_category_counts": by_category,
    }


# Backward-compatible name used by older tests
memory_collection_demo = summarize_records


class IngestionService:
    def __init__(self, raw_dir: Path | None = None) -> None:
        self.raw_dir = raw_dir or project_path("data", "raw")
        self.files = IngestFileDAO()

    def list_csv_files(self) -> list[str]:
        if not self.raw_dir.exists():
            return []
        out: list[str] = []
        for path in sorted(self.raw_dir.rglob("*.csv")):
            if not path.is_file():
                continue
            try:
                out.append(path.relative_to(self.raw_dir).as_posix())
            except ValueError:
                out.append(path.name)
        return out

    def resolve_csv(self, source_file: str) -> Path:
        rel = (source_file or "").strip().replace("\\", "/")
        if not rel:
            raise ValidationError("CSV file path cannot be empty.")
        if rel.lower().endswith(".csv") is False:
            raise ValidationError("Only CSV files are supported.")
        path = (self.raw_dir / rel).resolve()
        raw_root = self.raw_dir.resolve()
        if raw_root not in path.parents and path != raw_root:
            # allow exact file under raw_dir
            try:
                path.relative_to(raw_root)
            except ValueError as exc:
                raise ValidationError("CSV path must be inside data/raw.") from exc
        if not path.exists() or not path.is_file():
            raise NotFoundError(f"CSV file not found: {rel}")
        return path

    def _file_hash(self, path: Path) -> str:
        digest = hashlib.md5()
        with path.open("rb") as handle:
            for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
                digest.update(chunk)
        return digest.hexdigest()

    def _rel(self, path: Path) -> str:
        try:
            return path.relative_to(self.raw_dir).as_posix()
        except ValueError:
            return path.name

    def _read_csv(self, path: Path) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int, int]:
        """Return usable rows, total raw rows, usable count (chunked CSV read)."""
        from utils.progress import progress

        rel = self._rel(path)
        size_mb = path.stat().st_size / (1024 * 1024)
        warn_mb = max_csv_mb_warn()
        if size_mb >= warn_mb:
            progress(
                f"Large CSV detected: {rel} ({size_mb:.1f} MB). Reading in chunks..."
            )
            log.warning(
                "Large CSV: file=%s size_mb=%.1f warn_at=%s",
                rel,
                size_mb,
                warn_mb,
            )
        chunk_size = csv_chunk_rows()
        progress(f"Reading CSV in chunks of {chunk_size}: {rel}")
        usable: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        total = 0
        chunk_index = 0
        try:
            reader = pd.read_csv(path, chunksize=chunk_size, low_memory=True)
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            raise ValidationError(f"Failed to read CSV '{rel}': {exc}") from exc
        try:
            for chunk in reader:
                chunk_index += 1
                if chunk.empty:
                    continue
                chunk.columns = [str(c).strip().lower() for c in chunk.columns]
                rows = [normalize_row(row, source_file=rel) for row in chunk.to_dict(orient="records")]
                total += len(rows)
                usable.extend(r for r in rows if r.get("title"))
                if chunk_index == 1 or chunk_index % 5 == 0:
                    progress(
                        f"  chunk {chunk_index}: rows so far={total}, usable={len(usable)}"
                    )
                del rows
                del chunk
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            raise ValidationError(f"Failed while chunk-reading CSV '{rel}': {exc}") from exc
        progress(f"Finished reading {rel}: total={total}, usable={len(usable)}")
        return usable, total, len(usable)

    def file_needs_ingest(self, source_file: str) -> bool:
        path = self.resolve_csv(source_file)
        rel = self._rel(path)
        current = self.files.get_by_source(rel)
        if current is None:
            return True
        return current.get("file_hash") != self._file_hash(path)

    def list_new_or_changed_files(self) -> list[str]:
        return [rel for rel in self.list_csv_files() if self.file_needs_ingest(rel)]

    def load_one_csv(self, source_file: str, *, register: bool = True) -> dict[str, Any]:
        path = self.resolve_csv(source_file)
        rel = self._rel(path)
        rows, total, usable = self._read_csv(path)
        file_hash = self._file_hash(path)
        if register:
            self.files.upsert(
                source_file=rel,
                file_hash=file_hash,
                row_count=total,
                usable_row_count=usable,
                last_status="ingested",
            )
        log.info("Ingested file=%s total=%s usable=%s", rel, total, usable)
        return {
            "records": rows,
            "collections": summarize_records(rows),
            "source_files": [rel],
            "file_hash": file_hash,
            "row_count": total,
            "usable_row_count": usable,
        }

    def load_csv_files(self, source_files: list[str] | None = None, *, register: bool = True) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        from utils.progress import progress

        targets = source_files if source_files is not None else self.list_csv_files()
        rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        total = len(targets)
        for index, rel in enumerate(targets, start=1):
            progress(f"Loading file {index}/{total}: {rel}")
            payload = self.load_one_csv(rel, register=register)
            rows.extend(payload["records"])
        return rows

    def ingest_all_content(self, *, only_new: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
        from utils.progress import progress

        files = self.list_new_or_changed_files() if only_new else self.list_csv_files()
        if only_new and not files:
            progress("No new or changed CSV files to ingest.")
            return {
                "records": [],
                "collections": summarize_records([]),
                "source_files": [],
                "message": "No new or changed CSV files to ingest.",
            }
        progress(f"Ingesting {len(files)} CSV file(s)...")
        combined = self.load_csv_files(files, register=True)
        summary = summarize_records(combined)
        progress(f"Ingest complete: files={len(files)} rows={summary['list_count']}")
        log.info("Ingest complete files=%s rows=%s", len(files), summary["list_count"])
        return {
            "records": combined,
            "collections": summary,
            "source_files": files,
        }

    def ingest_one(self, source_file: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.load_one_csv(source_file, register=True)

    def ingest_pitches(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        path = self.raw_dir / "pitches.csv"
        if not path.exists():
            return []
        rows, _, _ = self._read_csv(path)
        for row in rows:
            row.setdefault("status", "submitted")
            row["source_type"] = "csv:pitches.csv"
        return rows

    def registered_files(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.files.list_all()

"""Journalist intelligence services (research, evidence, metadata, etc.)."""

from services.journalist.content_validate import validate_article
from services.journalist.credibility import assess_credibility
from services.journalist.duplicate_check import find_duplicates
from services.journalist.evidence_search import search_evidence
from services.journalist.metadata import extract_metadata
from services.journalist.patent_search import search_patents

"""5 — Content Validation (rules via utils.validators)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Mapping

from utils.validators import validate_content_record


def validate_article(record: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Check required fields / status using existing validators."""
    row = dict(record or {})
    errors = validate_content_record(row)
    return {
        "ok": len(errors) == 0,
        "errors": errors,
        "title": row.get("title") or "",
    }

FILE: services/journalist/credibility.py
